# Financial-2525 · the 48-agent usability fleet on r.012 (operator addendum 23)

Read-only review of Financial-2525 v.000_r.012 (commit 201a8ec), launched 2026-09-30 23:03 UTC: twelve reviewer lenses × (constructive walk · adversarial pass · synthesis) + twelve MoT coordinators. **Stopped short:** the run hit the session's model usage limit after the 24 reviewers and 8 of 12 syntheses; the remaining 4 syntheses and all 12 MoT coordinators did not run. It reviewed r.012; r.013–r.018 have since changed the form, the pickers, the budget table and the strip — read every finding against the revision it names. The live review is the simplicity fleet (docs/assessments/2026-10-01_financial_2525_fleet48_simplicity.md).

**Agents that returned:** 32 of 48 · **did not return:** 16 (Pangu:synthesis, Sofia:synthesis, Thoth:synthesis, Thor:synthesis, MoT:1, MoT:2, MoT:3, MoT:4, MoT:5, MoT:6, MoT:7, MoT:8, MoT:9, MoT:10, MoT:11, MoT:12)

Every reviewer's words below are verbatim; nothing is summarised or edited. Grades are the reviewers' own.


---

## Aset

### Aset · walk — grade B-

_Constructive walk — a stranger with the operator's own sheet opening /financial-2525 on a phone (390×844 and 844×390), 60 seconds top to bottom, forms read from code because they are sign-in gated_

**One sentence:** The ladder, the record and the brief agree to the cent at all eight units, but the glass tells the same deposit two different lengths, promises a Balance view that does not exist, records a timeline it never shows, and prints an empty section as $0.0000 in a table of $3,200.00.

**Findings**

- **One deposit, two lengths: the MoT field defaults 30.333 days while the Timeline dropdown defaults 'Every 33 days' on the same form** (major)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:145 (`dMot` = SRC.mot.payMotDays = 30.333) and :151 (`dRec` = "days33"), both committed on the same entry at :190 (`motDays: …, recurrence: dRec`). The worked example carries both too (:85 motDays 30.333 + recurrence "days33"). accrual.ts:75 releases at amount ÷ (motDays × 1440) = $0.0825/min; ladder.ts:114-116 actualsByField would spread the same money over recurrenceDays = 33 → $0.0759/min (ratio 1.088). The brief (addendum 22) asks for ONE 'MoT timeline established at transaction entry … 30.333 days etc.'
  - Fix: One length per transaction. Either derive recurrence from motDays (or motDays from the timeline, with 'Other' for a typed length — addendum 25 already asks this), or hide the MoT field when a timeline is chosen; never let two defaults disagree on the same form. Gate: a committed deposit has exactly one length.
- **The note under the ladder sends the reader to a 'Balance view' that does not exist on this surface** (major)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:363 renders fin.stock_note (lexicon-data.ts:3088: '… live on the Balance view — never per minute'). No route, link, tab or button named Balance exists anywhere in the component; the record itself says FIN-06.06 is 'DRAFT (planned for r.013)' (financial-2525.v.000.json crs/28). The glass claims a view the record says is not built.
  - Fix: Until r.013 lands, word the note as a promise ('… will live on a Balance view — r.013'), or drop the sentence; when the view ships, make the note a link to it.
- **The timeline is recorded inside the chain hash but never printed on the roster or the record** (moderate)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:215 (roster label: stamp · field · memo — no recurrence) and :373 (record row: stamp · kind · txWhat · memo · amount · motDays · hash — no recurrence). FIN-03.02's statement says 'the field and the timeline ride the entry … shown on the roster and the record'; tests/financial-surface.test.mjs:52 asserts only the field regex while its message claims 'the field and the timeline are … shown on the roster and the record' — the gate says more than it checks.
  - Fix: Print `t(`fin.rec.${tx.recurrence}`)` after the field on both the roster row (:215) and the record row (:373); extend the surface gate to match the recurrence print, so the gate's sentence is true.
- **A zero section prints as '$0.0000' in a table whose other rows print '$3,200.00'** (moderate)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:141 `usdUnit = cents >= 100 ? usd : usd4` — 0 falls into the four-decimal branch. Captured at per 33 days: 'H · Family $0.0000 · I · Debt service $0.0000 · J · Taxes $0.0000 · K · Work $0.0000 · M · Transfers $0.0000' beside 'A · Income $3,200.00' (fleet-fin/ladder-m33-portrait.png; the SSR text of frontend/out/financial-2525/index.html shows the same). At per second the inverse problem: Fitness ($50/33 d = $0.0000175/s) prints '$0.0000', identical to the truly empty Family row (ladder-sec-portrait.png).
  - Fix: Format zero as the unit's normal form ('$0.00') and let a nonzero-but-tiny figure show enough decimals to be nonzero (or '<$0.0001'), so an empty section and a small one never read the same.
- **The withdrawal form labels its date 'Deposit day and time (CST)'** (minor)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:314 reuses `t("fin.deposit_at")` (lexicon-data.ts:2910 'Deposit day and time (CST)') for the WITHDRAWAL's day-and-time input; the operator's own r.011 screenshot in addendum 24 shows the same wording under WITHDRAWAL.
  - Fix: A neutral key ('Day and time (CST)') for both forms — which addendum 24's one-form design will need anyway.
- **The duplicate-insurance warning exists in the model and has no place on the glass** (minor)
  - Evidence: ladder.ts:96 pushes 'DUPLICATE_INSURANCE' into `warnings`; command-ux1.tsx:138 stores `totals` and never reads `totals.warnings` (no render, no key). Brief rule 5 and FIN-06.05's metric say 'the duplicate-insurance warning fires' — it fires in a test only.
  - Fix: Render `totals.warnings` under the ladder (one amber line, lexicon key), so the warning the brief asks for can reach a person once the sheet is editable.
- **The phone strip shows two dollar figures with no words** (minor)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:392 `{usd(bal.releasedCents)} · {usd(bal.availableCents)}` — captured as '$0.00 · $0.00 DEPOSIT' (fleet-fin/strip-portrait.png). A stranger cannot tell released from available; the balance block above uses the words 'Released' and 'Available'.
  - Fix: Prefix each figure with its short word (the keys already exist: fin.released, fin.available).
- **The balance heading reads 'Released · Deposit' when nothing is releasing** (minor)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:262 falls back to `t(phaseDef.labelKey)` when ratePerMinCents is 0, so the line prints 'Released · Deposit' (captured, walk-portrait-full.png) — the phase word appended to a balance label reads as a heading with a stray noun.
  - Fix: When no rate is running, print the 'Released' label alone (the phase is already on the pill and the rail).
- **A code comment and the glass disagree on the pay MoT's A-units (298.3475..1826 vs 298.3463..2448)** (minor)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:430 comment: '30.333 d = 298.3475..1826 A on the exact Earth year'; the chart renders 298.3463..2448 (fleet-fin/chart-mot-portrait.png). 298.3475..1826 is 91 ÷ 3 = 30.3333… (mot.ts:59 MOT_PAY_MONTH_DAYS); 298.3463..2448 is the record's 30.333 (financial-2525.v.000.json example.motDays). Two spellings of the operator's month; the record rounds both to 298.97.
  - Fix: Pick one pay MoT (the operator wrote 30.333) and make mot.ts:59, the record's example.motDays and the :430 comment say the same number.
- **The ladder is always the operator's sheet, even for a signed-in person, and does not say so** (minor)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:138 `netLadder(SHEET_LINES, period)` — the seed from budget.ts:31-40 — under the title 'Personal finance ladder A–M' (:336) with no EXAMPLE badge, unlike the deposit block (:253, :328). Only the fields the sheet names get a child row; a signed-in person's own entries never reach it (actuals are FIN-06.06, r.013).
  - Fix: Badge the table as the sheet/example until actuals land (r.013), reusing fin.example_badge; when actuals arrive, show the person's rows.
- **A ladder field whose id is unknown wears the Lifestyle icon** (minor)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:180 `fieldOf(tx.field)?.section ?? "L"` — an entry with a retired or mistyped field id prints the party-popper icon of section L beside a raw id.
  - Fix: Fall back to a neutral icon (CircleEllipsis, as SectionIcon already does for an unknown section) rather than a real section's.

**Keep**

- The ladder agrees with the record and with independent arithmetic at every one of the eight units: A 3,200 · B 700 · C 1,800 · D 200 · E 150 · F 300 · G 50 · L 200 · Net −$200.00 per 33 days, −$0.0042 per minute, −$2,212.12 per year — exactly FIN-06.05's dtm and the r.006 table (fleet-fin/aset-walk.log vs aset-math.mjs); a unit switch is one constant, no drift (ladder.ts:79-80).
- The twenty sections and sixty labels on the glass are the brief's words verbatim — lexicon-data.ts:2999-3078 matches ladder.ts:31-70 matches addendum 22 — and the gate (tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs) pins them; U is nowhere on the glass, N–T are nowhere on the ladder.
- The year line is self-consistent with the perihelion law: Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91 · frame 9 · 7/33 · 2663.1922..2811 A all derive from the same 2026.01.03_11.15 CST perihelion (calendar.ts:37, :91-115) and check by hand (270.24 days elapsed ÷ 365.259636 × 3600).
- The MoT ⇄ Clock toggle is one state for the whole card: the chart title, the five axis marks and the year line all flip together (command-ux1.tsx:107, :270, :290, :433) — addendum 13/14 honoured, two dots before C everywhere.
- The unit dropdown is genuinely full width in portrait (measured 290 of 316 px) and its own width in landscape (224 of 694 px) — FD-24 holds on both orientations (aset-walk.log).
- No horizontal overflow and no page errors at 390×844 or 844×390; the section rows carry their icons (thirteen data-fin-sec-icon strokes) and the roster and the record print the entry's field, with older category entries still honoured (command-ux1.tsx:179-183).
- The record is honest where it counts: FIN-06.04/FIN-03.01 marked superseded rather than rewritten, FIN-06.06 marked draft, FD-25 says on the record that the ladder's 365-day year and the MoT's 365.259636-day revolution are two deliberate wholes (ladder.ts:13-15).

**Report (verbatim)**

THE WALK (390×844, the operator's sheet in my hand, 60 seconds)

0–10 s. The shell is the Session's: globe, three glyphs, FINANCIAL · 2525, 'the MoT Financial System · Version 000 · revision 0.012' — the record's project block word for word. A pill says DEPOSIT, a rail says Step 1 of 5, an amber card says YOUR TURN — Sign in. Under it 'EXAMPLE · the operator's paycheck as data'. I understand: this is someone else's paycheck until I sign in. I would press Sign in.

10–25 s. 'Your deposits: 1. 2026.10.01_07.00..00 · Income / Wages (take-home) · PAYROLL · PENDING DIRECT DEPOSIT — $3,604.49 · 30.333'. I read the amount; I do not know what 30.333 is (no unit on the roster, the record row, or the chart's second line — :215, :370, :447). Then the balance block: 'Released · Deposit $0.00 · In escrow $0.00 · Withdrawable $0.00 · Withdrawn $0.00 · Available $0.00'. Everything is zero because the example is dated tomorrow 07:00 CST. The headline of the whole system — money ticking per minute — is not visible on the day of this review; the record's example.ratePerMinUsd 0.0825 and perDayUsd 118.83 never appear on the glass until Oct 1. The chart draws a green line rising and an orange line falling with 'Pending — releases from 2026.10.01_07.00..00' and an axis 0 h 0 min · 7 d 13 h · 15 d 3 h · 22 d 17 h · 30 d 7 h. I understand a month-long release. I press the orbit icon: the axis becomes five three-line marks 2669 / .0833 / ..0514 …, the title becomes '0.0000..0000 / 298.3463..2448 A-units of the revolution', the year line below becomes 2663.1922..2811. I press the clock icon: everything returns. That toggle is the most consistent thing on the page.

25–35 s. YEAR POSITION — THE ORBIT (A.B..C): Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91 · 33-day frame 9 · 7/33 · SOURCED · 271/365 · Perihelion 2026.01.03_11.15..00 CST. I checked it by hand; every number derives from the same instant. (Small wrinkle: the label says '(A.B..C)' even in clock mode, where the value shown is 271/365 — lexicon-data.ts:2931.)

35–50 s. The forms are behind sign-in, so I read them. A deposit asks Amount · Deposit day and time (CST) · MoT · length in days [30.333] · Memo · Section [A · Income] · Field [Income / Wages (take-home)] · Timeline [Every 33 days]. Here the stranger stops: the same deposit is told it lasts 30.333 days and 33 days. The withdrawal asks 'Deposit day and time' for a withdrawal (:314). The section→field pickers are right — the field list changes with the section (:160-167), every picker w-full (:156) — and when I record, the entry carries field and recurrence into the hash (:190, :197). But the recurrence I chose is never printed back to me anywhere (:215, :373), while FIN-03.02 and the surface gate's own message say it is.

50–60 s. PERSONAL FINANCE LADDER A–M. Unit dropdown full width, 'per 33 days'. My sheet is here: A · Income $3,200.00 / Income / Wages (take-home); B · Housing $700.00 / Rent / Mortgage; C · Transportation $1,800.00 / Auto Payment; D · Insurance $200.00 / Auto / Renters / Home; E · Utilities $150.00 / Electric / Gas; F · Food $300.00 / Groceries; G · Health $50.00 / Mental Health / Physical Fitness; H I J K $0.0000; L · Lifestyle $200.00 / Fun / Hobbies / Clothing; M $0.0000; Net −$200.00 in red. I switch to per minute: Net −$0.0042. Per year: −$2,212.12. I checked all eight units independently; every figure agrees to the cent with the record's FIN-06.05 dtm. What jars: the five empty sections print $0.0000 while their neighbours print $3,200.00 — and at per second my Fitness line ($50) reads $0.0000, indistinguishable from the empty Family line. Then the note: 'Cash, investments, debts … live on the Balance view — never per minute.' I look for the Balance view. There is none; the record says r.013.

THE RECORD panel: '2026.10.01_07.00..00 · Deposit · PAYROLL · PENDING DIRECT DEPOSIT — $3,604.49 · 30.333 · On this phone only — a cloud copy follows once sign-in carries it'. Plain, vendor-free, consistent with the sign-in card. The strip at the bottom reads '$0.00 · $0.00 DEPOSIT' — two numbers, no nouns.

WHAT THE BRIEF PROMISES THAT I DO NOT SEE. (1) 'Warn' on a premium in both B and D — the model warns (ladder.ts:96), the glass has no line for it (:138 never reads totals.warnings). (2) 'N–T on a second surface' — named on the glass, not built (record: draft). (3) 'Each transaction has an MoT timeline' — built twice (motDays and recurrence), which is worse than once. (4) The detail sheet and U entry — correctly deferred and said so in the record; the glass does not pretend otherwise. (5) 'Field labels match this brief exactly' — they do, all sixty, on the glass and in the lexicon.

CONSISTENCY VERDICT. Record ⇄ model: strong (sections, fields, periods, Net, U refusals, planes all gated). Model ⇄ ladder table: strong (every unit matches). Ladder ⇄ chart ⇄ roster ⇄ record: adequate, with the two-lengths defect being the one place the surface contradicts itself in numbers rather than words. Glass ⇄ record on what exists: two overstatements (Balance view; timeline shown) and one gate whose sentence outruns its regex (financial-surface.test.mjs:52). Small drifts: the :430 comment's 298.3475..1826 vs the rendered 298.3463..2448 (91/3 vs 30.333 — the operator's own month written two ways across mot.ts:59 and the record's example.motDays).

Evidence lives under scratchpad/fleet-fin/: aset-walk.log (all eight units, both orientations), aset-math.mjs (independent arithmetic), walk-portrait-full.png, ladder-m33/min/sec-*.png, chart-clock/mot-*.png, year-*.png, ledger-*.png, strip-*.png. The export I probed was rebuilt at 23:07 under the bookkeeping commit 5c9d3cf; I diffed it against 201a8ec — only the generated ledger/domain stamps changed, no component or library code — so the captures stand for r.012.

Grade B−: the lock is honoured where it is hardest (the model and the math), and the surface is honest about most of what it has not built; it loses the grade on the one deposit told two lengths, the view it names but does not have, the timeline it keeps but never shows, and the $0.0000 rows that make an empty section and a small one read alike."

### Aset · adversarial — grade B−

_ADVERSARIAL PASS (B) on Financial-2525 v.000_r.012 = 201a8ec, read-only, from the commit (git show) plus headless measurements of frontend/out at 390×844 and 844×390 and pure-model probes of ladder.ts / record.ts extracted from the commit. Probe files and captures under scratchpad/fleet-fin/aset-b/ (probe.mjs, math-probe.mjs, probe-report.json, aset-b-*.png)._

**One sentence:** The model (ladder.ts) is exact and honest — sixty fields, the locked Net at all eight periods, a switch that is one constant to 2e-13 — but the glass and the record say two things the code does not do: the lock's rule 5 says "warn; do not double-count Net" and netLadder double-counts (warning never shown), and FD-26 / FIN-03.02 say "the timeline is the transaction's MoT" while the surface still runs $/min off a separate MoT field and never shows the timeline again after entry.

**Findings**

- **Rule 5 half-implemented: a premium in both B and D is warned about but still double-counted in Net, and the warning never reaches the glass** (major)
  - Evidence: Brief (docs/asks/…mot_financial_system.md, addendum 22, UI rule 5): "If a premium is entered in both, warn; do not double-count Net." ladder.ts:105-106 adds B.housing_insurance into sections.B and D.auto_renters_home into sections.D; ladder.ts:98,108 LIVING sums both; ladder.ts:110 comment says "Net counts what was entered". Probe (math-probe.mjs): both at 120/33 d → living 240, net −240, warnings [DUPLICATE_INSURANCE]. financial-ladder.test.mjs:49-51 asserts only the warning, never Net. command-ux1.tsx has no reference to totals.warnings (grep empty) — nothing on the surface can show it. The record softened the lock: FIN-06.05.statement says only "a premium in both B and D warns".
  - Fix: Decide on the record which premium is the policy (the brief: "Housing insurance stays in B; D is the policy map") and have netLadder count one of them (or return net and netDeduplicated), render totals.warnings under the ladder, and add a test asserting Net with both entered equals Net with one.
- **FD-26 and FIN-03.02 claim the timeline IS the transaction's MoT; the glass keeps two lengths per deposit and only motDays drives $/min — the worked example itself carries 30.333 and "every 33 days"** (major)
  - Evidence: Record decisions[25] FD-26: "the timeline is the transaction's MoT: its $/min runs from its date for that length"; crs[27] FIN-03.02.statement: "a transaction's $/min runs from its date for its timeline's length" (status implemented). command-ux1.tsx:85 EXAMPLE = { motDays: SRC.example.motDays (30.333), recurrence: "days33" }; :145 dMot defaults to payMotDays 30.333; :151 dRec defaults to "days33" (33 d); :190 commits both. accrual.ts:53,75 read motDays only. command-ux1.tsx:48 imports neither actualsByField nor recurrenceDays (grep empty) — ladder.ts:126-146 are model-only. Probe: the paycheck runs 8.252 ¢/min on its MoT, 7.585 on "every 33 days", 2.751 on "Monthly (91)" — a person who picks the natural word "Monthly" for a monthly paycheck records a length three times too long for the actuals that r.013 is meant to show.
  - Fix: One length per transaction: either derive motDays from the timeline (with Other → typed days, as addendum 25 now asks) or derive the timeline from motDays; mark FIN-03.02 partially implemented until the surface reads the timeline; rename fin.rec.month91 so "Monthly" is not 91 days on a personal form.
- **The recorded timeline is invisible after entry — roster and record print the field and motDays, never the recurrence** (medium)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:215 roster label = `${usd(d.amountCents)} · ${d.motDays}`; :373 record row prints rev · stamp · kind · txWhat (field/category) · memo · amount · motDays · hash; the only use of fin.rec.* is the picker option list (:171). FIN-03.02.dtm promises "a withdrawal typed as B · Rent / Mortgage · Monthly (91) appears on the record as Rent / Mortgage" — the timeline is written into the hash and then never shown, so the person cannot verify what was recorded.
  - Fix: Print t(`fin.rec.${tx.recurrence}`) beside the field on the record row and the roster; add a surface assertion that the timeline of a committed entry is rendered.
- **"PERSONAL FINANCE LADDER A–M" is the operator's seed sheet for every signed-in person; the record's own fields never reach it** (medium)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:138 `netLadder(SHEET_LINES, period)`; budget.ts:77 SHEET_LINES maps SHEET_BUDGET (the operator's eight lines) onto fields. Nothing on the ladder reads replay(record). The title (fin.ladder_title) and the note say nothing about it being an example, unlike the chart's fin.example_badge. FIN-06.06 (draft, r.013) owns the actuals column, so the gap is declared on the record but not on the glass.
  - Fix: Until r.013, label the table as the sheet example for a signed-in person (reuse fin.example_badge), or feed actualsByField(replay(record)) into a second column now.
- **One column, two precisions, and the printed rows do not foot to the printed Net** (medium)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:141 usdUnit = |cents| ≥ 100 ? 2 decimals : 4 decimals. Measured at 390×844 (probe-report.json): per 33 days "H · Family $0.0000 … L · Lifestyle $200.00"; per hour "A · Income $4.04 · B · Housing $0.8838 · C · Transportation $2.27 · D · Insurance $0.2525". Printed rows vs printed Net: day rows sum −6.07 vs Net −6.06; hour −0.2501 vs −0.2525; minute −0.0043 vs −0.0042; month (91) −551.53 vs −551.52; year −2212.11 vs −2212.12 (5 of 8 periods). The model has no drift (round-trip worst 2.3e-13); the glass introduces the cent.
  - Fix: One precision per unit (choose it from the unit, not the magnitude): 2 decimals for day and above, 4 below; print a zero as $0.00 in that unit; foot the section totals from the printed field figures or say the Net is computed unrounded.
- **On Mars the year panel says SOURCED · 271/365 and, on the next line, "no perihelion table for this planet yet — declared, not sourced"** (medium)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:290 prints `year.status` and `${year.day}/${Math.ceil(year.lengthDays)}`; calendar.ts:98-101 anchors every planet on Earth's PERIHELION table, so status is SOURCED and lengthDays is Earth's 364.4-day interval, while calendar.ts:111 computes abc on yearDays = 686.98. Measured (aset-b-portrait-mars-year.png): "Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91 · 33-day frame 9 · 7/33 | 2026.09.30_17.09..36 CST · 2026 · SOURCED · 1416.0643..2238 | The year position is on the perihelion anchor; no perihelion table for this planet yet — declared, not sourced". Day 271 of 365 and 1416 A of 3600 disagree about the whole (271/686.98 × 3600 ≈ 1420 is the A reading; the day count is Earth's).
  - Fix: When the planet has no perihelion table, positionInYear should return status DECLARED and lengthDays = yearDays (or the panel should hide day/quarter/frame for that planet); one truth per line.
- **Three writings of the worked example's MoT: 30.333 on the glass and in example.motDays, 91÷3 in accrual.ts and payMotNote, 298.97 in example.motInA** (low)
  - Evidence: financial-2525.v.000.json line 361 example.motDays 30.333; line 368 example.motInA 298.97 (r.003's reading); line 243 mot.payMotNote "298.3475..1826 A" computed on 91 ÷ 3; accrual.ts:135 exampleRatePerMin defaults motDays = 91/3; command-ux1.tsx:85 EXAMPLE.motDays = SRC.example.motDays = 30.333 → chart title measured "0.0000..0000 / 298.3463..2448 A-UNITS" (probe-report earthAbc.chartTitle). The record's note and the glass differ by 12 B-units on the same paycheck.
  - Fix: One primitive for the example length (91/3 or 30.333, on the record), example.motInA re-pinned to the r.007 whole, and a test that the chart's motAbc equals the record's figure.
- **A second entry with the same stamp and amount is silently dropped — the amount clears as if recorded** (medium)
  - Evidence: record.ts:34-35 append() returns the same record when tx.id already exists; command-ux1.tsx:190 id = `d-${when}-${cents}` (a typed stamp + the same amount collides), :191 then clears dAmt/dMemo; no refusal is set (:187-189 only check amount and stamp). Probe: append of the same id with a different memo → entries stay 1, same object. Two $50 grocery withdrawals typed for the same day and time: one on the record, nothing said. Same for withdrawals (:197).
  - Fix: Make the id unique (include a sequence or memo hash) or refuse with a reason when append returns the same record; the surface must never clear the form on a no-op.
- **A record that fails its chain is overwritten by the next entry, while the label still says the chain is broken** (medium)
  - Evidence: record.ts:65 loadRecord returns emptyRecord when verify() fails; command-ux1.tsx:114 sets that empty record; :177 commit() appends to it and saveRecord(next) writes over the tampered store — the evidence is gone on the first new deposit. `tampered` is never cleared, so :368 prints fin.chain_broken ("shown empty, never repaired silently") above a list of freshly verified entries.
  - Fix: Keep the failed store under a sidecar key before any write, refuse commits while tampered (or require an explicit "start a new record" action), and clear/keep the label from the actual state of what is listed.
- **The lock's own example destination id does not resolve** (low)
  - Evidence: Addendum 22, UI rule 7: `mapsTo: "B.property_tax"`. ladder.ts:52 names the field `B.property_tax_hoa_fees`. Probe: amortize({amount:1200, periodDays:365, mapsTo:"B.property_tax"}, "days33") → { refused: "NOT_A_FLOW_FIELD" }. The U entry is r.013 (FIN-06.06), so no person hits it yet, but the brief's example is refused by the model the brief locked.
  - Fix: Accept the brief's short id as an alias (fieldOf resolves a prefix match within the section) or record on FD-25 that ids are section.slug of the full label.
- **At the shipped SHA the 90 ladder keys read English in 32 languages; the gate passes them as PENDING; the export in frontend/out is not r.012's export** (low)
  - Evidence: lexicon-data.ts:2999-3088 (90 keys); lexicon-coverage.test.mjs:19-30 AFTER_FILL lists them; the gate run from the 201a8ec archive prints "PENDING es: 154 key(s) added after the last fill are English" for each language and passes; i18n-app/es.ts at 201a8ec carries 0 fin.sec/field/rec keys. - ladder.ts is the brief, verbatim and pure: 20 sections in the brief's order, 60 fields with per-section counts A5 B6 C5 D4 E4 F3 G4 H2 I2 J3 K2 L3 M3 N1 O3 P2 Q2 R1 S2 T3, labels exact; U never a section or field (ladder.ts:31-76; financial-ladder.test.mjs 59 assertions pass from the commit's archive).

- FD-25 says on the record what the code does: the ladder's year is 365 days while the MoT's whole stays 365.259636 — two constants, both named, neither hidden (ladder.ts:13-15; record decisions[24]).

**Report (verbatim)**

ASET · ADVERSARIAL PASS · Financial-2525 v.000_r.012 (201a8ec) · grade B−

Method. Every file read from the commit with git show; the pure model probed by extracting ladder.ts and record.ts from 201a8ec (scratchpad/fleet-fin/aset-b/math-probe.mjs); the r.012 gates re-run from a git-archive of the commit (financial-ladder 59/59, financial-surface 63/63, lexicon-coverage PENDING 154 keys per language); the export served from frontend/out and measured headless at 390×844 and 844×390 (probe.mjs → probe-report.json, seven captures). Note for the fleet: frontend/out is dated 23:07:26 and already carries the ×32 ladder fill committed at 50e57c0 (23:08) — it renders Spanish section names — so it is the post-fill export, not r.012's byte-for-byte. The signed-in forms cannot be reached headless; their widths were measured on a same-class replica of command-ux1.tsx:156-175 inside the :301 grid.

WHERE THE LADDER AGREES WITH THE RECORD. The model is the lock. Sections, fields, planes, the eight fixed periods, Net = I − L − Ds − Tx − Tr, transfers as outflow, P never netted, U refusing N–T — all in ladder.ts and all proven by behaviour tests, not regexes. toPeriod is one constant (worst round-trip error 2.3e-13 over 100,000 amounts). The glass renders thirteen rows with thirteen icons, the fields beneath, Net last and red (rgb 239,68,68). The unit dropdown is 290 of 292 px in portrait, 224 px in landscape. Old entries hash the same with the new optional keys. This half deserves an A.

WHERE IT DOES NOT.
1. Rule 5 (major). The brief says "warn; do not double-count Net". ladder.ts:105-110 counts both premiums and warns; the test (financial-ladder.test.mjs:49-51) checks only the warning; the surface never renders totals.warnings. The record's FIN-06.05 statement quietly dropped the second half of the rule.
2. FD-26 / FIN-03.02 (major). Both say the timeline IS the transaction's MoT and that $/min runs for the timeline's length. On the glass a deposit has two lengths — MoT 30.333 (command-ux1.tsx:145) and timeline "every 33 days" (:151) — the worked example carries both (:85), accrual reads only motDays (accrual.ts:75), and actualsByField/recurrenceDays are not imported (:48). "Monthly (91)" on a monthly paycheck would run at 2.75 ¢/min against 8.25 on its MoT. FIN-03.02 is marked implemented; its $/min sentence is not.
3. The timeline vanishes after entry (medium): roster :215 and record row :373 never print tx.recurrence.
4. The "personal" ladder is the operator's seed for everyone (:138 netLadder(SHEET_LINES)) with no example badge; declared as r.013 on the record, not on the glass.
5. Precision (medium): usdUnit (:141) picks decimals by magnitude, so "$0.0000" sits under "$3,200.00" and "$4.04" beside "$0.8838" in one column; printed rows fail to foot to the printed Net at five of eight periods by a cent.
6. Mars (medium): "2026 · SOURCED · 271/365" and "declared, not sourced" on one panel (:290-292; calendar.ts:98-101 anchors every planet on Earth's table); day 271 of 365 against 1416 A of a 686.98-day whole.
7. The record disagrees with itself on the worked paycheck's MoT: 30.333 (example.motDays, the glass, 298.3463..2448), 91÷3 (payMotNote 298.3475..1826, accrual.ts:135), 298.97 (example.motInA).
8. The record can say less than what happened (medium): a second entry with the same typed stamp and amount is a silent no-op (record.ts:35; :190-191 clears the form); a chain that fails verification is overwritten by the next commit while the label still says broken (record.ts:65; :114, :177, :368).
9. The brief's own mapsTo example "B.property_tax" is refused by amortize (ladder.ts:52 names it B.property_tax_hoa_fees).
10. At the shipped SHA the 90 keys read English in 32 languages (staged, PENDING, gate green); filled six minutes later.
11. The stock note points to a Balance view that is r.013.
12. Gates: the surface gate is regex-only; the pickers' full-width claim has no gate and no capture of the signed-in forms (replica measures 290/292 portrait, 330×2 landscape).

Verdict. The lock was implemented as a model and gated as behaviour — the right order, and the operator asked for exactly that. But the record now carries two claims the surface does not keep (rule 5's second half; the timeline as the MoT), and a reader of the glass meets four figures that disagree with each other (two precisions, Mars' two wholes, the example's three MoT writings, rows that do not foot). Aset's grade is B−: reinforce before extending — fold 1, 2, 3, 5 and 8 into r.013 alongside addendum 24's one form, and let the record say "partial" where it is partial.

### Aset · synthesis — grade B-

**One sentence:** The model keeps the lock to the cent at all eight units and the record is honest about what it has not built, but the glass tells one deposit two lengths, promises a Balance view that is r.013, hashes a timeline it never shows, half-keeps rule 5 while the record quietly drops its second half, and prints one column in two precisions so an empty section and a small one read alike.

**Top three**

- ONE DEPOSIT, TWO LENGTHS (major). frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:145 defaults the MoT field to 30.333 (SRC.mot.payMotDays) while :151 defaults the Timeline to "days33" (33 d); :190 commits BOTH on one entry; the worked example :85 carries both; accrual.ts:75 runs $/min off motDays only, and :48 never imports actualsByField/recurrenceDays — yet FD-26 (record decisions, line 1125) and FIN-03.02's statement (line 937, status implemented) say "the timeline IS the transaction's MoT: its $/min runs from its date for that length". Independent check: the paycheck runs 8.25 c/min on 30.333 d, 7.59 on "every 33 days", 2.75 on "Monthly (91)" (lexicon-data.ts:3082) — a person picking the natural word "Monthly" for a monthly paycheck records a length three times too long. FIX: one length per transaction — derive motDays from the timeline (Other → typed length, as addendum 25 asks) and make the accrual read that one length; the example carries one; mark FIN-03.02 partial until the surface reads the timeline; do not offer "Monthly (91)" as a recurrence on a personal form. Gate: a committed entry has exactly one length. (HEAD r.013 already derives motDays = lengthDays(rec, …) at :232/:239 — verify the EXAMPLE :129, which still carries both, and re-pin FIN-03.02.)
- RULE 5 HALF-KEPT, THE WARNING UNREACHABLE, THE RECORD SOFTENED (major by the lock, latent in exposure). Brief addendum 22 UI rule 5: "If a premium is entered in both, warn; do not double-count Net." frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts:104-111 sums B.housing_insurance into B and D.auto_renters_home into D, LIVING (:98) counts both, :110's comment says "Net counts what was entered" — the second half of the rule is not implemented; tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs:49-51 asserts only the warning, never Net; command-ux1.tsx:138 stores totals and has ZERO references to totals.warnings (grep: 0), so the warning the brief and FIN-06.05's metric say "fires" fires in a test only; FIN-06.05's statement (record line 926) reads "a premium in both B and D warns" — the lock's "do not double-count Net" is gone from the record. FIX: decide on the record which premium is the policy (the brief: housing insurance stays in B; D is the policy map) and have netLadder count one of them (or return net and netDeduplicated), add a test asserting Net(both) === Net(one), render totals.warnings as one amber line under the ladder (lexicon key), and restore rule 5's second half in FIN-06.05. (Still as-is at HEAD: ladder.ts:110, no warnings render.)
- THE GLASS SAYS MORE THAN EXISTS AND SHOWS LESS THAN IT RECORDS (major, two members of one class). (a) fin.stock_note (lexicon-data.ts:3088, rendered command-ux1.tsx:363) sends the reader to "the Balance view"; no route, tab or button named Balance exists, and the record's own FIN-06.06 (line 955-968) is status draft, "the surface lands as r.013". (b) The timeline rides the hash (:190, :197) but is printed nowhere: roster label :215 is stamp · field · memo / amount · motDays, record row :373 is rev · stamp · kind · field · memo · amount · motDays · hash — no recurrence, and no unit on the bare "30.333" (:215, :370, :447); yet FIN-03.02's statement says the timeline is "shown on the roster and the record" and tests/financial-surface.test.mjs:52's message claims the same while its regexes match only the field — the gate's sentence outruns its check. FIX: print t(`fin.rec.${tx.recurrence}`) after the field on both :215 and :373 (and a unit after motDays), extend the surface gate to match the recurrence print so its sentence is true; reword the note as a promise ("will live on a Balance view — next revision") until FIN-06.06 ships, then make it a link. (Still as-is at HEAD: stock_note unchanged; fin.rec.* appears only in the picker.)

**Report (verbatim)**

ASET · SYNTHESIS · Financial-2525 v.000_r.012 (commit 201a8ec) · grade B−

METHOD. Every file read from the commit with git show, never from the working tree: command-ux1.tsx, category-icon.tsx, ladder.ts, budget.ts, accrual.ts, record.ts, mot.ts, calendar.ts, planets.ts, the ask (addendum 22 — the lock — plus 24–25), the record JSON, the lexicon rows, and the two gates (financial-surface, financial-ladder). Both reviewers' numbers were re-derived independently (A-units of 30.333 vs 91/3; the three $/min rates; the printed-row footing at six units; the Mars elapsed/A reading). Their captures were opened (ladder-m33-portrait, strip-portrait, aset-b-portrait-mars-year, p-01-first-screen). I did not re-probe frontend/out: it is now stamped revision 0.017 (built 00:38) and even the 23:19 capture already reads "revision 0.013 · SHA 50e57c0", so only the 23:07 captures and the code at 201a8ec stand for r.012 — and every claim below is confirmed in that code, line by line. HEAD was read once, so the owed list does not re-report what r.013 already carries.

WHERE EVERY SCREEN, KEY AND FIGURE SAYS THE SAME THING (both reviewers, verified).
· The ladder is the brief verbatim and pure: twenty sections in the brief's order, sixty fields with the slash-merged labels exactly, U never a section (ladder.ts:31-76); the lexicon rows (lexicon-data.ts:2999-3078) carry the same words; the gate pins them with behaviour tests, not regexes.
· The locked Net holds at all eight units and a unit switch is one constant (ladder.ts:88): A 3,200 · B 700 · C 1,800 · D 200 · E 150 · F 300 · G 50 · L 200 → Net −$200.00 per 33 days, −$0.0042 per minute, −$2,212.12 per year — my arithmetic, the record's FIN-06.05 dtm and the captured table agree to the cent. P never nets against I; unknown and negative lines are never counted.
· One word per thing: the section and field labels on the pickers, the ladder rows and the record rows come from the same keys (command-ux1.tsx:154-155, :161, :166, :354-356, :180).
· The MoT ⇄ Clock toggle is one state for the whole card (:107, :270, :290, :433); the year line checks by hand (Day 271 · Q3 · 89/91 · frame 9 · 7/33 · 2663.19 A from the same 2026.01.03_11.15 CST perihelion).
· The unit dropdown is genuinely full width in portrait (290 of 292 px) and its own width in landscape (224 px) — measured, not asserted.
· Old entries hash the same with the new optional keys (record.ts:22-23); the record marks FIN-06.04/FIN-03.01 superseded rather than rewritten and FIN-06.06 draft rather than claimed; FD-25 says on the record that the ladder's 365-day year and the MoT's 365.259636-day whole are two deliberate constants.
This half is A-grade work, and it is the half the operator said matters most ("model first; styling second").

WHERE IT DOES NOT — reconciled and ranked.

1. One deposit, two lengths (major; both reviewers; verified at :85, :145, :151, :190; accrual.ts:75; :48 imports neither actualsByField nor recurrenceDays). The MoT field says 30.333, the Timeline says "Every 33 days", both ride the same entry, and only motDays drives $/min — while FD-26 and FIN-03.02 (status implemented) say the timeline IS the MoT. 8.25 ¢/min on 30.333 d; 7.59 on 33; 2.75 on "Monthly (91)" — the natural word for a monthly paycheck records a length three times too long for the actuals r.013 is meant to show. This is the one place the surface contradicts itself in numbers rather than words.

2. Rule 5 half-kept, the warning unreachable, the record softened (major by the lock, latent in exposure; walk's minor + adversarial's major merged). ladder.ts:104-111 counts both premiums and warns; the gate (financial-ladder.test.mjs:49-51) checks only the warning; the component has zero references to totals.warnings; FIN-06.05's statement (record line 926) says "warns" and drops the lock's "do not double-count Net". No person can hit this on r.012 (the ladder is the seed sheet), which is why it does not pull the grade below B−; but the lock is the lock, and the record now says less than the brief without saying so.

3. The glass says more than exists and shows less than it records (major; two members of one class). (a) fin.stock_note (lexicon-data.ts:3088, rendered :363) names a Balance view; FIN-06.06 is draft, r.013. (b) The timeline is hashed (:190, :197) and printed nowhere (:215, :373 — nor is there a unit on the bare "30.333" at :215, :370, :447), while FIN-03.02's statement and the surface gate's own message (financial-surface.test.mjs:52) claim it is shown; the regex checks only the field. A gate whose sentence outruns its check is the exact failure the lexicon and signer-voice gates exist to prevent.

4. One column, two precisions (moderate; both reviewers; verified at :141 and in the captures). usdUnit picks decimals by magnitude, so "H · Family $0.0000" sits under "A · Income $3,200.00", "$4.04" beside "$0.8838" per hour, and at per second Fitness ($50/33 d = $0.0000175/s) prints "$0.0000" — identical to the truly empty Family row. The adversarial's footing claim holds but needs its honest shape: at day/month/year the one-cent miss is the ordinary rounding of independently rounded rows; the real defect is hour and minute, where mixed precision makes the printed rows foot to −0.2501 against a printed Net of −0.2525.

5. Mars: two truths on one panel (moderate; adversarial; verified calendar.ts:98-113, command-ux1.tsx:290-292, capture). Every planet is anchored on Earth's perihelion table, so the line reads "2026 · SOURCED · 271/365" and the next line reads "no perihelion table for this planet yet — declared, not sourced"; Day 271 of 365 (Earth's interval) against 1416 A of a 686.98-day whole. My re-derivation: 270.25 d elapsed → 1416.2 A on Mars, 2663.5 on Earth — the panel mixes them.

6. The record can say less than what happened (moderate; adversarial; verified record.ts:35, :65; command-ux1.tsx:114, :177, :190-191). A second entry with the same typed stamp and amount is a silent no-op and the form clears as if recorded; a store that fails verify() reads as empty and is overwritten by the next commit, while the chain_broken label (:368) stays above freshly verified entries. Both are consistency defects in the strictest sense: the glass and the store disagree about what was kept.

7. The ladder is the operator's sheet for everyone (minor; both). :138 netLadder(SHEET_LINES) under "Personal finance ladder A–M" with no example badge, unlike the deposit block (:253, :328). Declared on the record (FIN-06.06), not on the glass.

8. Small drifts (minor): the strip's "$0.00 · $0.00" with no nouns (:392); "Released · Deposit" as a heading when no rate runs (:262); the withdrawal's date labelled "Deposit day and time" (:314); an unknown field id wearing section L's icon (:180); the year label saying "(A.B..C)" in clock mode (lexicon-data.ts:2931); the pay MoT written as 91/3 in mot.ts:59, accrual.ts:135 and the record's payMotNote (298.3475..1826) but 30.333 in payMotDays, example.motDays, the form default and the chart (298.3463..2448), with the :430 comment quoting the former while the glass renders the latter; the brief's own mapsTo example "B.property_tax" refused by amortize (ladder.ts:52 names B.property_tax_hoa_fees).

CLAIMS ADJUSTED OR DROPPED, AND WHY.
· "Q and R share one icon" (adversarial): true at category-icon.tsx:37, but N–T are never rendered on the r.012 glass (the pickers list FLOW_SECTIONS, the ladder rows are A–M), so no two glyphs collide on any screen; HEAD already gives Q its own stroke. Dropped as a defect.
· "Three writings of the example's MoT" (adversarial): 298.3463..2448 and 298.3475..1826 are both ≈ 298.97 in decimal, so example.motInA is a rounding of either, not a third length. It is two lengths (30.333 vs 91/3) written in several places. Kept, reworded.
· "Rows do not foot to Net at five of eight periods" (adversarial): kept for hour and minute (mixed precision), softened for day/month/year (ordinary cent rounding).
· "The export is not r.012's" (adversarial): true and now further gone (r.017). Kept as bookkeeping, not as a defect of r.012.
· The walk's reading of 298.97 "rounding both" and its unit-less "30.333" on the roster: verified and kept.

WHAT R.013 OWES — see the owed list; in short: one length per transaction (HEAD carries the derivation; the example and the record still need re-pinning), rule 5 whole with the warning on the glass and FIN-06.05 restored, the timeline printed where the record says it is printed and the gate made true, the stock note as a promise, one precision per unit with zero as $0.00, the Mars panel one truth, the record never silently dropping or overwriting, the ladder badged as the sheet, and the small drifts in one pass.

VERDICT. B−. The lock was implemented as a model and gated as behaviour — the right order, and the operator asked for exactly that; the ladder, the record and the brief agree to the cent at every unit. The grade is lost on the glass and the record's words: one deposit told two lengths, a view named but not built, a timeline kept but never shown, a rule the record now states by half, and a column whose two precisions let an empty section and a small one read the same. Reinforce before extending: fold 1–6 into the next revision alongside addendum 24's one form, and let the record say "partial" wherever it is partial.
