## Enki

### Enki · walk — grade C+

_The constructive walk — a stranger with the operator's own sheet, on a phone, 60 seconds on /financial-2525 at v.000_r.012 (commit 201a8ec)_

**One sentence:** The A–U model underneath is exact and gated, but the r.012 glass records the transaction timeline without ever showing it or using it, closes its own dropdowns every second, offers two disagreeing lengths for one deposit, and says "Done" while the 3-hour hold is still running — so a stranger walks away trusting the ladder and not the forms.

**Findings**

- **The timeline dropdown is write-only: recorded in the hash, never printed, never read by the ladder** (high)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:169-173 renders the TIMELINE select; :190 and :197 store `recurrence` on the entry; but the roster row (:215) prints `${fmtStampCST} · field · memo` and the record line (:373) prints kind · field · memo · amount · motDays · hash — no recurrence anywhere. `actualsByField` (frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts:134) is never imported; the ladder is `netLadder(SHEET_LINES, period)` (:138) with no actuals column. FIN-03.02 (docs/financial-2525/financial-2525.v.000.json) claims 'the field and the timeline ride the entry … shown on the roster and the record' — the timeline is not shown. A stranger picks 'Weekly', presses Record it, and nothing on the page changes.
  - Fix: Print the timeline on the roster and the record beside the field (`t(`fin.rec.${tx.recurrence}`)`), and either wire `actualsByField` over the current 33-day frame into an actuals column on the ladder or downgrade FIN-03.02's statement to 'recorded' until FIN-06.06 lands. Add the recurrence to the surface gate's roster/record regex so the claim cannot outrun the glass again.
- **Every picker on both forms remounts on the 1 Hz tick — an open dropdown closes by itself** (high)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:157 declares `const LadderPicker = (…) => (…)` INSIDE `FinancialCommandUX1`; :95 sets `now` every second, so each render creates a new component identity and React unmounts/remounts the three selects (section, field, timeline). On a phone the native picker sheet is dismissed within a second of opening. The operator reported exactly this on the r.011/r.012 glass (addendum 26, 'this drop down goes away quick!', commit 6da8a3c) and r.014 hoists the component — confirming the r.012 defect.
  - Fix: Hoist `LadderPicker` to module level (take `t` as a prop), as r.014 does; add a headless gate that opens a select, waits three ticks, and asserts the same DOM node is still open/focused.
- **One deposit, two lengths that disagree: MoT 30.333 days AND timeline 'Every 33 days'** (medium)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:145 defaults `dMot` to 30.333 (SRC.mot.payMotDays); :151 defaults `dRec` to "days33". The deposit form (:304 and :306) therefore shows 'MoT · length in days: 30.333' and 'Timeline: Every 33 days' for the same money. Escrow runs at amountCents ÷ (motDays × 1440) (accrual.ts:75) = 8.252 ¢/min for the worked paycheck; the timeline's `recurrenceDays('days33')` = 33 d (ladder.ts:126) would give 7.585 ¢/min. A stranger cannot tell which one is 'the' $/min, and the ask says a transaction has ONE MoT timeline established at entry.
  - Fix: One length per transaction: derive `motDays` from the timeline (with an 'Other' entry for 30.333 — what r.013 did), or label the MoT field as the escrow release length and the timeline as the budget period and make the defaults agree (both 30.333 or both 33).
- **A zero MoT (or any MoT under 3 hours) reads 'Done' and phase RECORD while nothing is withdrawable** (medium)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:74-80 `phaseOf` returns "record" when `v.fraction >= 1` BEFORE checking the hold; :210 then prints the guide 'Done — the whole deposit is released and on the record'. Probe: motDays 0 and motDays 1/24 at +1 h → phase=record, released=$3,604.49, withdrawable=$0.00 (accrual.ts:59 releases a zero-length MoT at once; :77 holds it until +3 h). The strip's Withdraw button (:394) is disabled at the same moment the guide says done — the panel claims more than happened.
  - Fix: Order the phases hold-first: if `now < holdUntilMs` return "hold" regardless of fraction; only 'record' when fraction ≥ 1 AND the hold has passed. Gate it with the 1-hour and 0-day MoT cases in financial-accrual.test.mjs.
- **Mars: the chart's A.B..C sub-line reads Earth positions while the axis reads Mars; the year panel says SOURCED and 'declared, not sourced' in the same breath** (medium)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:447 calls `positionInYear(from, planet.yearAnchor)` and `positionInYear(to, planet.yearAnchor)` WITHOUT `planet.yearDays`, while the axis at :433 passes `planet.yearDays` — probe: 2669.0833..0514 (Earth) vs 1419.0722..1931 (Mars) for the same instant; it shows once the example deposit starts releasing (after 2026-10-01 07:00) with the MoT icon on. :290 prints `year.status` = SOURCED and :292 prints fin.anchor_note 'no perihelion table for this planet yet — declared, not sourced' on the same panel (captured: walk-mars-year.png). Root: frontend/lib/financial-2525/calendar.ts:98-101 anchors every planet's year on EARTH's perihelion table (`perihelionOf(g)`), so Mars' 'Day 271 · Quarter 3' counts Earth days from Earth's perihelion and its `status` is Earth's.
  - Fix: Pass `planet.yearDays` at :447 (the r.008 fix reached the axis, not the sub-line); give `positionInYear` a planet-aware anchor (a Mars perihelion instant, DECLARED) and print DECLARED for a planet without a table, or hide the SOURCED word when the anchor note is shown.
- **Per-second view flattens small lines to '$0.0000' — Fitness $50 becomes indistinguishable from an empty section** (medium)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:141 `usdUnit` prints four decimals under $1. Probe and capture (walk-ladder-per-second.png): G · Health (the sheet's $50 Fitness) reads $0.0000, identical to H, I, J, K, M which hold nothing; a yearly $1,200 property tax through U at per second would also print $0.0000. In every unit, empty sections read '$0.0000' while filled ones read '$3,200.00' — two decimal conventions in one column (walk-ladder-landscape-min.png). The brief's 'no drift beyond cents' is met in the math (probe: year Net −2,212.12 = −200 × 365/33) but not in the print.
  - Fix: Print an empty section as '$0.00' (or '—') and scale decimals to the unit: at per second show enough significant digits that every non-zero sheet line is non-zero (e.g. 6 decimals below 1 ¢), or show per-second values in cents.
- **Odd dates roll over silently: 2026.02.30 is recorded as 2026.03.02, 2026.09.31 as 10.01** (medium)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/mot.ts:166-167 validates `d > 31` only, then `Date.UTC` rolls the overflow into the next month. Probe: '2026.02.30_07.00..00' → 2026.03.02_07.00..00, '2026.09.31_07.00..00' → 2026.10.01_07.00..00 (no refusal); '0000.01.01' → 1900.01.01. A typo lands a deposit on a day the person never typed, and the record is append-only.
  - Fix: After building the instant, re-read `cstParts(ms)` and refuse when y/mo/d differ from the typed parts (the stamp refusal already exists: fin.reason_stamp).
- **The deposit-time placeholder is one hour behind an Austin phone for eight months of the year, and the label only says '(CST)'** (medium)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/mot.ts:39 fixes UTC−6 (the operator's rule); frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:303 shows `fmtStampCST(now)` as the placeholder under the label 'Deposit day and time (CST)'. Probe: Austin wall clock 17:19:32 CDT on 2026-09-30 → placeholder 2026.09.30_16.19..32. A stranger copying the time off the phone's clock records an instant an hour later than intended; the year line shows the same skew (capture: 'Time: 18:25 CST' in the build stamp vs '17.35..11 CST' on the year panel, walk-390-viewport.png).
  - Fix: Keep the rule, say it on the glass: label the field '(CST standard, UTC−6 — no daylight time)' and show, beside the placeholder, the phone's local time so the person sees the offset before typing; consider a native datetime-local input that converts.
- **Two identical entries collapse silently: same instant + same amount is dropped without a refusal** (low)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:190 builds `id: d-${when}-${cents}`; frontend/lib/financial-2525/record.ts:35 returns the same record when the id exists; :177 `commit` never compares `next` to `record`, so no refusal is shown. Probe: two $150 yearly registrations typed at the same stamp → 1 entry, no message. Realistic for a yearly one-time entered from a statement date (00:00) or two same-amount withdrawals typed with an identical stamp.
  - Fix: Include a sequence (record.entries.length) or a nonce in the id, or refuse visibly when `append` returns the same record ('already on the record').
- **'Infinity' and '1e400' are accepted as amounts; the deposit prints $∞ then becomes $0 after reload** (low)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:185-187 checks `cents > 0` only (probe: Number('Infinity') → ACCEPTED, '0x10' → 1600 ACCEPTED); frontend/lib/financial-2525/record.ts:70 JSON.stringify turns Infinity into null, the chain still verifies (probe: verify=true) and accrual.ts sums null as 0 — the same entry shows two different amounts across a reload. Also '3,200' (the sheet's own comma) and '$50' are refused with 'Enter an amount above zero', a misleading reason.
  - Fix: Require `Number.isFinite(cents)` and a plain decimal regex; strip commas and a leading $ before parsing; a distinct refusal for an unreadable amount.
- **The duplicate-insurance warning exists in the model but has no place on the glass** (low)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts:110 pushes DUPLICATE_INSURANCE into `warnings`; frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:138 takes `totals` and never renders `totals.warnings`. Brief rule 5: 'If a premium is entered in both, warn'. Latent while the ladder is the fixed sheet, but the promise is not on the surface and no lexicon key is staged for it.
  - Fix: Render `totals.warnings` under the table with a staged key (fin.warn.duplicate_insurance) so the rule is visible the day budget lines become editable.
- **The MoT length prints bare ('· 30.333') on the roster, the record and the chart line** (low)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:215 `${usd(d.amountCents)} · ${d.motDays}`, :373 `· ${e.tx.motDays}`, :447 `· ${tx.motDays} ·`. A stranger reads '$3,604.49 · 30.333' (walk-390-viewport.png) and does not know it is days; the phone strip (:392) shows '$0.00 · $0.00' with no names for released vs available.
  - Fix: Append the unit (`30.333 d` or the fin.mot_days word) and label the strip's two numbers with their existing keys.
- **Two sections share one icon (Q · Net worth and R · Credit both Gauge)** (low)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/category-icon.tsx:37 `Q: Gauge, R: Gauge`. The law says every section has exactly one icon; on the coming Balance view the two rows will be told apart by the word only.
  - Fix: Give R a distinct stroke (e.g. a score/star mark) and gate icon uniqueness across SECTION_ICON.
- **The 'personal' ladder is the operator's sheet for everyone — a signed-in stranger cannot enter a single budget line** (low)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:138 `netLadder(SHEET_LINES, period)` and :355 iterate the hard-coded SHEET_LINES (frontend/lib/financial-2525/budget.ts:77). Nothing on the page edits a line; U (yearly one-time amortized into a field, ladder.ts:117) has no entry path; the 'Yearly' timeline option therefore leads nowhere on the ladder. The ask says 'first by testing and adjusting with my own finances', so this is scope, not a bug — but the title 'Personal finance ladder A–M' reads as the viewer's.
  - Fix: Until lines are editable, say on the panel whose sheet it is ('the operator's sheet as data', the same honesty the example badge uses), and plan the U entry with the N–T view (FIN-06.06).

**Keep**

- The model is the lock, verbatim and gated: 20 sections A–T, 60 fields with the brief's slash-merged labels exactly, planes, fixed period factors (never a 30-day month), U as a rule that refuses N–T — frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts with frontend/tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs.
- Net = I − L − Ds − Tx − Tr holds at every unit with no drift: probe on the operator's sheet gives −$200.00 / 33 d, −$0.0042 / min, −$2,212.12 / yr (= −200 × 365/33); a P balance never nets against income.
- The ladder table reads well on a phone: thirteen section rows with an icon, the sheet's fields beneath, Net last and red, the unit select at the panel's full width in portrait (measured 290/290 px) and its own width in landscape; no horizontal scroll at 390 px; no page errors.
- Honest sentences where the surface stops: 'Cash, investments … live on the Balance view — never per minute', 'On this phone only — a cloud copy follows once sign-in carries it', 'EXAMPLE · the operator's paycheck as data', and 'declared, not sourced' on a planet without a table.
- The Mars axis reads Mars A-units of its own 686.98-day revolution (r.008's fix is real on the glass: 1419.0722..1931 at the first mark), and the clock ladder now derives per hour / per day from the planet's LTU row rather than a typed 60/1440.
- The escrow law is pure and safe at the edges: a zero-length MoT releases at once, the 3-hour hold blocks a withdrawal at +1 s with a named reason (HOLD), a 99-year MoT accrues linearly (spanABC 356390.2151..2957, $505,050.51 released at 50 years) with no overflow; the record is append-only and chain-verified, and a tampered store says so.
- Odd stamps are mostly refused rather than guessed: month 13, second 60 and a dash-separated date are rejected with the existing fin.reason_stamp sentence.
- The year panel is one sentence a stranger can read (Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91 · 33-day frame 9 · 7/33) with the perihelion instant in Austin CST beside it; the Clock icon is the default and the MoT icon a reveal, as addenda 8, 13 and 14 ask.

**Report (verbatim)**

Read from commit 201a8ec (git show), not the working tree: command-ux1.tsx, category-icon.tsx, ladder/budget/accrual/record/mot/calendar/planets.ts, the ask (23 addenda, addendum 22 as the lock), the record JSON, the r.005–r.013 captures. The export under frontend/out had already moved on to r.014 while I read, so I probed it only for the parts the diff shows unchanged since r.012 (the ladder table, the unit select, the chart, the year panel, the phone strip) and read r.012's form code directly. Numeric edge probes ran against the r.012 pure libraries copied into fleet-fin/enki-walk (probe-edges.mjs); glass probes at 390×844 and 844×390 (glass-probe.mjs, captures walk-*.png). Nothing in the repository was touched.

THE SIXTY-SECOND WALK, as a stranger holding the operator's sheet.

0–10 s. A header I recognise from the Session — globe, three glyphs, FINANCIAL · 2525, 'the MoT Financial System · Version 000 · revision'. A card titled 'Money as time — this MoT' with a DEPOSIT pill and a five-step rail (DEPOSIT 1 · HOLD · RELEASE $0.00 · WITHDRAW 0 · RECORD 1). I understand: money goes into escrow, is held, released, withdrawn, recorded. Good. A yellow YOUR TURN card says 'Sign in to record your own deposits — the example below is the operator's paycheck as data'. Clear and honest.

10–20 s. YOUR DEPOSITS: '1. 2026.10.01_07.00..00 · Income / Wages (take-home) · PAYROLL · PENDING DIRECT DEPOSIT — $3,604.49 · 30.333'. I do not know what 30.333 is (days? A-units?). The Released block: 'Released · Deposit  $0.00 · In escrow $0.00 · Withdrawable $0.00 · Available $0.00' — everything is zero because the example starts tomorrow; that is fine, but nobody tells me so here; the chart line below does ('Pending — releases from 2026.10.01_07.00..00').

20–30 s. The chart: 'MONEY AS TIME — THIS MOT · 0 H 0 MIN ELAPSED', a Planet select (Earth · Mars) and two icons. I press the orbit icon and the title becomes '0.0000..0000 / 298.3463..2448 A-UNITS OF THE REVOLUTION' with three-line marks. I press the clock icon and it comes back. I understand the toggle; I do not understand A-units, and the surface does not try to explain them, which is the operator's intent (a reveal). I pick Mars: the marks change (1419…). The year panel below still says 'Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91 … 2026 · SOURCED' and then 'no perihelion table for this planet yet — declared, not sourced'. Both sentences on one panel; I believe neither.

30–40 s. The year panel on Earth reads plainly: Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91 · 33-day frame 9 · 7/33, the perihelion in Austin CST. I would press nothing here; it is a clock.

40–50 s. Sign-in gate; the forms are read from code. Once signed in I would see DEPOSIT: Amount $, Deposit day and time (CST) with a placeholder that is one hour behind my phone in September, MoT · length in days 30.333, Memo, then Section (A · Income), Field (Income / Wages), Timeline (Every 33 days). Two lengths for one paycheck — 30.333 and 33 — and I cannot tell which one makes the $/min. I open Section to pick C · Transportation for the car: the picker closes on me within a second (the LadderPicker is declared inside the component and the 1 Hz clock remounts it; the operator found this on his phone — addendum 26). I pick Weekly on the timeline, press Record it — the roster and the record print the field, the memo, the amount, '30.333', a hash — and nothing about Weekly. The ladder below does not move either. The timeline is written into the hash and read by nothing.

50–60 s. PERSONAL FINANCE LADDER A–M with a Unit select. Per 33 days it is my sheet exactly: A 3,200 · B 700 · C 1,800 · D 200 · E 150 · F 300 · G 50 · L 200 · Net −$200.00 red. Per year: −$2,212.12. Per second: Income $0.0011, Fitness $0.0000 — the same as the five sections that hold nothing. I would press a row to edit it; nothing is pressable. The last sentence tells me where N–T live (a Balance view I cannot see yet) — honest.

WHAT THE BRIEF PROMISES THAT I DO NOT SEE. (1) 'Warn' on a premium entered in both B and D — computed, never rendered. (2) N–T on a second surface — named, not built (FIN-06.06 draft says so). (3) A U entry naming its destination — no path; 'Yearly' in the timeline dropdown goes nowhere on the ladder. (4) The detail sheet with components. (5) 'each transaction has an MoT timeline … so when we do $/min it's based off transaction date and how long it lasts' — the timeline is stored, but no $/min anywhere is based on it; escrow still uses motDays and the ladder uses the fixed sheet. (6) FIN-03.02 says the timeline is shown on the roster and the record; it is not.

EDGE FINDINGS (the Enki lens), all reproduced in probe-edges.mjs: '3,200' (the sheet's own comma) is refused as 'above zero'; 'Infinity' and '1e400' are accepted, print $∞, and read $0 after a reload though the chain verifies; 2026.02.30 becomes 03.02 without a word; a zero MoT and a one-hour MoT read 'Done' while the hold still blocks withdrawal; the 99-year MoT is fine (linear, no overflow, DECLARED perihelion in 2125 said plainly); a yearly $150 through U becomes $0.0002853/min — correct, but $0.0000 on the glass per second; two same-instant same-amount entries collapse to one with no refusal; Mars mixes Earth and Mars A-units on one chart and SOURCED with 'not sourced' on one panel.

GRADE C+. The model deserves an A and its gate is real. The glass deserves a C: the forms' most visible new control does nothing visible, the pickers cannot be used on a phone, and the guide overclaims. The fixes are small and named above; the two high ones (print and use the timeline; hoist the picker — the latter already done in r.014) would lift this to a B on the next walk.

### Enki · adversarial — grade C+

_Adversarial pass (B) on Financial-2525 v.000_r.012, commit 201a8ec — read-only; code read with `git show 201a8ec:<path>`; pure laws re-run under node from the r.012 files; the r.012 tree rebuilt in the scratchpad (frontend/out on disk is stamped r.015, not r.012) and probed headless at 390×844 and 844×390_

**One sentence:** The ladder model itself is sound (Net holds at all eight periods, no drift, U refuses N–T, the unit dropdown measures 290 of 292 px in portrait), but around it r.012 ships four behaviour defects the gates cannot see: every picker remounts each second, a corrupted record is silently erased on the next entry, a back-dated withdrawal drives the balance negative behind a clamp, and a premium entered in both B and D is double-counted with the warning never rendered — plus Mars runs on Earth's perihelion and the new Timeline is recorded but changes no number on the glass.

**Findings**

- **Every picker remounts once a second — the dropdown closes under the person's finger (the class the operator later reported as addendum 26)** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:157 declares `const LadderPicker = (...) => (...)` INSIDE FinancialCommandUX1's render body; :95 `setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000)` re-renders the surface every second, so React sees a new component type each tick and unmounts/remounts the section, field and timeline `<select>`s (:160, :165, :170). The operator saw it on his phone (ask, addendum 26: "this drop down goes away quick!", persisted 6da8a3c) and r.014 (84f28f5) names LadderPicker as the r.012 member of the class and measured REMOUNTED on the r.013 export. At r.012 it is live and un-gated: financial-surface.test.mjs:51 regex-matches the JSX and passes.
  - Fix: Already corrected in r.014 (module-level LadderPicker). For the record: the r.012 revision entry should say its two dropdowns were unusable on a phone for the seconds a person needs to pick, and the surface gate should keep the 'no component declared inside the render body' assert.
- **A tampered or corrupted record is silently ERASED by the next entry — the whole history becomes a fresh chain from GENESIS** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: lib/financial-2525/record.ts:64-65 `return { rec: ok ? v : emptyRecord(owner), tampered: !ok }` — a failed verify hands the surface an EMPTY record; command-ux1.tsx:114 `setRecord(r.rec)`; :177 `commit` → `append(record, tx, at)` → `saveRecord(next)` overwrites the stored copy with a one-entry chain. Reproduced under node: one flipped amountCents → `verify` {ok:false, brokenAt:1}; the next append starts rev 1 from GENESIS. On reload the new chain verifies, so the 'chain broken' label (:368) disappears and the loss is invisible. A truncated localStorage write (a full phone — the class of AAR 2026-09-09) has the same effect as a tamper.
  - Fix: Keep the unverifiable bytes: load must return the raw record marked tampered and REFUSE to append (or append to a quarantined key), never replace it with empty and overwrite. Gate: 'a record that fails verify is never overwritten' in financial-accrual/record tests.
- **A back-dated withdrawal drives the balance negative; Math.max(0,…) hides it — the r.001 invariant is breakable from the form** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: accrual.ts:116 validates a withdrawal only at ITS OWN instant `balanceAt(txs.filter(x.id!==w.id), w.atMs)`, ignoring withdrawals already on the record that are dated later; :106 `availableCents: Math.max(0, withdrawableCents - withdrawnCents)` clamps the result. command-ux1.tsx:195 lets a person type any past stamp. Node reproduction on the r.012 files: deposit $1,000 over 1 day; withdraw $500 at T+12h (ok); then withdraw $250 dated T+6h (ok — validated at T+6h where $500 later is not yet counted); balance at T+12h: withdrawable 50000, withdrawn 75000, available 0, raw −25000. The comment at :90 'never negative on a valid record' is now false on a record the form itself built.
  - Fix: Validate a withdrawal against the whole record's timeline: for every instant ≥ w.atMs where a withdrawal sits, withdrawable − withdrawn must stay ≥ 0 (or simply refuse a stamp earlier than the last recorded transaction — append-only means time-ordered). Drop the clamp or print the negative in red; add the back-dated case to financial-accrual.
- **Duplicate insurance is double-counted in Net, and the warning never reaches the glass — exactly the brief's 'What not to build'** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: Brief (ask, addendum 22 rule 5): 'If a premium is entered in both, warn; do not double-count Net' and 'Silent double-count of the same premium in B and D' is forbidden. ladder.ts:105-111 adds B.housing_insurance AND D.auto_renters_home into `living` and pushes a string into `warnings` (:110 comment: 'Net counts what was entered'). Node: both at 120/33 d → net −240, warnings [DUPLICATE_INSURANCE]. command-ux1.tsx never reads `totals.warnings` (grep: no match in the file), so on the glass the double count IS silent. The gate financial-ladder.test.mjs:51 only asserts the flag exists, not what Net does.
  - Fix: Either count the premium once (the brief: B stays, D is the policy map — exclude D.auto_renters_home's home component when B.housing_insurance is present) or, until the detail sheet exists, render `totals.warnings` on the ladder in amber beside Net. Gate the Net value, not the flag.
- **One deposit carries TWO lengths that disagree by default (MoT 30.333 vs Timeline 'Every 33 days'), and the Timeline changes no number anywhere** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:145 `dMot = String(SRC.mot.payMotDays)` (30.333) and :151 `dRec = "days33"` (33 d) are both recorded on the same deposit (:190 `motDays`, `recurrence`). The clock, the chart and the roster use motDays (accrual.ts:53-76; command-ux1.tsx:215, :420); the recurrence feeds only `recurrenceDays`/`actualsByField` (ladder.ts:126-146), which the surface never imports (grep of command-ux1.tsx at 201a8ec: no actualsByField, ratePerMinute or recurrenceDays). The operator's own paycheck is 'monthly, 30.333' — the picker offers 33 or 91 ('Monthly (91)', lexicon-data.ts:3082), neither his month. A withdrawal with timeline 'Yearly' is an instant in accrual law (accrual.ts:96) — the choice is recorded, hashed, and meaningless.
  - Fix: One length per transaction: derive motDays from the timeline (once → the MoT input; weekly/33/91/yearly → 7/33/91/365) or make the timeline the deposit's MoT picker with 'Other' (which r.013 addendum 25 later asks for). Until actuals are on the glass, say on the form that the timeline is recorded for the actuals column to come.
- **Mars runs on EARTH's perihelion: its A.B..C never reaches its own whole and resets at Earth's; the chart footer uses Earth's whole while the axis uses Mars'** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: calendar.ts:97-101 anchors every 'perihelion' year on the Earth PERIHELION table regardless of planet; positionInYear only takes `yearDays`. Node on r.012 files: Mars at 2026-12-31 → 1895.3067..2475 A, startMs 2026-01-03T17:15Z (Earth's perihelion), lengthDays 364.39; at 2027-01-03T03:00Z → 0.0353 A, day 1 — reset after ~1914 of 3600 A, contradicting addendum 12 ('3600 units for mars from perihelion to perihelion'). Headless r.012 build, Mars selected, MoT reveal: axis 1419…1578 A while the year panel prints 'Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91' (Earth's count). command-ux1.tsx:447 footer calls `positionInYear(from, planet.yearAnchor)` WITHOUT `planet.yearDays` (axis at :433 passes it) — for a deposit in progress on Mars the footer would read 3565.2581..0951 beside axis marks near 1895 (node, same instant). The surface gate (financial-surface.test.mjs:29, :38) regex-checks the axis line only.
  - Fix: Give the Planet LTU row its own perihelion source (Mars: a declared perihelion instant + 686.98 d period) and have positionInYear take the planet's anchor table; pass `planet.yearDays` at :447; extend the gate to every positionInYear call on the surface.
- **A zero, negative or non-numeric MoT releases the whole deposit at once with no refusal, and the guide says 'Done' while nothing is withdrawable for three hours** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:190 `motDays: Math.max(0, Number(dMot) || 0)` — '0', '-5', 'abc' all become 0 silently; accrual.ts:59-60 `if (len <= 0) return tx.amountCents` (instant release). Node: MoT 0/−5/NaN at +1 min → state 'released', released 100000, rate 0, withdrawable 0. phaseOf (command-ux1.tsx:77) returns 'record' before checking the hold, so the guide (:210) prints fin.guide.done ('the whole deposit is released and on the record') while withdrawable is 0 until atMs+3 h. Same for any MoT under 0.125 d. The ledger line (:373) hides a 0 MoT (`e.tx.motDays ? … : ""`) while the roster prints '· 0'.
  - Fix: Refuse a MoT ≤ 0 or NaN with its own reason (fin.reason_mot); order phaseOf hold-before-record; print the MoT on the ledger line unconditionally.
- **Two transactions with the same amount at the same stamp: the second is silently dropped (id collision)** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:190 `id: \`d-${when}-${cents}\`` and :197 `w-${when}-${cents}`; record.ts:35 `if (rec.entries.some(e => e.tx.id === tx.id)) return rec` — idempotent by id, no refusal. Node: two $50 groceries typed at 2026.10.01_00.00..00 → 1 entry. With a blank stamp `when = at` (the 1 Hz tick), so two clicks in the same second also lose one. The form clears its fields (:191) as if both were recorded.
  - Fix: Make the id unique per entry (record length + stamp + amount, or a counter/nonce) or refuse with 'already on the record' and keep the form filled.
- **Odd dates roll over silently: Feb 31 → Mar 3, Sep 31 → Oct 1, year 0099 → 1999** (LOW)
  - Evidence: mot.ts:166 accepts any day 1..31 for any month; :154 `Date.UTC` rolls the overflow. Node: parseStampCST('2026.02.31_00.00..00') → 2026.03.03; '2026.09.31_07.00..00' → 2026.10.01; '0099.01.01' → 1999 (Date.UTC maps years 0–99 to 1900+). The deposit is recorded on a day the person did not type.
  - Fix: Round-trip check: refuse when cstParts(cstMs(...)) ≠ the typed parts; require year ≥ 1000.
- **The 90 r.012 lexicon keys are staged, so every non-English reader sees a half-translated ladder and forms** (LOW)
  - Evidence: Commit 201a8ec: '90 lexicon keys … STAGED in AFTER_FILL'; `git show 201a8ec:frontend/lib/i18n-app/es.ts | grep -c 'fin\.sec\.\|fin\.field\.'` = 0. Headless on the r.012 scratch build with locale es: ladderTitle 'Personal finance ladder A–M', th ['Section','por 33 días'], rowA 'A · Income', fieldA 'Income / Wages (take-home)', note in English, while 'Unidad' and 'Neto' are Spanish. The form's Section/Field/Timeline labels and all 60 field names read English in 32 languages until 50e57c0 lands. Listed, never silent — but shipped.
  - Fix: Already filled ×32 in 50e57c0. The record's r.012 entry should say the glass was mixed-language for the interval between the two ships.
- **The surface gate passes on regexes, not behaviour — every defect above sits behind a green gate** (LOW)
  - Evidence: tests/financial-surface.test.mjs is source-regex end to end: :51 matches the LadderPicker JSX (remounting or not), :54 matches the class string 'w-full … landscape:w-auto' (r.010 passed the same regex while measuring 145 of 292 px — the r.011 record), :29/:38 match the axis line but not the footer at command-ux1.tsx:447, :37 matches icon placement. financial-ladder.test.mjs:51 checks the warning flag not the Net; nothing gates a back-dated withdrawal, a zero MoT, an id collision, or a tampered record's survival.
  - Fix: Keep the regexes as a source contract but add one headless probe per behaviour claim with the clock running (the r.014 lesson) and accrual/record tests for the timeline-ordering and tamper-survival invariants.
- **Zero rows print '$0.0000' beside '$3,200.00' in the same column; per-second view rounds a live line to '$0.0000'** (LOW)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:141 `usdUnit = cents => |cents| >= 100 ? usd(round) : usd4` — a 0 total prints with four decimals (r013-ladder.png shows H/I/J/K/M as '$0.0000' under A '$3,200.00'; r.012 renders the same 13 rows through the same function). Per-second (headless r.012): G.mental_physical=$0.0000 for a real 50/33 d line (0.0000175 $/s). Landscape header 'PER 33 DAYS' wraps to two lines at 390 px.
  - Fix: Print an exact zero as '$0.00' (or '—'); below 0.00005 $/unit print more digits or the ¢/min form; consider a narrower unit header.
- **Q · Net worth and R · Credit share one icon (Gauge)** (LOW)
  - Evidence: category-icon.tsx:37 `Q: Gauge, R: Gauge` — two sections, one stroke; the file's own law (:33-34) is 'every ladder SECTION A–T carries its stroke icon'. Not on the r.012 glass (N–T are off it), but it is the map the Balance view will read. The operator raised it as addendum 27 (d9054ef).
  - Fix: Give Q its own stroke (r.015 is queued for this).
- **No r.012 capture exists in the shots folder the fleet was pointed at** (LOW)
  - Evidence: ls scratchpad/shots/ shows r005–r011 and r013 captures; no r012-*.png although scratchpad/r012-shots.mjs writes r012-ladder-portrait/landscape/ledger there and the r.012 commit message claims 'captures'. The bookkeeping commit 5c9d3cf touched only the four record files. The fleet had to rebuild r.012 to see it.
  - Fix: Persist the r.012 captures (or say on the record that r.012 shipped uncaptured and r.013's ladder capture stands for it).

**Keep**

- The ladder math holds under attack: Net at every one of the eight periods equals the 33-day Net × one constant; a round trip 33 d → second → year on odd cents matches periodFactor to 1e-16; a 1e9/s → year product is exact; a balance in P never nets; negative and unknown fields are dropped; U refuses N–T, a 'U.' id and a zero period (ladder.ts:88-121; financial-ladder.test.mjs:27-58 test behaviour, not regexes).
- The unit dropdown really is full width in portrait: measured 290 px of a 292 px panel interior at 390×844 on the r.012 scratch build, 224 px at its own width in landscape; no horizontal scroll (scrollW 390/844); no page errors in either orientation.
- Alcohol maps to exactly one target (F by default, L on request, never both) and the amortize rule lands in the named A–M field with the right per-period amount (1,200/yr → 108.49 per 33 d).
- Old entries survive: FinTx.field and .recurrence are optional (accrual.ts:36-37), stableJson only hashes present keys, so r.006–r.011 entries keep their hashes and still print their category with its icon (command-ux1.tsx:179-183).
- A 99-year MoT and a DECLARED year both behave: 99 y prints 356390.2151..2957 A unclamped at 1.92 ¢/min; a declared leap year (2128) reads day 366, pastFull true, 3607.1044..2400 — said, never hidden. CST standard is fixed (07:00 typed on 2026-10-01 → 13:00Z, 08:00 Austin wall clock) exactly as the operator ruled, and the label says CST.
- The rebuilt r.012 export renders the thirteen section rows A–M, eight sheet fields, Net −$200.00 red, the N–T note, and the perihelion in CST — the model-first ship the brief asked for.

**Report (verbatim)**

ENKI-B · adversarial pass on Financial-2525 v.000_r.012 (201a8ec). Read from the commit, never the working tree; the pure laws were extracted from the commit and run under node; the r.012 tree was rebuilt in the scratchpad because frontend/out on disk is stamped v.000_r.015 (the tree moved on twice while I read), and probed headless at 390×844 and 844×390 in English and Spanish. Everything I claim carries a file:line or a measurement.

WHAT HELD. The lock's model is good. netLadder gives Net = I − L − Ds − Tx − Tr at all eight periods as one constant multiple of the 33-day Net; toPeriod is a single multiply-divide with no drift to 1e-16 on odd cents and exact on 1e9/s → year; P never nets against I; U refuses N–T and a 'U.' destination; Alcohol counts in one section only. The portrait unit dropdown measures 290 of 292 px (landscape 224 px at its own width). Thirteen rows, Net red, no errors, no horizontal scroll. Old r.006–r.011 entries keep their hashes and print their category. CST standard is fixed and labelled.

WHAT BROKE. Four behaviour defects, none visible to the gates.
1. Every picker remounts every second (command-ux1.tsx:157 declares LadderPicker inside the render body; :95 ticks at 1 Hz). The operator reported the symptom on his phone (addendum 26, 6da8a3c) and r.014 measured REMOUNTED on the r.013 export; at r.012 it is live.
2. A record that fails verify is replaced by an empty one (record.ts:64-65) and the next entry overwrites the store (command-ux1.tsx:114, :177) — a tamper or a truncated write erases the whole history, and after reload the fresh chain verifies so the 'chain broken' label vanishes.
3. A withdrawal is validated only at its own instant (accrual.ts:116); typing a past stamp (command-ux1.tsx:195) after a later withdrawal passes and leaves withdrawn > withdrawable; Math.max(0, …) at :106 hides the −$250 I produced.
4. The brief says 'warn; do not double-count Net'; ladder.ts:105-111 counts both premiums (node: −240) and the surface never renders totals.warnings — the double count is silent on the glass. The gate checks the flag, not the Net.

WHAT IS ODD. A deposit carries two lengths — MoT 30.333 and Timeline 'Every 33 days' by default (command-ux1.tsx:145, :151) — and the Timeline feeds actualsByField, which the glass never imports; the operator's own 'monthly 30.333' is not among the timeline's choices. Mars is anchored on Earth's perihelion table (calendar.ts:97-101): headless, Mars reads 1416 A on Earth day 271 and, by node, resets to 0.0353 A at Earth's 2027 perihelion after ~1914 of its 3600; the chart footer at :447 omits planet.yearDays (3565 A beside axis marks of 1895 at the same instant). A zero/negative/'abc' MoT releases everything at once with no refusal (:190; accrual.ts:59) and the guide says 'Done' while withdrawable is 0 for three hours (phaseOf :77 checks record before hold). Two entries with the same amount at the same second collapse into one with no word (:190, :197; record.ts:35). Feb 31 becomes Mar 3 and year 0099 becomes 1999 (mot.ts:154-166). Zero rows print '$0.0000' under '$3,200.00'; per-second rounds Fitness to '$0.0000'. Q and R share the Gauge stroke (category-icon.tsx:37). In Spanish the r.012 glass reads 'Section' and 'Income / Wages (take-home)' beside 'Unidad' and 'Neto' — the 90 staged keys, listed, not silent, but shipped mixed. No r012-*.png capture exists in the shots folder the fleet was pointed at.

GRADE C+. The brief was implemented as a model and the numbers are right; the edges of the surface and the record are where a person with odd amounts, odd dates, a zero MoT or a second planet gets hurt, and every one of those edges sits behind a regex gate that reads the source, not the behaviour. The r.013/r.014 commits already close the picker class and fill the keys; the record-erasure, the back-dated withdrawal, the double-counted premium, the two-lengths deposit and the Mars anchor are still open and are small enough to fold together as one 'the record and the ladder tell the whole truth' revision with behaviour gates.

### Enki · synthesis — grade C+

**One sentence:** The A–U model underneath r.012 is exact and gated, but the glass and the record fail at their edges: every picker remounts once a second, a record that fails verify is silently erased by the next entry, a back-dated withdrawal drives the balance negative behind a clamp, and the new timeline is hashed but changes no number a stranger can see.

**Top three**

- 1. EVERY PICKER REMOUNTS ON THE 1 Hz TICK — the dropdown closes under the person's finger. frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:157 declares `const LadderPicker = (…) => (…)` INSIDE FinancialCommandUX1's render body; :95 `setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000)` re-renders the surface every second, so React sees a new component type each tick and unmounts/remounts the section, field and timeline <select>s (:160, :165, :170). Operator-confirmed (ask addendum 26, 'this drop down goes away quick!'); the surface gate (frontend/tests/financial-surface.test.mjs:51) regex-matches the JSX and passes either way. FIX: hoist LadderPicker to module level with `t` as a prop — DONE in r.014 (84f28f5, measured REMOUNTED on r.013 → SAME on r.014). Still owed: a clock-running probe that opens a select, waits three ticks and asserts the same DOM node, kept in test:ci so the class cannot return.
- 2. A RECORD THAT FAILS VERIFY IS SILENTLY ERASED BY THE NEXT ENTRY. frontend/lib/financial-2525/record.ts:65 `return { rec: ok ? v : emptyRecord(owner), tampered: !ok }` hands the surface an EMPTY record on a broken chain; frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:114 `setRecord(r.rec)` adopts it and :177 `commit` → `append(record, tx, at)` → `saveRecord(next)` overwrites the stored copy with a one-entry chain from GENESIS. Probe on the r.012 libraries: one flipped amountCents → verify {ok:false, brokenAt:1}; the next append → entries=1, rev 1, prev 0000000000000000, verify ok — after a reload the 'chain broken' label (:368) is gone and the loss is invisible. The realistic members are a hand-edited store and any future change to chainHash/stableJson (r.012 had to promise 'r.006–r.011 hashes untouched' precisely because of this). Unchanged at HEAD (record.ts:65). FIX: loadRecord returns the raw bytes marked tampered; commit REFUSES to append while tampered (or appends under a quarantine key); gate 'a record that fails verify is never overwritten' in the record tests.
- 3. A BACK-DATED WITHDRAWAL BREAKS 'NEVER NEGATIVE' AND Math.max(0, …) HIDES IT. frontend/lib/financial-2525/accrual.ts:116 validates a withdrawal only at ITS OWN instant `balanceAt(txs.filter(x.id !== w.id), w.atMs)`, so withdrawals already on the record but dated later are not counted; :106 `availableCents: Math.max(0, withdrawableCents − withdrawnCents)` clamps the result; command-ux1.tsx:195 accepts any past stamp. Probe: deposit $1,000 over 1 day; withdraw $500 at T+12 h (ok); withdraw $250 dated T+6 h (ok); balance at T+12 h = withdrawable 50000 · withdrawn 75000 · available 0 · raw −25000. The comment at accrual.ts:89 'never negative on a valid record' is false on a record the form itself built. Unchanged at HEAD (accrual.ts:116). FIX: validate against the whole timeline (for every instant ≥ w.atMs where a withdrawal sits, withdrawable − withdrawn ≥ 0), or simply refuse a stamp earlier than the last recorded transaction (append-only means time-ordered); drop the clamp or print the negative in red; add the back-dated case to financial-accrual.test.mjs.

**Report (verbatim)**

ENKI · SYNTHESIS — Financial-2525 v.000_r.012 (commit 201a8ec), reconciled from the walk (C+) and the adversarial pass (C+).

METHOD. Every file under review was read with `git show 201a8ec:<path>`, never from the working tree (the tree is at r.018 and frontend/out is stamped v.000_r.018). I re-ran the behaviours I doubted under node against the walk reviewer's extracted r.012 libraries after confirming they differ from the commit only in import suffixes (`./mot` → `./mot.ts`); the script is at /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-eXeL-AI-Polling/d1ee5405-1233-5ea7-aa59-4ce83334aed8/scratchpad/fleet-fin/enki-syn/verify.mjs. I checked HEAD for what has closed since, so nothing shipped is re-reported as owed. Nothing in the repository was touched.

WHAT HELD (both reviewers agree, and I confirm). The lock is implemented as a model and gated by behaviour, not regex: frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts carries the twenty sections, the sixty fields with the brief's labels, the fixed period factors, Net = I − L − Ds − Tx − Tr (ladder.ts:108-111), U as an amortize rule that refuses N–T (ladder.ts:117-121), Alcohol to one target (ladder.ts:83, :105); frontend/tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs:27-58 tests the numbers. Net at every one of the eight units is the 33-day Net times one constant with no drift (probe: −$200.00 / 33 d, −$0.0042 / min, −$2,212.12 / yr). The portrait unit dropdown measures 290 of 292 px; thirteen rows, Net red, no horizontal scroll, no page errors. The escrow law is safe at the far edges: a 99-year MoT accrues linearly without overflow, a DECLARED year (2125) is said plainly, the 3-hour hold blocks a withdrawal at +1 s with a named reason, old r.006–r.011 entries keep their hashes because field and recurrence are optional (accrual.ts:36-37). The Mars axis really reads Mars A-units (1419.0722..1931 at the first mark, walk-mars-mot.png). The honest sentences are there: 'declared, not sourced', 'On this phone only', 'EXAMPLE · the operator's paycheck as data'.

WHAT BROKE — reconciled into classes, each verified at the cited line.

Class 1 · THE PICKER (both HIGH, operator-confirmed). command-ux1.tsx:157 declares LadderPicker inside the render body; :95 ticks the clock at 1 Hz; React remounts the three selects every second and a phone dismisses its picker sheet with them. The gate at financial-surface.test.mjs:51 matches the JSX and cannot see it. r.014 (84f28f5) hoisted the component and measured REMOUNTED → SAME on the glass. Closed; the clock-running probe should stay in test:ci.

Class 2 · THE RECORD DOES NOT SURVIVE ITS OWN EDGES (adversarial HIGH ×2; the walk did not test the record). (a) record.ts:65 returns emptyRecord when verify fails; command-ux1.tsx:114 adopts it and :177 saves the next append over the store — probe: a tampered two-entry chain becomes a one-entry chain from GENESIS that verifies, so the 'chain broken' label (:368) disappears on reload. (b) accrual.ts:116 validates a withdrawal at its own instant only and :106 clamps; probe: withdrawable 50000, withdrawn 75000, available 0, raw −25000 after a back-dated $250. (c) Same stamp + same amount → the same id (command-ux1.tsx:190, :197), record.ts:35 returns the same record, no refusal, the form clears (:191). (d) 'Infinity' and '1e400' pass `cents > 0` (:187), become null in the store (record.ts:70), verify still passes, and the balance reads them as 0 — one entry, two amounts across a reload; '0x10' is $16; the sheet's own '3,200' is refused as 'above zero'. (e) mot.ts:166 accepts any day ≤ 31 and Date.UTC rolls 2026.02.30 to 03.02, 2026.09.31 to 10.01 and year 0099 to 1999. All five are un-gated and (a), (b), (e) are unchanged at HEAD. The invariant to write before the code: a person's record never loses an entry, never accepts an entry it cannot keep, and never shows a number it cannot defend.

Class 3 · THE GUIDE OVERCLAIMS AT A SHORT MoT (both MEDIUM). phaseOf at command-ux1.tsx:77 returns 'record' when fraction ≥ 1 before checking the hold at :78; a zero or one-hour MoT at +1 h reads 'Done — the whole deposit is released and on the record' (:210) while withdrawable is $0 (accrual.ts:77) and the strip's Withdraw is disabled (:394). `Math.max(0, Number(dMot) || 0)` at :190 turns '-5' and 'abc' into 0 silently, and accrual.ts:59 releases a zero MoT at once. Unchanged at HEAD (:121-122).

Class 4 · THE TIMELINE IS WRITTEN AND NOT READ (walk HIGH, adversarial MEDIUM → high-medium). The timeline select (:169-173) is stored on the entry (:190, :197) and nowhere else: the roster (:215) and the record (:373) print the field, the memo, the amount and a bare motDays; actualsByField (ladder.ts:134) is imported by nothing, at r.012 and still at r.013. FIN-03.02 (docs/financial-2525/financial-2525.v.000.json, status 'implemented') says 'a transaction's $/min runs from its date for its timeline's length so the ladder's actuals can be tracked' — no number on the glass does that. The second half of this class — one deposit carrying two lengths, MoT 30.333 at :145 and timeline 33 d at :151 — was closed in r.013 (motDays = lengthDays(rec, …), 'Other'); the write-only half remains.

Class 5 · MARS RUNS ON EARTH'S PERIHELION (both MEDIUM). calendar.ts:98-101 anchors every planet's perihelion year on Earth's PERIHELION table; the Mars row's yearAnchor is 'perihelion' (lib/planet-ltu.ts:55). Probe: Mars at 2026-12-31 reads 1893.0835..0355 A, day 362, start 2026-01-03T17:15Z, status SOURCED, and resets to 0.0353 A at Earth's 2027-01-03 perihelion — ~1893 of its 3600, against addendum 12. The year panel prints year.status = SOURCED (:290) beside 'declared, not sourced' (:292) on the same panel (walk-mars-year.png). The chart footer at :447 calls positionInYear without planet.yearDays while the axis at :433 passes it — probe: 2669.0833..0514 beside 1419.0722..1931 for the same instant; the gate (financial-surface.test.mjs:29, :38) checks the axis line only.

Class 6 · THE PRINT (both LOW–MEDIUM). usdUnit (:141) prints an empty section as '$0.0000' under '$3,200.00' (r013-ladder.png rows H/I/J/K/M) and rounds the real $50 Fitness line to '$0.0000' per second, identical to the empty sections (walk-ladder-per-second.png); the MoT number is bare ('· 30.333' at :215, :373, :447); the strip's two numbers (:392) have no names.

Class 7 · DUPLICATE INSURANCE (walk LOW, adversarial MEDIUM → MEDIUM). ladder.ts:105-111 counts both premiums into living (probe: −240) and only pushes a string; command-ux1.tsx never renders totals.warnings; the brief's rule 5 says 'warn; do not double-count Net' and forbids 'Silent double-count' outright. Latent on r.012's fixed sheet (budget.ts:73 maps Insurance to D only), reachable since r.016's edit mode lets a person add both lines. The gate (financial-ladder.test.mjs:51) asserts the flag, not the Net.

CLAIMS I DROPPED OR CORRECTED. (1) Adversarial: 'the r.012 commit message claims captures' — the commit message of 201a8ec contains no such word; the plan did. The fact stands (no r012-*.png in the shots folder; r011 and r013 are there); the attribution does not. (2) Adversarial: 'a truncated localStorage write has the same effect as a tamper' — setItem is atomic and a failed write throws into the catch at record.ts:70, which the surface shows as saveFailed; dropped that member, kept the hand-edited store and the future hash-law change. (3) Adversarial: 'two clicks in the same second also lose one' — the form clears the amount after the first commit (:191, :200), so the second click is refused as 'above zero', not collapsed; dropped. (4) Walk: the one-hour CST placeholder skew rated MEDIUM — mot.ts:39 says 'CST standard, never CDT (operator 2026-09-30)'; it is the operator's rule, not a defect, and the walk's evidence (build-stamp 'Time: 18:25 CST' versus the year line) compares the commit time with now, which proves nothing; downgraded to a wording note. (5) Walk: its captures are of the r.014 export (walk-390-viewport.png reads 'revision 0.014', SHA 6da8a3c), as the reviewer said; I relied on them only for the parts the diff shows unchanged. (6) The two reviewers' Mars figures differ slightly (1895 vs 1893 A on 2026-12-31) because they chose different hours; the direction and the reset instant agree with my probe exactly.

GRADE C+ (both reviewers; I concur). The model deserves an A and its gate is real. The glass deserves a C: the most visible new control did nothing visible, the pickers were unusable on a phone for the seconds a person needs, the guide said Done while the hold ran, and the record — the thing the whole surface exists to keep — could be erased or driven negative from its own form without a word. r.013–r.016 have closed the picker, the two lengths, the icon and the lexicon; the record's survival, the back-dated withdrawal, the hold-first phase, the Mars anchor, the double-counted premium and the write-only timeline are open at HEAD and small enough to ship together as one 'the record and the ladder tell the whole truth' revision — with behaviour gates, since every defect above sat behind a green regex.

## Enlil

### Enlil · walk — grade C+

_Constructive walk — a stranger with the operator's own sheet opening /financial-2525 on a phone for 60 seconds_

**One sentence:** The model under the glass is the brief, exactly, and it is gated; the glass at r.012 shows that model faithfully as a read-only table of the operator's sheet, but the one thing a signed-in person does — place a transaction — is built on a picker that unmounts every second and a "timeline" that the code records and never uses, while the decision text (FD-26) says it is the transaction's MoT.

**Findings**

- **The three ladder pickers are declared inside the render body, so the 1 Hz clock remounts them every second — a phone's picker sheet closes before the person can choose** (high)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:157 — `const LadderPicker = ({ … }) => (` is defined inside `FinancialCommandUX1`, whose `now` state ticks via `setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000)` at :95. A component created inside a render is a new type on every render, so React unmounts and remounts the `<select data-fin-section|field|timeline>` subtree each tick. The same class shipped in r.006's CategorySelect; r.012 rebuilt it with the same flaw. The operator reported it on his phone (addendum 26, "this drop down goes away quick!") and r.014 (84f28f5) moved the picker to module level — proof the defect was live at r.012. The unit dropdown (:341) and planet select (:279, passed as a node into module-level MotChart) do not share it.
  - Fix: Hoist `LadderPicker` to module scope (as r.014 did) and add a source gate in tests/financial-surface.test.mjs that fails on any `const X = (…) => (` returning JSX inside the component body — the class, not the instance.
- **A deposit carries TWO lengths — the typed MoT (30.333 days) that drives the escrow and a Timeline dropdown ("Every 33 days") that drives nothing — while FD-26 says the timeline IS the transaction's MoT** (high)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:145 `dMot` defaults to `SRC.mot.payMotDays` (30.333) and :304 renders it as "MoT · length in days"; :151 `dRec` defaults to `"days33"` (33 days) and :169-172 renders it as "Timeline". At :190 the entry is committed with `motDays: Number(dMot)` (used by accrual.ts releasedAt/depositView) and `recurrence: dRec` (stored only). On the glass nothing reads `recurrence`: `recurrenceDays`, `ratePerMinute`, `actualsByField` are imported by no surface file (grep count 0 in command-ux1.tsx). The record's FD-26 (financial-2525.v.000.json) states "the timeline is the transaction's MoT: its $/min runs from its date for that length" — the code does not do this. A stranger sees a 30.333-day MoT and a 33-day timeline on the same form and cannot tell which one his money follows.
  - Fix: One length per transaction: derive `motDays` from the chosen timeline (r.013 later did this with `lengthDays(rec, …)`), or drop the timeline until it drives the accrual. Until then, correct FD-26 on the record with an appended decision — the record must not claim what the code does not do.
- **A refused DEPOSIT prints its reason under the WITHDRAWAL form — the person taps Record it and sees nothing happen where they are** (medium)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:187-188 `recordDeposit` calls `setRefusal(t("fin.reason_amount"))` / `setRefusal(t("fin.reason_stamp"))`; the only render of `refusal` is :318 inside the withdrawal panel (`id="fin-withdraw-form"`, :310), below the deposit panel on a phone. `saveFailed` (:319) has the same placement and is set by `commit` (:177) for either form.
  - Fix: Render the refusal and save-failed lines inside whichever form raised them (or once, directly under the button that was pressed); keep one `refusal` state but tag it with the form.
- **One column, two number formats: an empty section prints "$0.0000" beside "$700.00"; five of thirteen section rows are zero rows with nothing beneath** (medium)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:141 `usdUnit = (cents) => Math.abs(cents) >= 100 ? usd(Math.round(cents)) : usd4(cents)` — zero and sub-dollar amounts get four decimals, dollar amounts two. Probe on the export at 390 px (fleet-fin/enlil-a/findings.json): "H · Family $0.0000 · I · Debt service $0.0000 · J · Taxes $0.0000 · K · Work $0.0000 · M · Transfers $0.0000" beside "B · Housing $700.00" (per 33 days); per hour the same column reads "$4.04" and "$0.8838". The table block is unchanged between 201a8ec and HEAD (no diff lines touch data-fin-budget/usdDollars), so the r.014 export shows the r.012 table. CLAUDE.md rule 8 (unit/format consistency) applies.
  - Fix: One precision per chosen unit for the whole column (e.g. 2 decimals at ≥ per-day, 4 below), and print an empty section as "—" or fold empty sections until they have a field (the brief wants 13 rows; a muted "—" keeps the row and drops the false precision).
- **The note under the ladder sends the person to a "Balance view" that does not exist at r.012** (medium)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:363 renders `t("fin.stock_note")` = "Cash, investments, debts, net worth, credit, protection and goals live on the Balance view — never per minute". No route, tab, button or panel named Balance exists in the component or the export (probe: no such element; FIN-06.06 is `draft` in financial-2525.v.000.json for r.013). A stranger reads a destination and looks for it.
  - Fix: Until FIN-06.06 ships, word the note as a promise ("… are kept off this ladder; a Balance view follows"), or ship a stub Balance panel listing N–T with no figures.
- **The ladder is the operator's sheet, hard-coded, unlabelled, not editable, and never meets the person's own transactions — the "$/min against budget" path is dead on the glass** (medium)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:138 `netLadder(SHEET_LINES, period)` over budget.ts:77 `SHEET_LINES` (a constant built from `SHEET_BUDGET`, the operator's figures). The panel (:334-364) carries no "EXAMPLE" badge (the paycheck has one at :253/:328; the ladder does not), and for a signed-in person it still shows the operator's Income 3,200 / Car 1,800. `actualsByField` (ladder.ts:134) — the operator's stated goal ("goal is to tract $/min against budget") — is exported and tested (financial-ladder.test.mjs) but called by no surface file. The record lists the actuals column under FIN-06.06 draft, so this is owed, not hidden; but on the walk the ladder reads as the stranger's own budget.
  - Fix: Badge the ladder as the operator's sheet until per-login lines exist; then feed `actualsByField(replay(record) → TimedTx[])` into a second column beside the budget figure (the r.013 promise), keyed by `FinTx.field` and `recurrenceDays(recurrence)`.
- **On the A.B..C reveal the chart's summary line uses Earth's revolution even when Mars is selected — the axis under it uses Mars's** (low)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:447 `positionInYear(from, planet.yearAnchor).abc` → `positionInYear(to, planet.yearAnchor).abc` omit the third argument `planet.yearDays`, while :433 (the axis marks) and :125 (the year panel) pass it. Probe on Mars reveal: axis marks 1419 … 1578 (Mars A-units) while the line, once the deposit is running, would print 2669 → 2968 (Earth A-units; the Earth axis reads 2669 … 2968). Not visible today because the example deposit is pending (branch `now < from` wins), which is why the surface gate's regex at r.008 never caught it — the regex checks the axis expression only.
  - Fix: Pass `planet.yearDays` at :447 (both calls) and extend the financial-surface gate to every `positionInYear(` call in the component.
- **Touch targets under 44 px on the phone: planet select 26 px, MoT ⇄ Clock buttons 32 px, unit select 36 px** (low)
  - Evidence: Probe at 390×844 (findings.json hits): unit 36, planet 26, abc 32 (sign-in 44). Sources: command-ux1.tsx:279 `px-2 py-1 text-xs` on `<select data-fin-planet>`; :442-443 `h-8 w-9` on the toggle buttons; :341 `py-2 text-sm` on `<select data-fin-budget-unit>`. CLAUDE.md release rule 9 (mobile-first: touch targets).
  - Fix: `min-h-[44px]` on every picker and the two toggle buttons in portrait (landscape may keep the compact height it already declares).
- **A length prints as a bare number — "$3,604.49 · 30.333" — on the roster, the record and the chart line** (low)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:215 roster label `${usd(d.amountCents)} · ${d.motDays}`; :370 and :373 the record `… · ${e.tx.motDays}`; :447 the chart line `{tx.motDays} ·`. Probe text: "$3,604.49 · 30.333". A stranger does not know 30.333 of what; the form's label says "length in days" but the readouts drop the unit.
  - Fix: Print the unit once ("30.333 d") or the sheet's own duration form `fmtDurationDays` (mot.ts:158) which already exists: 0000.00.30_08.00..00.
- **`height="auto"` on the chart SVG is not a valid length — a console error on every render** (low)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:448 `<svg viewBox=… width="100%" height="auto" …>`; probe console: `Error: <svg> attribute height: Expected length, "auto".` (both orientations). Rendering is unaffected (viewBox + width give the height), but the page logs an error per mount.
  - Fix: Drop the attribute and use the class `h-auto` (Tailwind) — the `className="mt-2 block"` is already there.
- **Two sections share one icon — Q · Net worth and R · Credit are both Gauge — under a commit that says "twenty strokes"** (low)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/category-icon.tsx:35 `Q: Gauge, R: Gauge`. Nineteen distinct icons for twenty sections; not visible on the r.012 ladder (A–M only) but the map is the law FD-22 extends to the sections, and the operator's later addendum 27 asks that all A–U carry their own.
  - Fix: Give Q its own stroke (Scale is used by P; a `Landmark`-free choice such as `PiggyBank`/`BadgeDollarSign`) and gate distinctness in financial-surface (`new Set(Object.values(SECTION_ICON)).size === 20`).
- **On the walk day the example deposit is PENDING, so the surface shows $0.00 everywhere and no NOW line — the "$/min real-time" promise is invisible until 2026-10-01 07:00 CST** (low)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:83-86 EXAMPLE atMs = 2026.10.01_07.00..00 (from SRC.example.depositStamp); probe: clock "$0.00", balance "In escrow: $0.00 · Withdrawable: $0.00", chart line "Pending — releases from 2026.10.01_07.00..00", the `bal.ratePerMinCents > 0` ladder at :267 hidden, `inside(now)` false so no NOW stroke (:456). The example is the operator's real posting date, which is right for the record, but the demo a stranger sees before Oct 1 is a static X of two lines.
  - Fix: Keep the real example on the record; for the unsigned demo, add a second illustrative deposit dated in the past (declared as example data) or let the chart say in one line what will start ticking and when.
- **No r.012 capture exists: the shot script targets r012-ladder-portrait / -landscape / -ledger, none was written; the r.012 gate chain ran tsc → test:ci → build only** (low)
  - Evidence: scratchpad/r012-shots.mjs writes `r012-ladder-portrait.png`, `r012-ladder-landscape.png`, `r012-ledger.png`; the shots directory holds r005–r011 and r013 captures but no r012-*; scratchpad/r012-chain.sh and r012f-chain.log show tsc/test:ci/build only. The ladder shipped without a picture in the ship chain, unlike every neighbouring revision.
  - Fix: Make the two-orientation capture a step of the chain script (fail the chain when the file is missing), as the drone deck-QA gate does.
- **The browser tab reads "eXeL AI Polling", not Financial-2525** (low)
  - Evidence: Probe document.title = "eXeL AI Polling" on /financial-2525/; frontend/app/financial-2525/page.tsx at 201a8ec exports no metadata and no layout.tsx sits beside it (git ls-tree). Drone-2525 had the same finding and fixed it (task "Drone tab title → exactly eXeL Drone-2525").
  - Fix: A `layout.tsx` (or `generateMetadata`) for /financial-2525 and /main/Financial-2525 with title "eXeL Financial-2525"; gate it in financial-surface.

**Keep**

- Net matches the equation at every time base on the glass, by a constant: probe Net −$0.0001/s · −$0.0042/min · −$0.2525/h · −$6.06/day · −$42.42/week · −$200.00/33 d · −$551.52/91 · −$2,212.12/year, red (rgb 239,68,68); the sheet's 144 $/D = 0.1 $/min ladder holds (test §3).
- Thirteen sections A–M render with the section's icon (probe: data-fin-sec-icon ABCDEFGHIJKLM), Net last; U appears nowhere as a chip; N–T appear nowhere per minute; labels are the brief's ("Income / Wages (take-home)", "Auto / Renters / Home", "Mental Health / Physical Fitness").
