- The build and gates are green at 201a8ec: tsc 0 errors, test:ci exit 0, build exit 0 (r012f-chain.log); the export emits both routes and the surface gate (63 asserts) reads the component's source for the ladder wiring.

**Report (verbatim)**

ENLIL — the code as built. I read everything from commit 201a8ec with git show, never the working tree. One caveat first: frontend/out is a v.000_r.014 export, not r.012 (the stamp in the built page says so). I still ran the headless walk on it at 390×844 and 844×390 because `git diff 201a8ec HEAD` shows the ladder table block, the balance block, the chart and the year panel unchanged; the forms changed in r.013/r.014, so for the forms I speak from the r.012 source only. Everything I wrote went under scratchpad/fleet-fin/enlil-a/ (probe.mjs, findings.json, five captures).

THE WALK, 60 SECONDS, PHONE, PORTRAIT.
0–10 s. The Session shell: globe, the three glyphs, FINANCIAL · 2525, "the MoT Financial System · Version 000 · revision 0.012". A card "Money as time — this MoT" with a DEPOSIT pill and a five-step rail (Deposit 1 · Hold · Release $0.00 · Withdraw 0 · Record 1). I understand there is a five-step life of a deposit. "MoT" I do not understand yet; the subtitle does not say Measure of Time.
10–20 s. An amber YOUR TURN card: "Sign in to record your own deposits — the example below is the operator's paycheck as data", a Sign in ↓ button, and a cyan EXAMPLE line. Clear. The roster shows one deposit, "2026.10.01_07.00..00 · Income / Wages (take-home) · PAYROLL · PENDING DIRECT DEPOSIT · $3,604.49 · 30.333". I do not know what 30.333 is (defect 9). The date format is the sheet's, fine once seen twice.
20–30 s. The Released block: big $0.00; "In escrow $0.00 · Withdrawable $0.00 · Withdrawn $0.00 · Available $0.00". The chart: a Planet select (Earth), two icon buttons, "Pending — releases from 2026.10.01_07.00..00", an X of a green line rising and an orange line falling, axis 0 h 0 min … 30 d 7 h. I press the orbit icon: the header becomes "0.0000..0000 / 298.3463..2448 A-UNITS OF THE REVOLUTION" and the axis becomes five three-line numbers. I press the clock icon and it comes back. The toggle works and is one state for the card (the year panel switches with it: "271/365" ⇄ "2663.2789..1706"). But today, before Oct 1 07:00, nothing ticks and there is no NOW line — the thing this surface exists to show is not on the screen (defect 12).
30–40 s. Year position: "Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91 · 33-day frame 9 · 7/33", then "2026.09.30_17.42..42 CST · 2026 · SOURCED · 271/365" and "Perihelion (Austin CST standard) · 2026.01.03_11.15..00 CST". I understand day-of-year, quarter and frame. "SOURCED" is the operator's word, not mine.
40–55 s. PERSONAL FINANCE LADDER A–M. Unit dropdown, full width, "per 33 days". Thirteen rows with icons: A · Income $3,200.00 with "Income / Wages (take-home)" beneath, B · Housing $700.00 / Rent / Mortgage, C · Transportation $1,800.00 / Auto Payment, D · Insurance $200.00 / Auto / Renters / Home, E · Utilities $150.00 / Electric / Gas, F · Food $300.00 / Groceries, G · Health $50.00 / Mental Health / Physical Fitness, H · Family $0.0000, I · Debt service $0.0000, J · Taxes $0.0000, K · Work $0.0000, L · Lifestyle $200.00 / Fun / Hobbies / Clothing, M · Transfers $0.0000, Net −$200.00 in red. With the operator's sheet in my hand, every line is found: Home under B, Car under C, Insurance under D, Electric under E, Food under F, Fitness under G (I looked under Food first), Fun under L. I switch to per minute: Income $0.0673, Net −$0.0042. Per year: −$2,212.12. The arithmetic is right at every base (I checked all eight). What I cannot do: change a number. It is the operator's sheet, and nothing says so on this panel (the paycheck above has an EXAMPLE badge; the ladder does not — defect 6). And "$0.0000" beside "$700.00" reads as a mistake (defect 4). The line under the table says the rest "live on the Balance view" — I look for it; there is none (defect 5).
55–60 s. THE RECORD · CHAIN VERIFIED, one row, "On this phone only — a cloud copy follows once sign-in carries it". The Trinity wheel folded. The R-CORE badge at the bottom, clear of the sticky strip (I measured: badge bottom 2677 px, page 2774 px, strip 95 px — no occlusion).

What I would press: Sign in (I cannot, in this sandbox), the unit dropdown (works), the orbit/clock toggle (works), the planet select (works; Mars shows "declared, not sourced"), and the DEPOSIT chip on the strip (it is a label, not a button, for an unsigned visitor).

THE FORMS, FROM THE CODE (sign-in gated, command-ux1.tsx:297-331). Signed in, the deposit form has Amount · Deposit day and time (CST) · MoT · length in days (30.333) · Memo, then Section (A · Income, with its icon) · Field (Income / Wages (take-home)) · Timeline (Every 33 days) and Record it. The withdrawal form has Amount · day and time · Section (B · Housing) · Field (Rent / Mortgage) · Timeline (One time) and Withdraw. Two things are wrong at r.012 and both are in the code, not in taste. First, `LadderPicker` is declared inside the component body (:157) and the surface re-renders every second (:95); React remounts the three selects each tick, so a phone's picker closes before the person picks. This is the r.006 CategorySelect class rebuilt with the same flaw; the operator hit it (addendum 26) and r.014 hoisted the picker — which proves it was live here. Second, the deposit form asks for the length twice: a typed MoT of 30.333 days and a Timeline of "Every 33 days". The commit (:190) writes `motDays` from the typed MoT — that is what accrual.ts releases against — and stores `recurrence` beside it, and nothing on the glass reads the recurrence afterwards (`recurrenceDays`, `ratePerMinute`, `actualsByField` are imported nowhere in the component). FD-26 on the record says the timeline IS the MoT and that the $/min runs from the date for that length. The code does not do that. A person setting 30.333 and "Every 33 days" on one form is told two different things about the same money. The refusal line for a bad amount or stamp on the deposit form is rendered only inside the withdrawal panel (:318), below the fold on a phone — press Record it with an empty amount and nothing appears where you are.

DEAD PATHS. ladder.ts exports amortize, actualsByField, ratePerMinute, periodFactor, alcoholSection, recurrenceDays — tested (59 asserts), reachable from no surface. The record says so (FIN-06.06 draft, "the U entry, the detail sheet, the actuals column — r.013"), which is honest; but the walk's operator goal — "$/min against budget" — has no glass at r.012. The Balance view named under the ladder does not exist. category-icon.tsx:35 gives Q and R the same stroke under a message that says twenty.

THE GATES. Green at 201a8ec: tsc 0 errors, test:ci exit 0 (financial-ladder 59, financial-surface 63, financial-i18n 507, lexicon-coverage 97, planet-ltu 24), build exit 0. The surface gate reads the component source for the ladder wiring, the unit dropdown geometry, the SectionIcon placements, the planet row reads. What it did not read: the picker declared inside render (a class the gate should have carried since r.006), the chart's :447 `positionInYear(from, planet.yearAnchor)` without `planet.yearDays` (the r.008 regex checks the axis expression only, so on Mars the line and the axis will disagree once the deposit runs), the `height="auto"` on the SVG (a console error per render in the probe), and the three pickers under 44 px. Also: no r.012 capture exists — r012-shots.mjs names three files, none was written, and the r.012 chain script runs tsc → test:ci → build with no shot step, unlike r.005–r.011 and r.013.

WHAT THE BRIEF (addendum 22) PROMISES THAT I DO NOT SEE. "N–T on a second surface (Balance / Goals)" — not built, named on the glass anyway. "Detail sheet may list components" — none. "U amounts must name a destination field" — no U entry. "amountNative … stored in user's entry period" — no entry; the ladder is the seed. "each transactions has an MoT timeline established at transaction entry … so when we do $/min its based of transaction date and how long it last" — the dropdown exists; the $/min does not follow it. "13 flow sections A–M render; Net matches the equation at every time base; switching multiplies by a constant; U never a chip; labels exact" — all five acceptance lines hold, on the glass and in the gate.

GRADE C+. The brief said model first, styling second, and the model is an A: exact, pure, locked by a real gate, and the ladder on the glass is right at every base. The grade is held down by the two form defects that are code, not taste — a picker that cannot be used on a phone, rebuilt from a known class, and a timeline that the record claims drives the money and the code ignores — plus a note that sends the person to a view that is not there. Fix those three and the walk reads B+; wire actualsByField into a second column and it reads A−."

### Enlil · adversarial — grade C

_Adversarial pass (B) on Financial-2525 v.000_r.012, commit 201a8ec, read-only_

**One sentence:** The ladder model transcribes the lock faithfully, but the glass double-counts the duplicate premium it only warns about in a warning nobody sees, records a timeline that moves no number, remounts its pickers every second, and overwrites a tampered record on the next save.

**Findings**

- **Duplicate insurance is double-counted in Net; the warning never reaches the glass** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts:110-111 pushes DUPLICATE_INSURANCE and then sums both rows ("Net counts what was entered"). Probe on the r.012 library: B.housing_insurance 120 + D.auto_renters_home 120 per 33 d → warnings [DUPLICATE_INSURANCE], sections.B 120, sections.D 120, net −240. The brief (addendum 22, rule 5 and "What not to build": "Silent double-count of the same premium in B and D") says warn AND do not double-count Net. `grep warnings` on frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx at 201a8ec returns nothing — totals.warnings is computed and never rendered, so on the glass it IS silent. frontend/tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs:49-51 asserts only that the warning fires, never what Net does.
  - Fix: In netLadder count one premium when both are entered (the brief: "Housing insurance stays in B; D is the policy map" — exclude the D row's home component or the B row, one rule, stated in the file header); render totals.warnings as a red line under the ladder; add to financial-ladder a gate that Net with both premiums equals Net with one, and to financial-surface a gate that `totals.warnings` is rendered.
- **The timeline dropdown drives no number; a deposit carries two lengths that disagree** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/accrual.ts:53 (`motMs` from motDays) and :75 (ratePerMinCents from motDays) — `recurrence` is stored (:37) and never read. command-ux1.tsx:145 defaults the MoT input to 30.333 days while :151 defaults the timeline to `days33` (33 days); the seed EXAMPLE at :85 ships with motDays 30.333 AND recurrence "days33". Probe: depositView on the example → 8.2520 ¢/min over 30.333 d; if the timeline governed it would be 7.5852 ¢/min over 33 d; setting recurrence to weekly or yearly leaves releasedCents and ratePerMinCents identical. recurrenceDays, ratePerMinute and actualsByField are exported from ladder.ts:126-146 and unreferenced in the component (grep exit 1). The record claims more than happened: FD-26 ("the timeline is the transaction's MoT: its $/min runs from its date for that length") and FIN-03.02 (status implemented: "a transaction's $/min runs from its date for its timeline's length") are false on the glass.
  - Fix: One length per transaction: derive motDays from the timeline (once → the typed length, otherwise recurrenceDays) or make the timeline the only MoT picker; re-state FIN-03.02 as partly implemented (field and timeline recorded; accrual still on motDays) until the glass reads recurrence; add an accrual gate: a deposit's endsMs − atMs equals its timeline's days.
- **Both pickers remount on every 1 Hz tick — the open dropdown closes and focus is lost within a second** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:95 `setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000)` re-renders the component each second; :157 defines `const LadderPicker = (...) => (...)` INSIDE the component body and :306/:315 render `<LadderPicker …/>` — a new component identity each render, so React unmounts and remounts the three selects every tick. Measured on Athena-adv's fixture that copies this exact shape (fleet-fin/athena-adv/remount.html: parent with the 1 Hz setNow, picker defined inside): focused select → after 1.6 s sameDomNode false, oldNodeDetached true, stillFocused false. The operator hit it on his phone as addendum 26 ("this drop down goes away quick!"); r.014 (84f28f5) moved the picker to module level. The plain inputs at :302-305 are not nested components and survive; only the section, field and timeline selects die.
  - Fix: Module-level LadderPicker with t passed as a prop (as r.014 did); a headless gate in test:ci that focuses a select, waits three ticks and asserts the same DOM node still holds focus.
- **A tampered record is shown empty and then OVERWRITTEN by the next save — the entries are gone for good** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/record.ts:65 returns `emptyRecord(owner)` when verify fails; command-ux1.tsx:114 sets that empty record as state; :177 `commit` appends to it and :70 `saveRecord` calls localStorage.setItem on the same key. Probe with a fake localStorage: two saved deposits, one digit altered outside the app → loadRecord tampered=true, 0 entries shown; one new deposit → saveRecord true; the store now holds ONE entry [d3], verify ok, the chain reads CHAIN VERIFIED. record.ts:10 states the invariant "a deposit or withdrawal, once recorded, is on the record for good" and the `fin.chain_broken` copy promises "shown empty, never repaired silently" — the app itself erases it on the very next action.
  - Fix: Before any write while `tampered`, copy the raw store to a quarantine key (`exel-fin:<owner>:broken-<at>`) and refuse to save over it; render the unverifiable entries greyed with the break point (verify already returns brokenAt); gate: load(tampered) → append → save leaves the original bytes reachable.
- **A second identical deposit or withdrawal is silently swallowed** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:190 builds the id as `d-${when}-${cents}` (and :197 `w-${when}-${cents}`); record.ts:35 returns the same record when the id exists; the form then clears at :191 with no refusal. Probe: two $100.00 deposits typed with the stamp 2026.10.01_07.00..00 → entries 1, the second append returns the identical record object. Two paychecks of the same amount on the same day and time (a common real case for split deposits) lose one.
  - Fix: Id from the append itself (`${rev}-${at}` or a nonce) or, if idempotency on id is wanted, surface it: `fin.reason_duplicate` naming the rev already on the record.
- **Mars' year is Earth's: anchored on Earth's perihelion, labelled SOURCED beside "declared", printed /365** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/calendar.ts:97-101 — the perihelion branch calls perihelionOf(year), Earth's table, for every planet; `yearDays` changes only the A.B..C divisor (:111-112) and lengthDays stays the Earth interval (:113). Probe: positionInYear(now, perihelion, 686.98).startMs === Earth's, status SOURCED, day 271, lengthDays 365, abc 1416.0635..2723 (= 271 Earth days since EARTH's perihelion ÷ 686.98 × 3600). On the glass (lines :286-292, byte-identical r.012→HEAD, measured on the export): "Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91 · 33-day frame 9 · 7/33 · 2026 · SOURCED · 271/365" directly above "no perihelion table for this planet yet — declared, not sourced". The same panel says SOURCED and declared; a 686.98-day year reads 271 of 365.
  - Fix: positionInYear takes a per-planet anchor table (or returns status DECLARED and the surface hides day/quarter/frame for a planet without one); print `/${Math.ceil(yearDays)}`; gate: for mars, status !== SOURCED and startMs !== Earth's.
- **The chart subtitle drops planet.yearDays — on Mars its A.B..C range reads Earth positions while the axis reads Mars; the gate regexes only the axis line** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:447 `positionInYear(from, planet.yearAnchor).abc` and `positionInYear(to, planet.yearAnchor).abc` (two arguments) versus :433 `positionInYear(from + f * len, planet.yearAnchor, planet.yearDays)` (three). Probe on Mars for the example's window: subtitle 2669.0833..0514 → 2968.0708..2340; axis marks 0 and 4 read 1419.0722..1931 → 1578.0566..0971 — the r.008 defect ("the Mars axis read Earth positions") survives on one line. frontend/tests/financial-surface.test.mjs:29 and :38 assert the three-argument form on the axis line only, so the gate is green. Not yet visible on the export because the example is Pending until 2026-10-01 07:00 CST (:447 takes the `now < from` branch first); from that instant every Mars view shows it.
  - Fix: Pass planet.yearDays at :447; gate: no `positionInYear(` call in the component without a third argument (`!/positionInYear\([^)]*yearAnchor\)\./`).
- **The per-second column prints $0.0000 for a live line; one column mixes 2- and 4-decimal figures** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: command-ux1.tsx:140-141 `usdUnit`: ≥ 100 cents → whole cents, below → four decimals. Measured on the export (block byte-identical r.012→HEAD) and in Aset's r.012-stamped walk (fleet-fin/aset-walk.log): at per second "G · Health $0.0000 · Mental Health / Physical Fitness $0.0000" for the sheet's $50 / 33 d line (1.75e-5 $/s); Net prints −$0.0001. The per-hour column shows "$4.04" beside "$0.8838"; empty sections print "$0.0000" beside "$50.00" (visible in aset-b-es-ladder.png). The operator's own sheet carries a $/sec column, so a zero for a real line is a wrong figure, not a rounding nicety.
  - Fix: Format by significant figures below one cent (three significant digits, e.g. $0.0000175) or in a smaller unit (¢/s, µ$/s), one precision per column; print $0.00 for a true zero.
- **At 201a8ec the 90 ladder keys read English in every other language (staged, said on the record, filled six minutes later)** (LOW)
  - Evidence: frontend/tests/lexicon-coverage.test.mjs:28 at 201a8ec stages fin.sec.a–t, the 60 fin.field.*, fin.rec.*, fin.section/field/timeline/ladder_title/stock_note in AFTER_FILL; frontend/lib/i18n-app/es.ts at 201a8ec contains no fin.sec.a (count 0; fin.cat.home 1); frontend/lib/lexicon-context.tsx:224 falls back to englishDefault. So at the reviewed commit a Spanish reader sees "A · Income", "Rent / Mortgage", "Section", "Timeline", "Every 33 days" in English. The commit message says so; the ×32 fill landed as 50e57c0 (23:08). The r.012 export and the aset-b Spanish capture were built after the fill, so the capture is filled — the shipped commit itself is not.
  - Fix: Nothing beyond the fill that landed; the ship-on-record line for r.012 should say "English in 32 languages between 201a8ec and 50e57c0".
- **The gates pass on source text and structure, not on behaviour — including the class the r.011 record promised to retire** (LOW)
  - Evidence: frontend/tests/financial-surface.test.mjs is 93 lines of regex over the .tsx: :54 asserts the full-width claim by class names (`w-full … landscape:w-auto`) — the exact failure mode the r.011 record describes ("a layout claim needs a measured capture, not a class-name gate"), yet no measured gate entered test:ci; :51-53 assert the three selects exist, not that they stay open or that a choice reaches a number; :35 covers the ten categories only, while category-icon.tsx:36-37 maps Q and R both to Gauge — the commit says "twenty strokes", nineteen are distinct (r.015 fixed). financial-ladder.test.mjs never asserts Net under duplicate premiums (:49-51) nor that recurrence moves any accrual figure. financial-crs.test.mjs checks id shapes and commit hashes (55 asserts), none of them the glass.
  - Fix: One headless gate in test:ci on the export: select width in portrait, a focused picker surviving three ticks, the Mars subtitle equal to axis mark 0, the per-second column non-zero for every non-zero line; plus the two model asserts (Net under both premiums; endsMs follows the timeline).
- **Dead model paths on the surface: U, actuals, alcohol target, warnings — and amortize takes a negative amount** (LOW)
  - Evidence: ladder.ts exports amortize (:117), actualsByField (:134), recurrenceDays (:126), ratePerMinute (:129), alcoholSection (:83) and returns warnings; none is referenced in command-ux1.tsx (grep exit 1) — the ladder on the glass is netLadder over the static SHEET_LINES (:138), so the person's own record never touches the ladder and "$/min against budget" exists only in the test file. FIN-06.06 says so honestly (draft, r.013). amortize({amount −1200, 365 d, B.property_tax_hoa_fees}) returns −108.49 into B (probe) — a negative amortization is accepted silently. The two years on one screen (ladder "per year" = 365 d; the MoT whole 365.259636 d) differ by $25.18 on the sheet's income; said on the record (FD-25) but never on the glass.
  - Fix: Refuse `amount <= 0` in amortize; when the actuals column lands (r.013+), feed actualsByField from replay(record) and print which year a "per year" figure means.

**Keep**

- The model is the lock transcribed: frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts carries the twenty sections A–T in the brief's order, sixty fields with the slash-merged labels exactly (46 flow · 14 off), two planes, U as a rule that refuses N–T, the eight fixed period factors with no 30-day month, toPeriod as one constant (a 33-day → minute → 33-day round trip returns the amount to 1e-9), Net = I − L − Ds − Tx − Tr holding at all eight periods, a balance in P never netted — 73 assertions in frontend/tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs, wired into test:ci.
- The unit dropdown IS the panel's full width in portrait and its own width in landscape — measured, not read from classes: Aset's r.012-stamped run gives select 290 px in a 316 px panel (292 px inner); my run on the byte-identical block of the current export gives 290 / 292 portrait and 224 / 670 landscape; no horizontal overflow, no page errors (FD-24 met on the glass).
- The ladder-by-section table renders A–M with section icons, the sheet's fields beneath, Net last and red: −$200.00 per 33 days, −$0.0042 per minute — the r.006 figure reproduced, and the Spanish fill that followed reads correctly (ESCALERA DE FINANZAS PERSONALES A–M).
- Backwards compatibility of the record is real: FinTx.field and FinTx.recurrence are optional (accrual.ts:35-37), so every r.006–r.011 entry and its chain hash are untouched; txWhat (command-ux1.tsx:179-183) and the roster (:215) still print an older entry's category with its icon.
- The record is honest about scope where it matters: revision 0.012 is kind decision, FIN-06.06 (the N–T view, U entry, detail sheet, actuals column) is marked draft for r.013, the 90 staged keys are listed in AFTER_FILL rather than hidden, and the handoff hash follows the ask through addenda 22–24.
- The chain hash and verify are sound for what they claim (FNV-1a two-lane tamper evidence over prev | rev | at | key-sorted tx; revs 1..n; prev pointers), replay(rev) is the ledger's own rule, and loadRecord never trusts an unverified store.

**Report (verbatim)**

## Scope and method

Code read from commit 201a8ec with `git show`, never the working tree (which had moved to r.015 by the time I read). The record (docs/financial-2525/financial-2525.v.000.json) and the ask (docs/asks/2026-09-30_financial_2525_mot_financial_system.md, addendum 22 as the lock) read at the same commit. The r.012 libraries were copied under /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-eXeL-AI-Polling/d1ee5405-1233-5ea7-aa59-4ce83334aed8/scratchpad/fleet-fin/enlil-b/lib/ (aliases rewritten to relative paths, nothing in the repository touched) and probed with node --experimental-strip-types (probe.mjs → the JSON quoted below). The export at frontend/out was already the r.015 build (its header reads revision 0.015), so on-glass measurements (glass.mjs → glass.json, four PNGs in the same folder) were confined to blocks I first proved byte-identical between 201a8ec and HEAD (the unit dropdown, the usdUnit formatter, the year panel, the chart subtitle and axis lines); for r.012 itself I also cite Aset's walk log, whose header carried revision 0.012. The picker-remount claim was measured on Athena-adv's fixture that copies the r.012 shape line for line. The r.012 captures the task pointed at (scratchpad/shots/r012-*.png) do not exist in that folder; r010, r011 and r013 do.

## The ladder math

netLadder is right where the brief is arithmetic: every period is the 33-day figure times one constant, income 3,200 → living 3,400 → Net −200 per 33 days, −$0.0042 per minute, P ignored, transfers and taxes and debt service subtracted once. Two lock breaches sit beside it. Rule 5 says a premium entered in both B and D must warn AND must not double-count Net; ladder.ts:110-111 warns and then sums both (probe: net −240 for two 120 premiums), and the warning is never rendered — command-ux1.tsx has no reference to `warnings`, so on the glass it is exactly the silent double-count the brief lists under "What not to build". The ladder test asserts the warning and never Net. Alcohol's one-target rule exists in the library (alcoholSection) and is unreachable from the surface; U (amortize) and actualsByField are tested, honest as an r.013 draft, and dead on the glass; amortize accepts a negative amount. The formatter usdUnit prints $0.0000 for the sheet's $50/33-day fitness line at per second (1.75e-5 $/s) and mixes "$4.04" with "$0.8838" in one column; the operator's own sheet has a $/sec column, so that zero is a wrong figure.

## The pickers

Section → field works in the handler (a section change resets the field to the section's first). But LadderPicker is defined inside the component body (:157) while the clock re-renders the component every second (:95); each render is a new component identity, React remounts the three selects, and a dropdown the person has opened closes. Athena-adv's fixture with this exact shape measures the DOM node replaced and focus lost within 1.6 s. The operator reported it on his phone within the hour (addendum 26); r.014 fixed it by moving the picker to module level. At r.012 it shipped.

## The timelines

The timeline is recorded inside the hash and shown on the record — and moves no number. accrual.ts reads motDays only (:53, :75); recurrence is never read. The deposit form ships with two lengths that disagree by default (MoT 30.333 days at :145, timeline "Every 33 days" at :151), and the seed example itself carries motDays 30.333 with recurrence days33 (:85): 8.2520 ¢/min if the MoT governs, 7.5852 ¢/min if the timeline did. FD-26 and FIN-03.02 (status implemented) say the timeline is the transaction's MoT and its $/min runs for that length; on the glass that is not so. This is the record claiming more than happened.

## The record

Chain hash, verify and replay are sound for what they promise. The failure is the class around them: a store that fails verify is shown empty (record.ts:65), the surface adopts that empty record (:114), and the next deposit saves over the tampered bytes (:177, record.ts:70) — probe: two entries, one digit altered, one new deposit, store now holds one entry and reads CHAIN VERIFIED. The invariant at record.ts:10 ("on the record for good") is broken by the app's own save path. Separately, the transaction id is `d-${when}-${cents}` (:190), append is idempotent on id, and the form clears without a refusal — a second identical deposit at the same typed stamp is swallowed. Older entries with a category and no field print correctly (:179-183, :215).

## The calendar

Earth is right: perihelion 2026.01.03_11.15..00 CST, day 271, quarter 3, 89/91, frame 9, 7/33, A.B..C unclamped, pastFull flagged. Mars is Earth wearing a different divisor: calendar.ts:97-101 anchors every planet on Earth's perihelion table, so the Mars panel reads "Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 2026 · SOURCED · 271/365" directly above the sentence "no perihelion table for this planet yet — declared, not sourced". The chart subtitle at :447 also drops planet.yearDays (the axis at :433 passes it): on Mars the range would read 2669 → 2968 while the axis reads 1419 → 1578 — reachable from 2026-10-01 07:00 CST when the example stops being Pending; the surface gate regexes only the axis line.

## The unit dropdown (portrait)

Measured full width: 290 px in a 292 px panel inner (Aset's r.012 run; mine on the identical block); 224 px at its own width in landscape; label above in portrait, beside in landscape; no overflow. FD-24 is met.

## The lexicon

At 201a8ec the 90 ladder keys are staged (lexicon-coverage AFTER_FILL:28) and absent from lib/i18n-app/es.ts, so t() falls to englishDefault (lexicon-context.tsx:224): a Spanish reader of the shipped commit sees "A · Income", "Section", "Timeline", "Every 33 days". The commit says so; the fill landed six minutes later (50e57c0), and the Spanish capture the fleet holds was taken after it.

## The gates

financial-surface.test.mjs is regex over source: it asserts class names for the full-width claim (:54) — the pattern the r.011 record itself called insufficient — and the presence of three selects, not that they stay open or that a pick reaches a figure. financial-ladder asserts the warning, not Net; nothing asserts recurrence changes accrual; the icon gate covers ten categories while SECTION_ICON gives Q and R the same Gauge (nineteen distinct strokes under a "twenty strokes" commit line). A headless gate on the export — select width, a focused picker across three ticks, Mars subtitle equal to axis mark 0, no zero print for a non-zero line — would have caught four of the findings above before the operator did.

## Grade

C. The model deserves an A and is gated as one. The glass ships two lock breaches (double-count, decorative timeline), one invariant breach in the record (overwrite after tamper), and a picker that closes every second; the record marks two of these as done. What is right is measured and real: the section table, the full-width picker, backwards-compatible entries, honest drafts for N–T.

### Enlil · synthesis — grade C

**One sentence:** The model is the lock, pure and gated, but the glass at r.012 ships a picker that unmounts every second, a timeline the record says drives the money and the code never reads, a duplicate premium the brief said never to double-count counted twice with its warning never rendered, and a save path that overwrites a tampered record — four defects in code, two of them the record claiming more than happened.

**Top three**

- PICKER UNUSABLE ON A PHONE — frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:157 declares `const LadderPicker = (…) => (` inside `FinancialCommandUX1`, and :95 re-renders the component every second (`setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000)`); a component created inside a render is a new type each render, so the three selects (:160 section, :165 field, :170 timeline) on both forms (:306, :315) unmount and remount every tick and an open dropdown closes before the person picks. The operator hit it (addendum 26, 'this drop down goes away quick!'); r.014 (84f28f5) hoisted the picker, which proves it was live at r.012 and is the same class as r.006's CategorySelect. FIX: module-level `LadderPicker` with `t` as a prop (as r.014 did) PLUS two gates that do not exist yet — a source gate in tests/financial-surface.test.mjs refusing any `const X = (…) => (` that returns JSX inside the component body, and a headless gate that focuses a select, waits three ticks, and asserts the same DOM node still holds focus.
- TWO LENGTHS FOR ONE MONEY, AND THE RECORD CLAIMS THE WRONG ONE — command-ux1.tsx:145 defaults the typed MoT to 30.333 days and :151 defaults the Timeline to `days33` (33 days); :190 commits `motDays: Number(dMot)` and `recurrence: dRec` side by side; frontend/lib/financial-2525/accrual.ts:53 (`motMs`) and :75 (`ratePerMinCents`) read only `motDays` — `recurrence` is stored (:37) and never read (my probe: weekly, yearly, once and days33 all give 8.2521 ¢/min, released 118,831 ¢, the same endsMs; the timeline would give 7.5852 ¢/min). `recurrenceDays`, `ratePerMinute`, `actualsByField` (ladder.ts:126–146) are referenced nowhere in the component. Yet FD-26 (financial-2525.v.000.json:1126, 'the timeline is the transaction's MoT: its $/min runs from its date for that length') and FIN-03.02 (:937–954, status `implemented`, 'a transaction's $/min runs from its date for its timeline's length') say otherwise. FIX: one length per transaction — derive `motDays` from the timeline (`once` → the typed length, otherwise `recurrenceDays(rec)`), or make the timeline the only MoT picker; APPEND a decision restating FIN-03.02 as partly implemented (field and timeline recorded; accrual still on motDays) until the glass reads recurrence; add an accrual gate that a deposit's `endsMs − atMs` equals its timeline's days.
- THE DUPLICATE PREMIUM IS DOUBLE-COUNTED AND THE WARNING NEVER REACHES THE GLASS — frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts:106 sums every line into its section and :110–111 only pushes `DUPLICATE_INSURANCE` ('Net counts what was entered'); my probe: B.housing_insurance 120 + D.auto_renters_home 120 → warnings [DUPLICATE_INSURANCE], Net −240 against −120 for one. The brief (ask :252, rule 5: 'If a premium is entered in both, warn; do not double-count Net'; :261 under What not to build: 'Silent double-count of the same premium in B and D') forbids exactly this, and `totals.warnings` is never rendered — `warnings` does not appear in command-ux1.tsx at 201a8ec — so on the glass it IS silent. tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs:51 asserts only that the warning fires, never what Net does. FIX: one rule in `netLadder` (count one premium when both are entered — the brief's own 'Housing insurance stays in B; D is the policy map'), stated in the file header; render `totals.warnings` as a line under the ladder; gate in financial-ladder that Net with both premiums equals Net with one, and in financial-surface that `totals.warnings` is rendered.

**Owed next**

- Picker law: hoist LadderPicker to module level (landed r.014, 84f28f5) — STILL OWED: the source gate 'no component declared inside the render body' and the headless gate 'a focused select survives three 1 Hz ticks' in test:ci (command-ux1.tsx:157, :95).
- One length per transaction: motDays derived from the timeline (once → typed, else recurrenceDays); appended decisions correcting FD-26 (record :1126) and FIN-03.02 (record :937–954, status implemented → partly) so the record stops claiming what accrual.ts:53/:75 does not do; accrual gate endsMs − atMs === timeline days.
- Duplicate premium: count one premium in netLadder (ladder.ts:106–111), render totals.warnings under the ladder (command-ux1.tsx:334–364 has no such line), gate Net(both) === Net(one) in financial-ladder (:51 asserts only the warning) and 'warnings rendered' in financial-surface.
- Tamper quarantine (the close fourth): record.ts:65 returns emptyRecord on a failed verify, command-ux1.tsx:114 adopts it, :177 commit + record.ts:70 saveRecord write over the tampered bytes (probe: two entries, one digit altered, one new deposit → store holds one entry, verifies, original gone) — this breaks the invariant at record.ts:10. Before any write while `tampered`, copy the raw store to `exel-fin:<owner>:broken-<at>` and refuse to save over it; render unverifiable entries greyed with `brokenAt`; gate: load(tampered) → append → save leaves the original bytes reachable.
- Refusal where it was raised: `refusal` and `saveFailed` render only inside the withdrawal panel (command-ux1.tsx:318–319) though recordDeposit sets them (:187–188) — render them under the button that was pressed; with r.013's one form, gate that the refusal line sits inside that form.
- Transaction id: `d-${when}-${cents}` (:190) / `w-${when}-${cents}` (:197) + append's idempotency (record.ts:35) swallows a second identical deposit and the form clears (:191) with no word — id from the append (rev + at + nonce) or surface `fin.reason_duplicate`.
- One precision per column: usdUnit (command-ux1.tsx:141) prints $0.0000 beside $700.00 for empty sections H I J K M, and $0.0000 for the sheet's real $50/33 d Health line at per second (probe nets.sec.g) — one precision per chosen unit, '—' for an empty section, $0.00 only for a true zero, significant figures (or ¢/s) below one cent so a non-zero line never prints as zero.
- The Balance view: fin.stock_note (:363, lexicon-data.ts:3088) sends the person to a view that does not exist (FIN-06.06 draft, record :955–970) — reword as a promise or ship a stub listing N–T until FIN-06.06 lands.
- Whose figures: the ladder is netLadder(SHEET_LINES, period) (:138, budget.ts:77) — the operator's sheet with no EXAMPLE badge on the panel (:334) while the paycheck has one (:253/:328); badge it until per-login lines exist (r.016 edit mode addresses editability; the 'operator's sheet' label for an unsigned visitor is still owed), then feed actualsByField(replay(record)) into a second column — the '$/min against budget' the ask names (:276).
- Chart subtitle on Mars: :447 `positionInYear(from, planet.yearAnchor).abc` and `positionInYear(to, …)` omit planet.yearDays while :433 and :125 pass it — pass it, and extend the surface gate (financial-surface :29/:38 regex the axis line only) to every positionInYear( call in the component.
- Mars's year is Earth's: calendar.ts:97–101 anchor every planet on perihelionOf (Earth's table) with status SOURCED, so the panel (:290) prints 'SOURCED · 271/365' directly above 'declared, not sourced' (:292) for a 686.98-day year — per-planet anchor table or status DECLARED with day/quarter/frame hidden; print /ceil(yearDays); gate mars status !== SOURCED.
- Touch targets in portrait: planet select 26 px (:279 px-2 py-1 text-xs), MoT ⇄ Clock buttons 32 px (:442–443 h-8 w-9), unit select 36 px (:341 py-2) — min-h-[44px] on each (CLAUDE.md release rule 9).
- A length with its unit: '$3,604.49 · 30.333' on the roster (:215), the record (:370, :373) and the chart line (:447) — print '30.333 d' or fmtDurationDays (mot.ts:172, already written: 0000.00.30_07.59..31).
- SVG height="auto" (:448) logs 'Expected length' on every mount (enlil-a findings errs) — drop the attribute, use class h-auto.
- Section icons: category-icon.tsx:37 maps Q and R both to Gauge under a commit that says 'twenty strokes' (landed r.015, 7112170) — STILL OWED: gate `new Set(Object.values(SECTION_ICON)).size === 20`.
- The capture as a chain step: r012-shots.mjs names r012-ladder-portrait / -landscape / -ledger, none exists in scratchpad/shots (r011 then r013), and r012-chain.sh / r012f-chain.sh run tsc → test:ci → build with no shot step — make the two-orientation capture a step that fails the chain when the file is missing.
- Tab title: document.title is 'eXeL AI Polling' on /financial-2525/ (page.tsx at 201a8ec exports no metadata; no layout.tsx beside it) — layout.tsx or generateMetadata with 'eXeL Financial-2525' for both routes, gated as Drone-2525's was.
- amortize (ladder.ts:117–121) accepts a negative amount (probe: −1200 → −108.49 into B) — refuse amount <= 0.
- The ship line for r.012 on the record should say the 90 ladder keys read English in 32 languages between 201a8ec and 50e57c0 (lexicon-coverage AFTER_FILL :28; es.ts had no fin.sec.a at 201a8ec).
- A headless gate on the export in test:ci — select width in portrait, a focused picker across three ticks, the Mars subtitle equal to axis mark 0, no $0.0000 for a non-zero line — because financial-surface is regex over source (its :54 class-name assert is the pattern the r.011 record itself called insufficient at record :187).

**Report (verbatim)**

## Scope and method

I read everything under review from commit 201a8ec with `git show`, never the working tree (which is at r.018 now). Two Enlil reviewers reported; my job was to reconcile them into one package and to drop any claim that does not hold. I set out to drop claims. None fell. Every file:line both reviewers cited is in the bytes at 201a8ec, and the four model-level claims only the adversarial pass made — the double-counted premium, the decorative timeline, the tamper-then-overwrite, the swallowed duplicate id — I re-ran myself on the r.012 library copies (fleet-fin/enlil-syn/probe.mjs → findings.json) and they reproduce to the digit. Two line numbers are corrected below: the Q/R icon entries are at category-icon.tsx:37 (both reviewers wrote :35, the map's opening line), and `fmtDurationDays` is at mot.ts:172 (the walk wrote :158).

One caveat the two reviewers already carried and I confirm: frontend/out was never an r.012 export. The enlil-a probe ran on r.014 (its findings.json stamp says so), enlil-b on r.015 ten minutes later. I checked that `git diff 201a8ec 84f28f5` touches none of the lines the on-glass measurements depend on (usdUnit, the ladder table, the stock note, the chart subtitle, the SVG, the planet select), so those measurements stand for r.012. For the forms I speak from the r.012 source only, since r.013 collapsed them into one.

## Where the two reports agree

Both found the same two high defects and graded within a third of a letter of each other (C+ and C). Both found the picker declared inside the render body, the timeline that is stored and never read, the Mars subtitle that drops the planet's year, the mixed-precision column, the duplicate icon, and the missing r.012 capture. Both praised the same things: the model is the brief verbatim, Net matches the equation at all eight bases by a constant, the unit dropdown is measured full width, older entries keep their hashes, and the record is honest that FIN-06.06 is a draft.

## Where only one looked, and whether it held

The walk alone found: the refusal rendered under the wrong form (:318 — holds), the Balance view named at :363 that does not exist (holds; FIN-06.06 is `draft` at record :970), the unbadged operator's sheet (:138 over budget.ts:77, no EXAMPLE badge in :334–364 — holds), the touch targets (26 / 32 / 36 px — holds against :279, :442–443, :341), the bare 30.333 (holds), the SVG height (holds, console error in findings), the pending example (holds), the tab title (holds; no metadata, no layout.tsx).

The adversarial alone found: the double-count (holds — ladder.ts:106 sums, :110 only warns, `warnings` is absent from the component, ask :252 and :261 forbid it, financial-ladder :51 asserts only the warning), the tamper overwrite (holds — record.ts:65 → command-ux1.tsx:114 → :177 → record.ts:70; my probe shows the store end with one entry, verified, original bytes gone), the swallowed duplicate id (holds — :190, record.ts:35, :191), Mars anchored on Earth's perihelion with SOURCED printed above 'declared' (holds — calendar.ts:97–101, component :290/:292), the lexicon staged at 201a8ec (holds — AFTER_FILL :28, es.ts count 0; the commit says so), amortize taking a negative (holds), and the gate's class-name assert (holds — financial-surface :54; record :187 has the r.011 lesson in those words).

## The model

An A, and I say that having read it. ladder.ts:31–40 carries the twenty sections in the brief's order, :48–76 the sixty fields with the slash-merged labels exactly, :27 the eight fixed factors with no 30-day month, :88 toPeriod as one constant, :100–112 Net = I − L − Ds − Tx − Tr with transfers as outflow and P never netted, :117–121 U as a rule that refuses N–T, :83 Alcohol one target. The commit says the ladder gate is 59 asserts and the r.012 chain passed it (r012f-chain.log: tsc 0, test:ci 0, build 0). On the glass the thirteen sections render with their icons, Net is red and right at every base (probe: −$0.0001/s · −$0.0042/min · −$0.2525/h · −$6.06/day · −$42.42/week · −$200.00/33 d · −$551.52/91 · −$2,212.12/year).

The one breach inside the model is rule 5. The brief says warn AND do not double-count; the code warns and double-counts, and the warning never leaves the function. That is the brief's own 'what not to build' line, shipped.

## The glass — what the code does against what the model says

Four things are code, not taste.

First, the picker. `LadderPicker` is a component defined inside `FinancialCommandUX1` (:157). The surface re-renders every second (:95). A component type created inside a render is new each time, so React unmounts and remounts the three selects on both forms each tick, and a phone's picker sheet closes with the element it belonged to. The operator reported it within the hour; r.014's commit message names it as the class that shipped in r.006 and again in r.012. The gate that should have caught it — 'no component declared inside the render body' — still does not exist.

Second, the timeline. The deposit form asks for the length twice: a typed MoT (30.333) at :145/:304 and a Timeline ('Every 33 days') at :151/:169. The commit at :190 writes both. accrual.ts reads only `motDays` (:53, :75). My probe: every recurrence value gives the identical rate, released amount and end instant. FD-26 and FIN-03.02 (status `implemented`) state that the timeline IS the transaction's MoT. The record claims more than happened. A person who sets 30.333 and 'Every 33 days' on one form is told two different things about one sum of money, and the ask's own line — '$/min its based of transaction date and how long it last' — is not what runs.

Third, the duplicate premium, above.

Fourth, the record's save path. A store that fails verify is shown empty (record.ts:65) and the surface adopts the empty record (:114). The next deposit appends to it and saves over the original bytes (:177, record.ts:70). The chain then reads CHAIN VERIFIED over one entry. record.ts:10 says 'on the record for good'; the copy under the record says 'shown empty, never repaired silently'. The app's own save erases it. I rank this a close fourth rather than in the top three only because it needs an outside edit of the store to trigger; as a defect class it is the most serious thing here for a financial ledger.

Smaller but real: the refusal for a bad deposit prints under the withdrawal panel (:318); a second deposit with the same stamp and cents is silently dropped (:190 + record.ts:35); the per-second column prints $0.0000 for the sheet's real $50 Health line and the 33-day column prints $0.0000 beside $700.00 (:141); the chart subtitle on Mars reads Earth A-units while the axis reads Mars (:447 vs :433 — invisible until the example starts releasing on 2026-10-01 07:00 CST, which is why the regex gate at financial-surface :29/:38 never saw it); the Mars year panel prints SOURCED and 271/365 above 'declared, not sourced' (calendar.ts:97–101); three targets under 44 px; a length printed without its unit in four places; an invalid SVG attribute logging on every mount; Q and R sharing Gauge (:37); no tab title.

## Dead paths

`amortize`, `actualsByField`, `recurrenceDays`, `ratePerMinute`, `periodFactor`, `alcoholSection` and `totals.warnings` are exported or computed and reached by no surface line. The record says so for the first two (FIN-06.06 draft), which is honest. It does not say so for the warning, and the stock note at :363 sends the person to a Balance view that is a draft. The ladder on the glass is the operator's sheet (SHEET_LINES) and never meets the person's own transactions, so the operator's stated goal — '$/min against budget' — has no glass at r.012.

## The gates

Green at 201a8ec and insufficient. financial-surface.test.mjs is regex over the .tsx: it asserts the three selects exist (:51), the field and timeline are recorded (:52), the full-width claim by class names (:54 — the exact pattern the r.011 record at :187 says is not enough), the axis line's three-argument positionInYear (:29, :38) but not the subtitle's. financial-ladder asserts the warning (:51) and never Net under both premiums; nothing asserts that recurrence moves an accrual figure. No gate reads the rendered export. No r.012 capture exists: r012-shots.mjs names three files, the shots folder goes r011 → r013, and both chain scripts run tsc → test:ci → build with no shot step. A headless gate on the export — select width, a focused picker across three ticks, the Mars subtitle equal to axis mark 0, no zero print for a non-zero line — would have caught four of these before the operator did.

## Grade

C. The walk's C+ was given before the double-count and the tamper overwrite were known; the adversarial's C stands once they are. The model deserves an A and is gated as one. The glass ships a brief-forbidden double-count with its warning never rendered, a timeline the record marks implemented and the code ignores, a picker that cannot be used on a phone (rebuilt from a known class), and a save path that breaks the record's own written invariant. Two of those four are the record claiming more than happened, which is the thing this project's own rules weigh heaviest. Fix the picker, the timeline, and the premium and the walk reads B; quarantine the tampered store and wire actualsByField into a second column and it reads A−.
