## Krishna

### Krishna · walk — grade C+

_Constructive walk — a stranger with the operator's own sheet (Income 3,200 · Home 700 · Car 1,800 · Insurance 200 · Electric 150 · Food 300 · Fitness 50 · Fun 200 per 33 days) opening /financial-2525 on a 390×844 phone for 60 seconds_

**One sentence:** The model under the glass is one truth and exactly the brief, but the glass shows a stranger three incomes, three months and two lengths per transaction, and nothing he records ever reaches the ladder — the pieces are each honest and not yet one system.

**Findings**

- **One transaction carries two lengths that disagree by default: the MoT days and the timeline** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:85 — EXAMPLE has motDays: 30.333 AND recurrence: "days33" (33 days). :145 dMot defaults to 30.333 while :151 dRec defaults to "days33"; :190 commits both, unlinked, into the hash. frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts:124-126 — RECURRENCES has no 30.333 option at all, and "month91" (the option labelled "Monthly (91)") maps to 91 days, while the operator's own words in addendum 22 say "monthly … how long it last 30.333 days". The record's FD-26 says "the timeline IS the transaction's MoT"; the glass asks for both and lets them differ. Two truths for the length of the same paycheck: the escrow releases over 30.333 days; actualsByField would spread it over 33.
  - Fix: One length control per transaction: the timeline dropdown gains the pay MoT (91 ÷ 3) and 'Other (n · unit)'; motDays is DERIVED from it (motDays = recurrenceDays(rec)), never typed beside it. Rename 'Monthly (91)' to 'Every 91 days' — never the word 'month' for 91. (r.013 at HEAD did exactly this with `paymot` and lengthDays — confirming the r.012 defect was real; the r.012 tree under review still carries it.)
- **The record never reaches the ladder: what the person records does not move a single ladder row, and the clock's income ≠ the ladder's income** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:48 imports netLadder, toPeriod, RECURRENCES but NOT actualsByField; :138 `netLadder(SHEET_LINES, period)` and :355 `SHEET_LINES.filter(...)` — the ladder is the static seed only. ladder.ts:137-150 actualsByField (the operator's '$/min against budget') has no caller on the glass. So on one screen the stranger reads Income $3,604.49 over 30.333 d on the clock/record (:85, :370) and Income $3,200.00 per 33 days on the ladder (:354) — two incomes, no sentence saying which is plan and which is actual. He records a $700 withdrawal on B · Rent / Mortgage and the B row stays $700.00 plan with no actual beside it. The record marks this FIN-06.06 'draft — r.013', but the glass itself never says 'plan only'.
  - Fix: Until the actuals column lands, label the ladder heading 'plan (the sheet)' and print one line under Net: 'Actuals from your record arrive in r.013'. Then wire actualsByField(replay(record) → TimedTx via recurrenceDays, frame.startMs, frame.endMs) into a third column 'actual' per field, in the same unit, so the ladder × the record become one table.
- **Rule 5's duplicate-insurance warning is computed and thrown away** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts:110 pushes "DUPLICATE_INSURANCE" into totals.warnings; frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:138 takes `totals` and reads only .sections and .net (:354, :360) — `warnings` is never rendered, no lexicon key exists for it. Today it cannot fire (SHEET_LINES maps Insurance to D only, budget.ts:73), but the moment the record feeds the ladder a premium in B and D will be double-counted SILENTLY — the exact thing the brief's 'What not to build' forbids.
  - Fix: Render totals.warnings under the Net row as an amber line ('Housing insurance is in B and D — Net counts both; choose one in the detail sheet') with a lexicon key; add a surface assert that `totals.warnings` is read on the glass.
- **On Mars the chart's summary line reads Earth-year positions while its axis reads Mars positions** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:447 — `positionInYear(from, planet.yearAnchor).abc` → `positionInYear(to, planet.yearAnchor).abc` omit the third argument, so yearDays defaults to Earth's 365.259636; :433 passes `planet.yearDays` for the five axis marks. Probe numbers on the r.015 export (same call sites at HEAD:474): Mars axis reads 1419.0722..1931 → 1578.0559..3533; the :447 line, once the deposit is running, will print 2669.0833..0514 → 2968.0696..2962 — Earth's coordinates under a Mars axis. The r.008 record says this class was fixed; tests/financial-surface.test.mjs:38 regexes only three specific calls and so misses this fourth one.
  - Fix: Pass `planet.yearDays` at :447 (both calls); change the gate to assert that EVERY `positionInYear(` on the surface carries `planet.yearDays` (count the calls, not three named ones).
- **Latent: the MoT length is measured in Earth days by the accrual and in planet days by the chart** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/accrual.ts:53 `motMs = motDays * MS_PER_DAY` and :75 `amountCents / (motDays * MIN_PER_DAY)` — Earth constants; frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:105 `dayMs = daySecOf(planet) * 1000` and :420 `len = motDays * dayMs` — the planet's day. :424 `series([...], from, to, len/120)` samples balanceAt (Earth length) across a chart whose width is the planet length. Identical today only because FD-16 sets Mars's LTU to Earth hours; addendum 12 says the Admin table WILL change and 'everything should modularly adjust' — when it does, the green Released line reaches full before the chart's right edge and the $/min under the clock (:262) disagrees with the chart.
  - Fix: Give the accrual its day: depositView/balanceAt/series take `dayMs` (default MS_PER_DAY) and the surface passes the planet's; or declare on the record that a MoT is always Earth days and make the chart's `len` use MS_PER_DAY, converting only the labels.
- **The timeline is inside the hash but invisible on the roster and the record** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:215 roster label `${usd(d.amountCents)} · ${d.motDays}` — prints '$3,604.49 · 30.333' with no unit and no recurrence; :373 the record row prints motDays (`· ${e.tx.motDays}`) and the field via txWhat (:179-183) but never `e.tx.recurrence`. The one new choice the stranger made at entry (FIN-03.02) cannot be read back; a wrong pick is undiscoverable until the actuals column exists.
  - Fix: Print the timeline word (`t(`fin.rec.${tx.recurrence}`)`) on both rows and give motDays its unit ('30.333 d'); extend the surface gate to require `recurrence` in txWhat.
- **'Month' has three lengths on one screen** (LOW)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:134 unit 'per month (91)'; ladder.ts:126 timeline 'Monthly (91)' = 91 d; :145 the deposit MoT 30.333 (the operator's 'monthly'); :133 'per 33 days' (the sheet's month); :289 '33-day frame'. FD-04 and FD-25 declare all three on the record, but the stranger sees the word 'month' meaning 91 days in two places and his paycheck 'monthly' meaning 30.333 in a third. Probe: Income per month (91) = $8,824.24, per 33 days = $3,200.00, on the clock $3,604.49/30.333 d.
  - Fix: Reserve the word 'month' for nothing: 'per 91 days', 'every 91 days', 'pay MoT 30.333 d', 'per 33 days'. One vocabulary across the unit dropdown, the timeline and the frame line.
- **Empty ladder sections print '$0.0000' beside '$3,200.00'** (LOW)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:141 `usdUnit = cents => Math.abs(cents) >= 100 ? usd(...) : usd4(cents)` — zero falls into the sub-cent branch; probe: five rows (H, I, J, K, M) read '$0.0000' at every unit, including per 33 days and per year. A stranger reads four decimals as 'a tiny amount', not 'nothing entered'.
  - Fix: `cents === 0 ? '—' (or usd(0)) : ...` — zero is not a sub-cent figure.
- **Net is coloured green when ≥ 0 — a colour version of the 'you can afford it' copy the brief bans** (LOW)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:360 `${totals.net < 0 ? "text-red-500" : "text-green-500"}`; the brief says only 'Red if Net < 0' and 'What not to build: “Safe / proven / you can afford it” copy from Net'. Green is that copy without words.
  - Fix: Red below zero, the foreground colour otherwise.
- **The guide sentence and the example badge are stale or doubled against the r.012 form** (LOW)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:207 uses fin.guide.first_deposit — 'amount, day and time, and the MoT it covers' (lexicon-data.ts:2985) — while the r.012 form (:301-306) also asks section, field and timeline; :253 and :328 both print fin.example_badge, so the signed-out stranger reads 'EXAMPLE · the operator's paycheck as data — sign in to record your own' twice within one screen height (probe capture 01-top-390.png).
  - Fix: Update the guide key to name the five entries; print the badge once (keep :328, drop :253).
- **At r.012, 32 of 33 languages read the ladder in English (declared, still a walk finding)** (LOW)
  - Evidence: commit 201a8ec staged 90 keys (20 sections · 60 fields · 5 timelines · 5 labels) in tests/lexicon-coverage AFTER_FILL (lexicon-data.ts:2897 comment; the commit message says the ×32 fill follows). A non-English stranger at r.012 sees 'A · Income … Fun / Hobbies / Clothing' untranslated under a translated shell. Declared on the record, not silent — noted because the walk is in the stranger's language.
  - Fix: None beyond what the record already owes: the fill commit (landed after r.012 per the task list).
- **The Planet LTU table's ladder makes Month and Quarter the same 91 days (read by this surface via planets.ts)** (LOW)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/planet-ltu.ts:75 `ltuDays = r => ({ D: 1, W: 7, M: r.monthDays, Q: 91, Y: r.yearDays })` with the seed monthDays = 91 (:50) — M = Q. Outside the eight files named for this lens but reached through frontend/lib/financial-2525/planets.ts:15 readPlanetLtu; mot.ts:57 LTU_DAYS says M 33 · Q 99. Three ladders of D·W·M·Q·Y exist (sheet 33/99, table 91/91, brief 33/91/365) and the surface now uses the third for the budget (FD-25) and the second for the clock.
  - Fix: Say on the LTU table which of M/Q is the analysis unit, or set Q to 4 × 91 ÷ … — at minimum not equal to M; and record in FD-25 which ladder each panel reads (budget: brief; clock: table; frame: sheet).

**Keep**

- ladder.ts IS the brief, to the letter: 20 sections A–T in order, 60 fields with the slash-merged labels exactly, U absent as a section, the fixed factors (never a 30-day month), Net = I − L − Ds − Tx − Tr — and 59 asserts in tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs hold every rule including drift-to-a-billionth on eleven odd-cent lines.
- A period switch is one constant, proven on the glass: the probe read Net −$0.0042 per minute · −$200.00 per 33 days · −$551.52 per 91 days · −$2,212.12 per year and Income $0.0673 · $3,200.00 · $8,824.24 · $35,393.94 — each pair the same factor; the sheet's −200 lands as the brief predicts.
- The calendar, the chart axis and the chart title agree on A.B..C to the unit: the year line reads 2663.3024..0600 now and the deposit axis opens at 2669.0833..0514 (Oct 1 07:00 CST) — and on Mars both move together (1416 → 1419). Where the reveal is one function it is one truth.
- The glass draws from the model, not a second table: thirteen rows + Net come from netLadder(SHEET_LINES) (command-ux1.tsx:138, :352-360); no typed factor anywhere on the surface (the r.005 law, gated).
- Backward compatibility is real: FinTx.field and .recurrence are optional (accrual.ts:41-43); an r.006–r.011 entry still prints its category with its icon (command-ux1.tsx:181) and its hash is untouched.
- The record is honest about itself: the ask's sha256 on the record (a465d3bd…) recomputes exactly from the ask file at 201a8ec; FD-25 says on the record that the ladder's 365-day year differs from the MoT's 365.259636-day revolution instead of hiding it; the record panel names no vendor.
- The phone holds: 390 px scrollWidth = innerWidth (no horizontal scroll), zero page errors in portrait and landscape, every picker full width in portrait, 44 px buttons, section icons before every word.

**Report (verbatim)**

WHAT I READ AND WHAT I RAN. The r.012 code came from `git show 201a8ec:` for every file named (command-ux1.tsx 465 lines, category-icon.tsx, ladder/budget/accrual/record/mot/calendar/planets.ts, the ask with addenda 1–24, the record JSON). Two honesty notes before the walk. First, there are no r012-*.png captures in the shots folder (r011-* and r013-* exist; the r012-shots.mjs script names them but they were never written there), so I looked at r011-portrait, r013-ladder, r013-ledger and r013-form-portrait and then ran my own headless probe. Second, frontend/out at run time is the r.015 export (stamp `v.000_r.015 · d9054ef`), not r.012; my probe (fleet-fin/krishna-walk/probe.mjs, captures 01–10, probe.log) therefore shows the r.015 glass. Where r.013–r.015 changed something I say so; every defect below is cited against the 201a8ec source and re-checked at HEAD where it survives.

THE SIXTY-SECOND WALK (390×844, signed out, 17:52 CST on 2026-09-30).
0–10 s. Globe, three glyphs, FINANCIAL · 2525, 'the MoT Financial System · Version 000 · revision 0.012'. A card 'Money as time — this MoT' with a DEPOSIT pill and a five-step rail: DEPOSIT 1 · HOLD · RELEASE $0.00 · WITHDRAW 0 · RECORD 1. I understand: money goes into escrow and comes out over time. I do not yet know whose money.
10–20 s. YOUR TURN — 'Sign in to record your own deposits — the example below is the operator's paycheck as data', a Sign in button, and the same sentence again in cyan under it. Then YOUR DEPOSITS: '2026.10.01_07.00..00 · Income / Wages (take-home) · PAYROLL · PENDING DIRECT DEPOSIT — $3,604.49 · 30.333'. I read 30.333 as a number with no unit; I would press the row and nothing would happen. The big clock reads $0.00 Released, escrow $0.00 — the example starts tomorrow at 07:00, so the one thing the surface exists to show, money ticking per minute, shows nothing today. Not a code defect, but the example's date makes the demo silent for the whole first day.
20–35 s. The chart: 'MONEY AS TIME — THIS MOT · 0 H 0 MIN ELAPSED', Planet [Earth ▾], two icon buttons (orbit / clock). Two straight lines crossing, an axis 0 h 0 min → 30 d 7 h. I press the orbit icon: the title becomes '0.0000..0000 / 298.3463..2448 A-UNITS OF THE REVOLUTION' and the axis becomes fifteen numbers in three lines (2669 / .0833 / ..0514 …). I would not know what this is; the clock icon brings the hours back, so the reveal is safe. I switch Planet to Mars: the axis changes to 1419…1578 and the year line to 1416.1203..0783, but the quarter/frame line still says 'Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91' — Earth's day count under a Mars orbit number. (And in code, command-ux1.tsx:447 will print the Earth coordinates in the summary line once the deposit is running — defect 4.)
35–45 s. YEAR POSITION — 'Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91 · 33-day frame 9 · 7/33 · 2026.09.30_17.52 CST · 2026 · SOURCED · 271/365 · Perihelion (Austin CST standard) · 2026.01.03_11.15..00 CST'. I understand there is a year that starts at perihelion, a 91-day quarter and a 33-day frame. I now hold three calendars in my head.
45–55 s. The forms are behind Sign in, so I read their code. Deposit: Amount · Deposit day and time · MoT · length in days (30.333) · Memo · Section [A · Income] · Field [Income / Wages (take-home)] · Timeline [Every 33 days]. Withdrawal: Amount · 'Deposit day and time' (the label says deposit on a withdrawal, :314) · Section [B · Housing] · Field [Rent / Mortgage] · Timeline [One time]. I would type 3,604.49, leave 30.333 and 'Every 33 days' as they are, and have just recorded one paycheck with two lengths (defect 1). The Section → Field cascade is right: pick a section, the field list narrows, the first field is chosen for me. Good. The timeline offers 'Monthly (91)': I am paid monthly, I would pick it, and my money would be spread over a quarter.
55–60 s. PERSONAL FINANCE LADDER A–M, Unit [per 33 days ▾]. My own sheet, exactly: A Income 3,200 · B Housing 700 · C Transportation 1,800 · D Insurance 200 · E Utilities 150 · F Food 300 · G Health 50 (my 'Fitness' lives under Health — the brief's row, fine) · H $0.0000 · I $0.0000 · J $0.0000 · K $0.0000 · L Lifestyle 200 · M $0.0000 · Net −$200.00 in red. I switch to per minute: −$0.0042. To per year: −$2,212.12. This is the first moment the surface does what it promises and it does it well. Then a sentence: 'Cash, investments, debts, net worth, credit, protection and goals live on the Balance view — never per minute' — there is no Balance view to press. Then THE RECORD · CHAIN VERIFIED with the one example row and 'On this phone only'. The bottom strip says '$0.00 · $0.00 DEPOSIT'.

WHAT I UNDERSTOOD. A paycheck is put in escrow and released per minute; a 3-hour hold; the year runs perihelion to perihelion; my budget can be read in any time base and the Net is one equation. WHAT I COULD NOT. Why the clock's income is $3,604.49 over 30.333 days and the ladder's income is $3,200 over 33 days on the same screen; what '30.333' is beside a dollar amount; why 'month' is 91 days in the unit dropdown and the timeline but my paycheck is 'monthly' at 30.333; whether anything I record will ever appear on the ladder (it will not — command-ux1.tsx:138/:355 draw only SHEET_LINES; actualsByField in ladder.ts:137 has no caller). WHAT I WOULD PRESS THAT DOES NOTHING. The deposit row in YOUR DEPOSITS; the words 'Balance view'.

THE LENS: ONE TRUTH OR SEVERAL. I counted the truths.
· Time bases: the ladder's fixed factors (ladder.ts:26 — 33 d, 91 d, 365 d) · the sheet's LTU_DAYS (mot.ts:57 — M 33, Q 99, Y 365) · the Planet LTU table's ltuDays (planet-ltu.ts:75 — M 91, Q 91, Y 365.259636) · the calendar's grid (calendar.ts — 91-day quarters, 364 + a down day) · the MoT whole (365.259636). Five ladders of the same letters. FD-25 declares the ladder's departure from the table on the record — good — but the glass never says which panel reads which ladder; a stranger reads 'per year' on the budget and '271/365' on the year line as the same year, and they are not.
· Lengths of a transaction: motDays (the accrual's release) and recurrence (the ladder's spread) — two fields, two defaults (30.333 vs 33), one hash (defect 1). r.013 at HEAD collapsed them into one control with a `paymot` preset — which tells me the r.012 defect was real and was found by the operator's next addendum rather than by a gate.
· Days: the accrual counts a MoT in Earth days (accrual.ts:53, :75) and the chart in the planet's day (command-ux1.tsx:105, :420). Identical today by FD-16's 'Earth hours for now'; the day the Admin table changes Mars's hours — the very thing addendum 12 asks to be adjustable — the two lengths part (defect 5).
· Positions: three of the four positionInYear calls on the surface take the planet's revolution; the fourth (command-ux1.tsx:447) does not (defect 4). The surface gate names three call sites, so a fourth slipped.
· Income: the record's $3,604.49 / 30.333 d and the sheet's $3,200 / 33 d never meet (defect 2). The brief's 'goal is to tract $/min against budget' has its model (actualsByField, gated with three window cases) and no glass.
· Warnings: rule 5's DUPLICATE_INSURANCE is computed (ladder.ts:110) and dropped (command-ux1.tsx:138) (defect 3).
Where the pieces are ONE function they ARE one truth: the year line, the axis and the title all read the same A.B..C (2663 → 2669; Mars 1416 → 1419); the thirteen rows and Net come from one netLadder call; a period switch is one constant, proven to the cent on the glass. The integration failures are all at the seams between modules, never inside one.

WHAT THE BRIEF PROMISES THAT I DO NOT SEE. 'N–T on a second surface (Balance / Goals)' — a sentence points to it, nothing opens it (FIN-06.06 draft, said on the record, not on the glass). 'U amounts must name a destination field' — amortize() exists (ladder.ts:118) with no entry. 'Detail sheet may list components' — none. 'If a premium is entered in both, warn' — computed, never shown. 'each transactions has an MoT timeline … so when we do $/min its based of transaction date and how long it last 30.333 days' — the timeline exists, but 30.333 is not among its choices and the record never prints it back.

GRADE C+. The model deserves an A and its gates are real; the glass is an honest, non-overflowing, well-labelled phone page; but the stranger with the operator's sheet leaves holding three incomes, three months and one transaction with two lengths, and nothing he records can ever reach the ladder he was shown. Fix the seams (one length per transaction, the record into the ladder, the warning on the glass, the fourth positionInYear, the accrual's day) and this is a B+ surface on the same model.

Files written (read-only elsewhere): /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-eXeL-AI-Polling/d1ee5405-1233-5ea7-aa59-4ce83334aed8/scratchpad/fleet-fin/krishna-walk/{probe.mjs, probe.log, 01-top-390.png, 02-balance.png, 03-chart-clock.png, 04-chart-mot.png, 05-year-mot.png, 06-chart-mars-mot.png, 07-ladder-min.png, 08-ledger.png, 09-top-844.png, 10-ladder-844.png}. No repository file was edited, nothing committed, nothing pushed.

### Krishna · adversarial — grade C−

_Adversarial pass (read-only) on Financial-2525 v.000_r.012, commit 201a8ec_

**One sentence:** The pure ladder model is exact and well-gated, but the glass that ships it holds several truths at once — two MoT lengths per deposit, a duplicate premium counted twice and warned nowhere, Earth's perihelion under Mars' whole, a down day that is not Dec 31, English ladder labels in 32 languages — and the headline pickers are remounted every second by the $/min clock, so on a phone they close before a person can pick.

**Findings**

- **The three new pickers (section · field · timeline) are declared inside the render body and remounted by the 1 Hz clock — on a phone the dropdown closes before the person picks** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:157 `const LadderPicker = (...) => (...)` is defined inside FinancialCommandUX1; :95 `setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000)` re-renders the surface every second; :306 and :315 mount <LadderPicker> on both forms. A component type created during render has a new identity each render, so every <select data-fin-section|field|timeline> is unmounted and remounted each tick. Not measurable headless at r.012 (the forms render only behind sign-in, :297), but the same construct was measured on the r.013 export by the r.014 commit (84f28f5: 'section · field · length · unit selects read REMOUNTED 3.3 s later') after the operator reported 'this drop down goes away quick!' (addendum 26). The r.012 surface gate asserts the pickers' markup (tests/financial-surface.test.mjs:51) and never that a select survives a tick. My probe shows the inline unit select is stable (unitSelectSameAfter3s: true) — the defect is the class 'component declared in render', not the select element.
  - Fix: Hoist LadderPicker (and PICK) to module level with t and state passed as props; add a surface gate that opens the export with the clock running and asserts element identity of every <select> after ≥3 s (r.014 did both — fold its gate back to cover r.012's three pickers explicitly).
- **A premium entered in both B and D is counted twice in Net, and the DUPLICATE_INSURANCE warning is never shown on the glass — the brief's 'silent double-count' built as written** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts:104-111 — the loop adds every flow line to its section (`sections[section] += toPeriod(...)`), then :110 only pushes a warning; probe (fleet-fin/krishna-adv/model-probe.json dupInsurance): B 120 + D 120 → living 240, net −240, warnings ['DUPLICATE_INSURANCE']. command-ux1.tsx:138 `netLadder(SHEET_LINES, period)` — `totals.warnings` is read nowhere in the component (grep: zero references). The lock says 'If a premium is entered in both, warn; do not double-count Net' (ask :252) and lists 'Silent double-count of the same premium in B and D' under What not to build (:261). The gate tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs:49-51 asserts the warning exists and never asserts the Net.
  - Fix: Decide the count in the model (count B.housing_insurance, exclude D.auto_renters_home when both present, or vice versa — the brief: 'Housing insurance stays in B; D is the policy map') and return which line was excluded; render totals.warnings on the ladder as an amber row; extend the ladder gate to assert Net equals the single-premium Net when both are entered.
- **One deposit carries two lengths — motDays for the escrow and recurrence for the ladder — and they disagree by default (30.333 d vs 33 d, an 8.79 % rate gap)** (HIGH)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/accrual.ts:32 `motDays?` and :37 `recurrence?` on the same FinTx; command-ux1.tsx:85 the EXAMPLE has motDays 30.333 AND recurrence 'days33'; :145 the deposit form defaults dMot to SRC.mot.payMotDays (30.333) while :151 defaults dRec to 'days33'; :190 commits both. Probe (model-probe.json twoLengths): escrow 8.252 ¢/min ending 2026.10.31_15.00..00 vs ladder 7.585 ¢/min ending 2026.11.03_07.00..00 for the same $3,604.49. The record holds both truths: FD-26 'the timeline is the transaction's MoT: its $/min runs from its date for that length' vs FIN-03.02 'a deposit keeps its MoT length'.
  - Fix: One length per transaction: derive motDays from the chosen timeline (with an 'Other' entry for a typed length, as addendum 25 later asks) or derive the ladder length from motDays; refuse an entry whose two lengths differ; gate it (a committed deposit's motDays === recurrenceDays(recurrence) unless recurrence is 'other').
- **The timeline labels promise repetition ('Every 33 days', 'Weekly', 'Monthly (91)', 'Yearly') but the model is a single span that never recurs** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts:123-126 — `Recurrence` is defined as 'the length its money covers, from its date' and recurrenceDays returns one span; lexicon-data.ts (201a8ec diff) fin.rec.days33 = 'Every 33 days', fin.rec.weekly = 'Weekly'. Probe (recurrenceRepeats): a paycheck tagged 33 days, summed by actualsByField over a 66-day window, yields 3,604.49 — one occurrence, not 7,208.98. A person reading 'Every 33 days' on the picker will expect the next paycheck to appear on the ladder.
  - Fix: Either rename the type and labels to a span ('covers 33 days', 'covers 7 days') or implement recurrence (generate occurrences until an end date) and say which on the record; the lock's own words are 'timeline so when we do $/min its based of transaction date and how long it last' — a span — so the labels should say so.
- **Mars is Earth's calendar with Mars' denominator: the year panel reads Earth's perihelion, Earth's quarter grid and 'SOURCED' while the next line says 'declared'; the chart subtitle and axis would disagree on the same instant** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/calendar.ts:97-101 — on the perihelion anchor start/end are always Earth's PERIHELION table (no Mars perihelion exists); :111-113 only the abc and pastFull use yearDays (686.98 from lib/planet-ltu.ts:54). Measured on the r.012 export (glass-probe.json, portrait, planet Mars, MoT reveal): year panel 'Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 89/91 … 2026 · SOURCED · 1416.1326..3160' followed by 'no perihelion table for this planet yet — declared, not sourced'. pastFull can never fire on Mars (reset at ~365 d, whole 686.98 d). command-ux1.tsx:447 `positionInYear(from, planet.yearAnchor)` omits planet.yearDays (Earth whole → 2669.0833..0514 for the example deposit) while :433 passes it (axis 1419.0722..1931); the subtitle branch was hidden in the probe only because the example deposit was still Pending — the surface gate :38 regex-checks only the :433 expression.
  - Fix: Pass planet.yearDays at :447; on a planet without a perihelion table, print the year line's status from the planet row (DECLARED), not Earth's; either add Mars' perihelion instants or say plainly that the year panel is Earth-anchored on Mars; gate every positionInYear call site, not one.
- **The down day is not Dec 31 on the default (perihelion) anchor, and a 'day' rolls at 11:15 CST** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/calendar.ts:13 ('Dec 31 stays the one system-offline day') and lib/planet-ltu.ts:50 (`offlineDay: '12-31'`) vs calendar.ts:104-105 (`day = floor(elapsed/MS_PER_DAY)+1; down = day > 364`). Probe (model-probe.json downDay): 2026-12-31 12:00 CST → day 363, quarter 4, down false; the down day runs 2027.01.02_11.15 → 2027.01.02_20.33 CST (perihelion 2027). Day boundaries fall at perihelion + n×24 h = 11:15 CST, not at a CST midnight (the YearPosition comment at :80 admits 'whole 24 h periods from the instant').
  - Fix: Pick one law on the record: the offline day is a calendar date (then compute `down` from cstParts, not from day 365) or it is day 365 from perihelion (then change the Admin row's offlineDay and the header); say which; gate Dec 31 and day 365 explicitly on the perihelion anchor.
- **A second transaction with the same amount and stamp is silently dropped — the form clears as if it were recorded** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:190 `id: \`d-${when}-${cents}\`` and :197 `w-${when}-${cents}`; lib/financial-2525/record.ts:35 `if (rec.entries.some((e) => e.tx.id === tx.id)) return rec;`; :177 commit() never checks that append changed the record; :191 clears the amount and memo. Probe (sameIdDropped): two appends of a $700 rent at 2026.10.03_00.00..00 → 1 entry, same object. Two identical rents on one date typed with the same stamp is an ordinary case.
  - Fix: Make the id unique per entry (a counter or the chain's rev plus the hash prefix) and have commit() refuse out loud when append returns the same record; gate: two equal transactions on one stamp produce two entries.
- **A tampered or corrupt store empties the whole record from view, and the next entry saves an empty+1 record over it — the record's own 'never lost' invariant is broken in practice** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/lib/financial-2525/record.ts:65 `return { rec: ok ? v : emptyRecord(owner), tampered: !ok }`; command-ux1.tsx:114 `setRecord(r.rec)`; :177 `const next = append(record, tx, at); … saveRecord(next)` writes under the same key (record.ts:70). Probe (tamper): three entries, one cent flipped in entry 2 → verify brokenAt 2 → all three entries gone; the first entry was never tampered yet is discarded. The glass only shows 'chain broken' (:368).
  - Fix: Keep the prefix up to brokenAt−1 as the trusted record (verify already returns brokenAt), quarantine the rest under a sibling key, and never overwrite a store that failed verification; gate it with a tampered fixture.
- **At r.012 the ladder reads English in the other 32 languages — 90 keys staged, 0 fills — a Spanish reader sees 'Personal finance ladder A–M · Section · A · Income · Income / Wages (take-home)'** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/tests/lexicon-coverage.test.mjs:28 (201a8ec) stages the 90 fin.sec/field/rec keys in AFTER_FILL; `git show 201a8ec:frontend/lib/i18n-app/es.ts` holds 0 of them (99 other fin.* keys present). Measured on the r.012 export with locale es (glass-probe.json spanish): title 'Personal finance ladder A–M', th 'Section', row 'A · Income', field 'Income / Wages (take-home)', the N–T note in English; only 'Unidad', 'por 33 días', 'Neto' are Spanish (capture fleet-fin/shots/r012-es-ladder.png). The commit says staged, never silent — true on the record, bilingual on the glass; the fill landed later (50e57c0).
  - Fix: Ship the fill with the surface in one commit when the surface adds keys, or hold the surface until the fill is in; the staging list makes the coverage gate pass on a list, not on the glass.
- **A refused deposit shows its reason under the WITHDRAWAL panel — on a phone the Record it button appears to do nothing** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:187-188 recordDeposit calls setRefusal(t('fin.reason_amount') | 'fin.reason_stamp'); the only render of `refusal` is :318, inside `<div id="fin-withdraw-form">` (:310), below the deposit panel and off-screen at 844 px tall. saveFailed (:319) has the same placement.
  - Fix: Render the refusal (and save-failed) beneath the button that was pressed, or once above both panels with the form named; gate that each form has its own refusal node.
- **Amounts are parsed en-US only — a decimal comma ('700,50') or a thousands comma ('1,800') is NaN and refused; currency prints en-US in every language** (MEDIUM)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:185 `Math.round(Number(dAmt) * 100)` and :194; probe (amountComma): Number('700,50') → NaN, Number('1,800') → NaN. :302/:313 `inputMode="decimal"` yields a comma key on de/fr/es/pt keyboards; the surface is lexiconed in 33 languages. :53 `usd()` is fixed to 'en-US'.
  - Fix: Parse with the active locale (strip grouping, accept comma or dot as the decimal mark), or constrain the input to a numeric field with a locale-aware formatter; print money with the active locale; gate '700,50' and '1.800,50'.
- **The printed ladder column does not foot to the printed Net at 5 of 8 periods, and one column mixes two precisions ('$0.0000' beside '$3,200.00')** (LOW)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:140-141 `usdUnit` rounds each row independently and switches 2 ↔ 4 decimals at $1.00; probe (footing): minute rows sum −0.0043 vs Net −0.0042; hour −0.2501 vs −0.2525; day −6.07 vs −6.06; month91 −551.53 vs −551.52; year −2,212.11 vs −2,212.12; zero sections print '$0.0000' beside '$3,200.00' (capture fleet-fin/shots/r012-portrait-ladder-sec.png). Within the brief's 'no drift beyond cents' in the model (toPeriod round-trips exactly) — the drift is at print.
  - Fix: One precision per column (decide by the column's smallest non-zero magnitude) and print Net as the sum of the printed rows, or label it as computed from unrounded amounts; keep the model as is.
- **The gates pass on regexes and lists where the defect is behaviour: no gate measures a width, a select's survival, a rendered warning, two lengths agreeing, or a translation on the glass** (LOW)
  - Evidence: frontend/tests/financial-surface.test.mjs:22-79 is entirely source regex (e.g. :54 asserts the class string 'w-full … landscape:w-auto' — the same assertion passed r.010 while the select measured 145 of 292 px, per r.011's own message; :51 asserts the three pickers' markup, not that they persist across a tick; :38 regex-checks one positionInYear call and misses :447). tests/lexicon-coverage.test.mjs:77-78 exempts AFTER_FILL. tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs is behavioural but model-only (:49-51 warning without Net; no test calls netLadder with the alcohol target from the glass, which never passes one — command-ux1.tsx:138).
  - Fix: Add one measured gate on the export with the clock running (select identity, unit width, the warning row, the Mars subtitle/axis equality) and one model gate for motDays === recurrenceDays and for Net under a duplicate premium.
- **Small splits: Q and R share one stroke; alcohol's L target is unreachable; motDays prints as a bare number** (LOW)
  - Evidence: frontend/components/financial-2525/category-icon.tsx:37 `Q: Gauge, R: Gauge` — the commit says 'twenty strokes'; nineteen are distinct (r.015 later fixed Q). command-ux1.tsx:138 passes no alcohol target so ladder.ts:81-83's 'F or L' is a constant F on the glass. :215 roster label `${usd(d.amountCents)} · ${d.motDays}` and :373 ` · ${e.tx.motDays}` print '30.333' with no unit.
  - Fix: One stroke per section (done in r.015); surface the alcohol target in the field picker as two fields or a setting; print motDays with its unit ('30.333 d').

**Keep**

- The model is one truth: frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts carries the twenty sections and sixty labels exactly as the brief writes them, PERIOD_SECONDS as one fixed table (:27), toPeriod as one constant (:88) — probe round-trip 0.07 → second → days33 returns 0.07 exactly, and Net = I − L − Ds − Tx − Tr holds at all eight periods (financial-ladder.test.mjs:35).
- The record's hash contract survives the new optional keys: an r.006–r.011 entry with category and no field re-hashes to the same value and verifies (probe oldEntryHash ok); replay/verify/append are pure and small.
- Measured on the r.012 export: the unit dropdown is 290 of 290 px (label 290 px) in portrait and 224 px at its own width in landscape, no horizontal scroll, and the select's identity is stable across 3 s of the clock (unitSelectSameAfter3s: true) — addendum 21 holds for that control.
- The r.008 Mars axis fix holds on the axis: five marks read A-units of Mars' revolution (1419.0722..1931 → 1578.0559..3533), three lines each, no overprint at 390 px.
- The record is honest about what is not built: FIN-06.06 is a draft, the 90 keys are listed as staged in the commit, FD-25 says which earlier decision it supersedes, and the ask hash history keeps addenda 23–24 as owed.

**Report (verbatim)**

KRISHNA · INTEGRATION · ADVERSARIAL PASS on Financial-2525 v.000_r.012 (commit 201a8ec). Read from the commit, never the working tree (the tree had moved to r.015; frontend/out is an r.015 build, stamp v.000_r.015, so I probed the r.012 export another fleet agent built from the same tree at fleet-fin/r012src/frontend/out — stamp v.000_r.012; its command-ux1.tsx, ladder.ts and calendar.ts diff clean against 201a8ec). Probes and captures are under scratchpad/fleet-fin/krishna-adv/ (model-probe.mjs → model-probe.json on the r.012 pure libs; glass-probe.mjs → glass-probe.json plus unit-/mars-mot-/year-mars-{portrait,landscape}.png at 390×844 and 844×390). Nothing in the repository was edited.

ONE TRUTH OR SEVERAL — the verdict by seam.

Ladder × ladder (the model alone): one truth. Sections, labels, planes and the eight fixed factors are exactly the lock; Net holds at every period by one constant; a balance in P never nets; U refuses N–T. The gate financial-ladder is behavioural and good. Two cracks inside the model: (1) rule 5 — a premium in both B and D is counted twice (ladder.ts:104-111; probe: living 240, net −240) while the brief says 'warn; do not double-count Net' and names the silent double-count under What not to build; (2) the labels of the five timelines promise repetition ('Every 33 days') while ladder.ts:123-126 defines a single span (probe: one paycheck over a 66-day window, not two).

Ladder × accrual (one deposit, two lengths): several truths. FinTx carries motDays for the escrow AND recurrence for the ladder (accrual.ts:32,37); the EXAMPLE has 30.333 d and 'days33' at once (command-ux1.tsx:85), the form defaults disagree the same way (:145 vs :151). For the operator's own paycheck that is 8.252 ¢/min ending Oct 31 15:00 CST against 7.585 ¢/min ending Nov 3 07:00 CST — an 8.79 % gap on the one number this surface exists to show. The record states both: FD-26 'the timeline is the transaction's MoT' and FIN-03.02 'a deposit keeps its MoT length'.

Ladder × glass: the warning never reaches the glass (totals.warnings has no reader), the alcohol target is a constant F (:138 passes none), and the printed column does not foot to the printed Net at five of eight periods because each row rounds alone and the column mixes two and four decimals ('$0.0000' beside '$3,200.00'). The unit dropdown, by contrast, measures full width in portrait (290/290 px) and keeps its own width in landscape (224 px) — addendum 21 holds there.

Glass × clock: the headline of r.012 — the section → field → timeline pickers — is declared inside the render body (:157) and the 1 Hz clock (:95) re-renders the surface, so every one of those selects is unmounted and remounted each second. A phone dismisses its picker sheet with the element. I could not sign in headless to measure it on r.012, but the construct is the one r.014 measured as REMOUNTED on the r.013 export after the operator's 'this drop down goes away quick!' (addendum 26), and the inline unit select, which is not declared in render, measured stable in my probe. The feature shipped unusable on the device it was asked for; the surface gate asserted its markup and passed.

Record × glass: the chain is sound and the old entries' hashes are untouched by the new optional keys (verified). But two ordinary paths lose data or lie: an entry whose id (amount + stamp) repeats an earlier one is dropped silently while the form clears as if recorded (record.ts:35, command-ux1.tsx:177,190-191); a store that fails verification is replaced by an empty record and the next entry saves that empty+1 record over the key (record.ts:65, command-ux1.tsx:114,177) — the untampered prefix is lost too.

Calendar × planet table: the default anchor is the perihelion, yet the header, the Admin row and the brief say Dec 31 is the offline day. On the perihelion anchor Dec 31 2026 is day 363 of quarter 4 and the down day is Jan 2 2027 from 11:15 to 20:33 CST (probe downDay); days roll at 11:15 CST. On Mars the anchor is still Earth's perihelion table (calendar.ts:97-101) with Mars' 686.98-day whole only in the A.B..C: the year panel on the r.012 export reads 'Day 271 · Quarter 3 · 2026 · SOURCED · 1416.1326..3160' and, one line down, 'declared, not sourced'. The chart subtitle (:447) omits planet.yearDays while the axis (:433) uses it — the same instant would read 2669.0833..0514 and 1419.0722..1931 on one card once the deposit is releasing; the regex gate checks only the axis.

Lexicon: 90 keys staged, zero fills at 201a8ec; measured in Spanish, the ladder's title, header, section rows, field labels and the N–T note read English while 'Unidad / por 33 días / Neto' read Spanish. The commit says staged, never silent — honest on the record, bilingual on the glass.

Gates: financial-surface is source regex end to end; it passed r.010's 'full width' while the select measured 145 of 292 px, and it passes r.012's pickers while they cannot be used. lexicon-coverage passes on a list. No gate reads a warning on the glass, compares motDays to recurrence, measures a width or a select's survival, or checks every positionInYear call site.

GRADE C−. The model earns an A on its own; the integration holds at least six pairs of truths, the record can lose entries on two ordinary paths, and the feature the revision is named for was unusable on a phone at ship. What is real and small for r.013: hoist the pickers (done in r.014 — carry the measured gate), pass yearDays at :447, print the warning row and fix rule 5 in netLadder, derive one length per transaction, refuse a duplicate id out loud, keep the verified prefix of a broken record, move the refusal under the pressed button, and decide the down-day law on the record.

### Krishna · synthesis — grade C

**One sentence:** The pure ladder is one truth and exactly the lock, but every seam the glass crosses holds two — a deposit with two lengths, a ladder the record never reaches and a warning it never shows, a Mars chart with Earth's coordinates in its own subtitle — and the three pickers r.012 is named for remounted every second under the $/min clock, so the revision shipped unusable on the phone it was built for.

**Top three**

- ONE DEPOSIT, TWO LENGTHS (HIGH). frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:85 the EXAMPLE carries motDays 30.333 AND recurrence "days33"; :145 dMot defaults to 30.333 while :151 dRec defaults to "days33"; :190 commits both, unlinked, into the chain hash. frontend/lib/financial-2525/accrual.ts:32/:37 two fields on one FinTx; frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts:124-126 RECURRENCES has no 30.333 and labels 91 days 'Monthly'. The escrow releases the operator's paycheck at 8.252 ¢/min ending Oct 31 15:00 CST; actualsByField would spread it at 7.585 ¢/min ending Nov 3 07:00 — an 8.79 % gap on the one number the surface exists to show. The record says both (FD-26 'the timeline IS the transaction's MoT' vs FIN-03.02 'a deposit keeps its MoT length'). FIX: one length control per transaction — the timeline dropdown gains the pay MoT (91 ÷ 3) and 'Other (n · unit)', and motDays is DERIVED from it (motDays = recurrenceDays(rec)), never typed beside it; a model gate asserts a committed deposit's motDays === its timeline's days unless 'other'. (Closed at HEAD by r.013 — command-ux1.tsx:240 motDays: lengthDays(rec, …), preset 'paymot' — which proves the r.012 defect was real; the gate is still owed.)
- THE PICKERS REMOUNT EVERY SECOND — THE HEADLINE FEATURE UNUSABLE ON A PHONE (HIGH). frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:157 declares `const LadderPicker = (…) => (…)` INSIDE FinancialCommandUX1's render body; :95 `setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000)` re-renders the surface every second; :306 and :315 mount <LadderPicker> on both forms. A component type created during render has a new identity each render, so every <select data-fin-section|field|timeline> is unmounted and remounted each tick and the phone dismisses its picker sheet with the element. The surface gate (frontend/tests/financial-surface.test.mjs:51) asserts the pickers' markup and never that a select survives a tick, so it passed. Confirmed by the r.014 commit (84f28f5): on the r.013 export the selects 'read REMOUNTED 3.3 s later' after the operator's 'this drop down goes away quick!'. FIX: hoist LadderPicker and PICK to module level with t and state as props; add a measured gate that opens the export with the clock running and asserts element identity of EVERY <select> after ≥ 3 s. (Closed at HEAD by r.014 — command-ux1.tsx:72 is module-level; carry the measured gate so it covers r.012's three pickers by name.)
- THE LADDER × THE RECORD × THE WARNING: NOTHING RECORDED REACHES THE LADDER, AND RULE 5 IS A SILENT DOUBLE-COUNT (HIGH, still open at HEAD). frontend/components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx:48 imports netLadder/toPeriod/RECURRENCES but NOT actualsByField; :138 `netLadder(SHEET_LINES, period)` and :355 `SHEET_LINES.filter(…)` — the ladder is the static seed only, so the stranger reads Income $3,604.49 / 30.333 d on the clock and record (:85, :370) and Income $3,200.00 / 33 d on the ladder (:354) with no sentence saying which is plan; a $700 withdrawal he records on B · Rent / Mortgage moves no row. frontend/lib/financial-2525/ladder.ts:104-111 adds EVERY flow line to its section and :110 only pushes 'DUPLICATE_INSURANCE' (probe: B 120 + D 120 → living 240, net −240); :138 on the glass reads only totals.sections and totals.net (:354, :360) — `warnings` has no reader and no lexicon key. The lock says 'warn; do not double-count Net' and lists 'Silent double-count of the same premium in B and D' under What not to build. At r.012 only SHEET_LINES reach the ladder so it cannot fire on the glass; r.016's edit mode lets a person add both lines and the warning still has no reader at HEAD. FIX: (a) render totals.warnings under Net as an amber row with a lexicon key and a surface assert that `totals.warnings` is read; (b) in netLadder count one premium when both are present (B stays, D is the policy map) and return which line was excluded; extend tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs:49-51 to assert Net equals the single-premium Net; (c) wire actualsByField(replay(record) → TimedTx via recurrenceDays, frame.startMs, frame.endMs) into a third column 'actual' per field in the same unit; until it lands, head the ladder 'plan (the sheet)' and print one line under Net saying actuals from the record are not yet shown.

**Owed next**

- GATE the one-length law r.013 implemented: a committed deposit's motDays === lengthDays(recurrence) unless recurrence is 'other' (model gate in tests/financial-ladder.test.mjs); r.013 fixed the instance, nothing yet refuses the class.
- GATE the picker law r.014 implemented, measured: open the export with the clock running and assert element identity of every <select> on the surface after ≥ 3 s, naming the section · field · timeline · unit selects.
- command-ux1.tsx:447 (HEAD :545, still open): pass planet.yearDays to BOTH positionInYear calls in the chart subtitle; change tests/financial-surface.test.mjs:38 to count every `positionInYear(` on the surface and require `planet.yearDays` on each, instead of regexing three named calls.
- Render totals.warnings on the ladder (amber row under Net, lexicon key) and fix rule 5 in netLadder so a premium in both B and D is counted once and the excluded line is returned; gate Net under a duplicate premium (ladder.ts:104-111; command-ux1.tsx:138 — no reader at HEAD).
- Wire actualsByField (ladder.ts:134, no caller at HEAD) into a third 'actual' column per field over the 33-day frame; until it lands, label the ladder heading 'plan (the sheet)' and say under Net that recorded actuals are not yet shown — the glass never says 'plan only' today.
- One vocabulary for a length: the timeline is a SPAN (ladder.ts:123-126 — one occurrence over a 66-day window, never two), so the labels must say so — 'covers 7 days / 33 days / 91 days / 365 days / 30.333 days (pay MoT)' — never 'Every' and never 'Monthly'; the unit dropdown reads 'per 91 days' (command-ux1.tsx:134), not 'per month (91)'. Reserve the word 'month' for nothing on this surface.
- Print the timeline word and the unit of motDays on the roster (command-ux1.tsx:215 '$3,604.49 · 30.333') and the record row (:373); tests/financial-surface.test.mjs:52 claims in prose that the timeline is 'shown on the roster and the record' while its regex checks only the field — make the gate test what it says.
- Give the accrual its day: depositView / balanceAt / series take dayMs (default MS_PER_DAY) and the surface passes the planet's (accrual.ts:53, :75 Earth constants vs command-ux1.tsx:105, :420 the planet's day) — or declare on the record that a MoT is always Earth days and make the chart's len use MS_PER_DAY. Latent while FD-16 keeps Mars on Earth hours; addendum 12 says the table will change.
- record.ts:65 — on a failed verify keep the prefix up to brokenAt − 1 as the trusted record, quarantine the rest under a sibling key, and never let commit() (command-ux1.tsx:177) save over a store that failed verification; gate with a tampered fixture (probe: one flipped cent in entry 2 discards all three entries from view).
- Unique ids per entry: `d-${when}-${cents}` (command-ux1.tsx:190, :197; HEAD :240/:247) collides for two equal amounts on one typed stamp and record.ts:35 drops the second silently while :191 clears the form as if recorded — use the chain's next rev plus a hash prefix, and have commit() refuse out loud when append returns the same record; gate two equal transactions on one stamp → two entries.
- Decide the down-day law on the record: lib/planet-ltu.ts:33 says offlineDay is '—' when the anchor is the perihelion, yet the Earth seed row :50 carries offlineDay '12-31' with yearAnchor 'perihelion', and calendar.ts:104-105 makes day 365 the down day (Dec 31 2026 is day 363; the down day runs 2027.01.02 11:15 → 20:33 CST; days roll at 11:15 CST). Either the offline day is a calendar date (compute `down` from cstParts) or it is day 365 from perihelion (fix the Admin row and the header); gate Dec 31 and day 365 explicitly on the perihelion anchor.
- Mars year panel: calendar.ts:97-101 always opens the year on EARTH's perihelion table, so the glass prints '2026 · SOURCED' (command-ux1.tsx:290) one line above 'declared, not sourced' (:292); print the status from the planet row (DECLARED) and say plainly the panel is Earth-anchored on Mars until a Mars perihelion table exists; pastFull can never fire on Mars (reset at ~365 d, whole 686.98 d).
- lib/planet-ltu.ts:75 ltuDays makes M = Q = 91 on the Admin table; mot.ts:62 LTU_DAYS says M 33 · Q 99; ladder.ts:27 says 33 · 91 · 365 — three ladders of the same letters. Record in FD-25 which ladder each panel reads (budget: the brief; clock: the table; frame: the sheet) and make Q ≠ M on the table.
- Print hygiene on the ladder: zero is not a sub-cent figure — command-ux1.tsx:141 usdUnit prints '$0.0000' beside '$3,200.00' on five rows (HEAD :204 unchanged); Net is green when ≥ 0 (:360; HEAD :445) which is the 'you can afford it' copy the lock bans, in colour — red below zero, foreground otherwise; one precision per column so the printed rows foot to the printed Net (probe: 5 of 8 periods do not foot).
- Guide and badge: fin.guide.first_deposit (lexicon-data.ts:2985) names three entries while the r.012 form asks five (:301-306); fin.example_badge prints twice for a signed-out visitor (:253 and :328) — update the key, print the badge once.
- The alcohol target is unreachable from the glass (command-ux1.tsx:138 passes no third argument, so ladder.ts:81-83 is a constant F) — surface it as a setting or say on the record that the glass maps alcohol to F only.
- Amounts parse en-US only (command-ux1.tsx:185, :194 — Number('700,50') and Number('1,800') are NaN and refused) under inputMode="decimal" keyboards that offer a comma; parse with the active locale and gate '700,50' and '1.800,50'.
- NOT owed any more: the ×32 fill of the 90 staged keys (filled after r.012, then frozen by the operator's addenda 30/32 until the English is final); the refusal node under the WITHDRAWAL panel (:318 — r.013's one form put the one refusal under the one button); Q and R sharing Gauge (category-icon.tsx:37 — r.015).

**Report (verbatim)**

KRISHNA · INTEGRATION · SYNTHESIS — Financial-2525 v.000_r.012, commit 201a8ec. Read-only; nothing in the repository edited, committed or pushed.

HOW I READ. Every file named for this lens was read from `git show 201a8ec:` — command-ux1.tsx (465 lines), category-icon.tsx, ladder/budget/accrual/record/mot/calendar/planets.ts, lib/planet-ltu.ts, the two gates (financial-surface, financial-ladder), the coverage gate, the ask with addendum 22 in full, and the record JSON (FD-04, FD-16, FD-25, FD-26, FIN-03.02, FIN-06.05, FIN-06.06). Where a claim's present status mattered I grepped HEAD (09fe7a1, r.018). I did not run new probes: the two reviewers' probe outputs are on disk (fleet-fin/krishna-walk/probe.log on the r.015 export; fleet-fin/krishna-adv/{model,glass}-probe.json on a separate r.012 export stamped v.000_r.012) and where they overlap they agree to the digit — Mars axis 1419.0722..1931 → 1578.0559..3533 on both, Earth year line 2663.30 on both. The walk is honest that there are no r012-*.png captures in the shots folder (r011-* and r013-* exist) and that its own captures are of the r.015 glass; every defect below is cited against 201a8ec source, which is the thing under review.

THE VERDICT BY SEAM — one truth or several.

Ladder × ladder (the model alone): ONE truth. ladder.ts is the lock to the letter — twenty sections A–T in order, sixty slash-merged labels exactly, U absent as a section, PERIOD_SECONDS one fixed table (:27, never a 30-day month), toPeriod one constant (:88), Net = I − L − Ds − Tx − Tr (:111) holding at all eight periods; the ladder gate is behavioural and real. Two cracks inside the model, both verified: rule 5 counts a premium entered in B and D twice and only warns (:104-111; probe living 240, net −240) where the lock says 'warn; do not double-count Net'; and the five timelines are a single span (:123-126 — recurrenceDays returns one length, actualsByField spreads one amount once) while their labels say 'Every 33 days' and 'Monthly (91)'.

Ladder × accrual (one deposit, two lengths): SEVERAL. FinTx carries motDays for the escrow and recurrence for the ladder (accrual.ts:32, :37). The EXAMPLE has 30.333 d and 'days33' at once (command-ux1.tsx:85); the form defaults the same way (:145 vs :151) and :190 commits both into the hash. For the operator's own paycheck that is 8.252 ¢/min ending Oct 31 15:00 CST against 7.585 ¢/min ending Nov 3 07:00 CST — 8.79 % apart on the one number this surface exists to show. The record holds both truths (FD-26 vs FIN-03.02). r.013 at HEAD derives motDays from one picker (command-ux1.tsx:240 `motDays: lengthDays(rec, …)`, preset 'paymot'): the defect was real and was found by the operator's next addendum, not by a gate — and still no gate refuses the class.

Ladder × glass: SEVERAL. totals.warnings has no reader (:138 → :354/:360 read sections and net only; grep at HEAD still empty); the alcohol target is a constant F (:138 passes none); the printed column rounds row by row and mixes two and four decimals, so '$0.0000' sits beside '$3,200.00' on five rows (:141) and the rows do not foot to the printed Net at five of eight periods; Net is green when ≥ 0 (:360) — the banned 'you can afford it' copy, in colour. Where the glass is one function it is one truth: thirteen rows and Net from one netLadder call; a period switch proven on the glass as one constant (−$0.0042 / −$200.00 / −$551.52 / −$2,212.12).

Ladder × record: SEVERAL — or rather, none: nothing the person records reaches the ladder. :48 does not import actualsByField; :138 and :355 draw SHEET_LINES only. The stranger reads $3,604.49 over 30.333 d on the clock and record and $3,200 over 33 d on the ladder with no sentence saying which is plan. The record marks FIN-06.06 'draft — r.013'; the glass does not say 'plan only'. At HEAD actualsByField still has no caller.

Glass × clock: the headline of r.012 — section → field → timeline — is declared inside the render body (:157) under a 1 Hz clock (:95), so every picker select is remounted each second and a phone dismisses its picker sheet with the element. Not measurable headless at r.012 (forms are behind sign-in, :297) but confirmed by the r.014 commit's measured probe on the r.013 export ('REMOUNTED 3.3 s later') and by the operator's 'this drop down goes away quick!'. The surface gate (:51) asserted the markup and passed. The walk missed this; it is the most user-hurting defect on the page, and it is a seam (the clock × the forms), so it stands in this lens.

Record × glass: the chain is sound and the new optional keys leave an r.006–r.011 hash untouched (probe oldEntryHash ok). Two ordinary paths lose data or lie: an entry whose id (`d-${when}-${cents}`, :190) repeats is dropped silently by record.ts:35 while :191 clears the form as if recorded; a store that fails verify is replaced by an empty record (record.ts:65) and the next commit() (:177) saves that empty+1 record over the key — the untampered prefix is lost too, which breaks the record's own 'never lost' sentence (record.ts:10).

Calendar × planet table: SEVERAL. The default anchor is the perihelion, yet the Earth row says offlineDay '12-31' (planet-ltu.ts:50) while the field's own comment (:33) says '—' on the perihelion anchor; calendar.ts:104-105 makes day 365 the down day, so Dec 31 2026 is day 363 and the down day runs Jan 2 2027 11:15 → 20:33 CST, with days rolling at 11:15 CST. On Mars the year is still opened by Earth's perihelion table (calendar.ts:97-101) with Mars' whole only in the A.B..C, so the glass prints '2026 · SOURCED' (:290) one line above 'declared, not sourced' (:292). The chart subtitle (:447) omits planet.yearDays while the axis (:433) passes it — the same instant would read 2669.0833..0514 and 1419.0722..1931 on one card once the deposit is releasing; the gate (:38) regexes three named calls and misses the fourth. Still open at HEAD (:545). And the accrual counts a MoT in Earth days (accrual.ts:53, :75) while the chart counts it in the planet's day (:105, :420) — identical only while FD-16 keeps Mars on Earth hours, which addendum 12 says will change.

Lexicon: 90 keys staged, zero fills at 201a8ec (coverage :28); in Spanish the ladder's title, header, rows and the N–T note read English under 'Unidad / por 33 días / Neto'. Honest on the record, bilingual on the glass; filled in the next commit, then frozen by the operator (addenda 30/32) until the English is final — so no longer owed.

Gates: the surface gate is source regex end to end. It passed r.010's 'full width' while the select measured 145 of 292 px; it passes r.012's pickers while they cannot be used; :38 names three positionInYear calls and misses :447; :52 says in prose that the timeline is 'shown on the roster and the record' while its regex checks only the field. No gate reads a warning on the glass, compares motDays to the timeline, or measures a select's survival. The ladder gate is behavioural but model-only and asserts the warning without the Net.

WHERE THE TWO REVIEWERS DISAGREED, AND HOW I SETTLED IT.
1. Grade C+ vs C−: C. The model earns an A; the glass is an honest, non-overflowing phone page; but the stranger leaves with three incomes, three months and a deposit with two lengths, nothing he records reaches the ladder, and the feature the revision is named for was unusable on a phone at ship. The walk did not see the picker remount; with it the page is below C+. The adversarial's C− weighs the lexicon and two record edge paths heavily; the lexicon is declared and since frozen by the operator, and the record paths are ordinary but not daily — C.
2. The walk's fix 'rename Monthly (91) to Every 91 days' is DROPPED in part: the model is a single span (ladder.ts:123-126; probe over 66 days = one occurrence), so 'Every' promises a recurrence that does not exist. The adversarial is right; the lock's own words are 'how long it last'. Settled: 'covers N days', never 'Every', never 'Monthly'.
3. The walk's 'DUPLICATE_INSURANCE cannot fire today' HOLDS at r.012 (only SHEET_LINES reach netLadder) but does not lower the severity: the model itself double-counts and r.016's edit mode lets a person add both lines, while the warning still has no reader at HEAD. Merged into top-3 item 3 as HIGH.
4. The adversarial's 'refusal shows under WITHDRAWAL' HOLDS at :318 and is closed by r.013's one form (HEAD :375); 'Q and R share Gauge' holds (category-icon.tsx:37) and is closed by r.015. Both kept on the record as r.012 findings, removed from what is owed.
5. Both reviewers' r.012 numbers: the walk probed the r.015 export, the adversarial a real r.012 export; where they overlap they agree, so I accepted both evidence sets and cited source for everything.

STRENGTHS THAT HOLD (verified). ladder.ts IS the brief, to the letter, with a behavioural gate; a period switch is one constant and the sheet's −200 lands on the glass as the brief predicts; the year line, the chart axis and the chart title read the same A.B..C from one function (Earth 2663 → 2669; Mars 1416 → 1419 together); the glass draws from the model with no typed factor (the r.005 law, gated); FinTx.field and .recurrence are optional so every older entry and its hash survive; the record names no vendor and says what is draft (FIN-06.06), what supersedes what (FD-25 over FD-20), and that the ladder's 365-day year differs from the 365.259636-day whole; the phone holds — 390 px with no horizontal scroll, zero page errors in both orientations, the unit select 290 of 290 px in portrait and its own width in landscape, stable across 3 s of the clock.

GRADE C. Every integration failure is at a seam between modules, never inside one — which is the good news: the fixes are small and named. One length per transaction with a gate (r.013 did the first half), the pickers hoisted with a measured gate (r.014), the warning on the glass and rule 5 counted once, the record wired into a third column, the fourth positionInYear, the accrual's day, the record's two loss paths, and one vocabulary for a length — and this is a B+ surface on an A model.
